### [1] [Band geometry, Anderson's theorem, and vortex nucleation in unconventional 2D superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15872)
*Vladislav Poliakov,Inti Sodemann Villadiego,T. Senthil*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究了非平庸能带几何下无序对超导的影响，并应用于扭角MoTe2及手性时间反演破缺超导体中的涡旋玻璃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中手性超导的发现促使理解无序在具有非平庸能带几何超导体中的作用。

Method: 推广Anderson定理和Abrikosov-Gorkov无序配对破缺理论，纳入正常态Bloch函数结构；并分析随机势无序能否在手性时间反演破缺超导体中形核超导涡旋。

Result: 在扭角MoTe2中，BCS配对机制与测得的正常态剩余电阻率存在矛盾；对于BCS机制稳定的手性超导体，弱无序不会诱导涡旋玻璃相，这与由任意子流体形成的超导不同。

Conclusion: 能带几何显著修正无序对超导的影响，且超导配对机制可通过无序响应与正常态输运加以区分。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent discovery of chiral superconductors in 2d materials, we study the effects of disorder on superconductivity in materials with non-trivial band geometry. First, we show how Anderson's theorem and the conventional Abrikosov-Gorkov description of disorder-induced pair breaking are modified by the structure of the normal-state Bloch functions. We apply these results to twisted MoTe2 and show that a BCS pairing mechanism for the observed superconductor is in tension with the measured normal state residual resistivity. Second, we study whether random potential disorder can nucleate superconducting vortices in a chiral time- reversal-breaking superconductor. We show that, for a superconductor stabilized by the usual BCS mechanism, weak disorder does not induce a vortex glass phase, in contrast to superconductivity formed from an anyon fluid.

</details>


### [2] [Little-Parks Oscillations and Parity Effects in Mesoscopic Superconducting Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14597)
*Iogann Tolbatov,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文解析研究介观准一维超导环中的Little-Parks效应，从弱耦合BCS极限到中间耦合区，给出GL框架下Tc(Φ)的闭式表达式，并区分有限尺寸拓扑奇偶干涉与超流质心动能两类贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LP效应通常由磁通调控Tc周期振荡，但在有限尺寸、不同耦合强度及拓扑边界条件下，离散奇偶效应与连续质心动能如何分别影响Tc尚需统一解析描述。

Method: 采用带扭曲边界条件的Ginzburg-Landau展开，同时纳入离散有限尺寸拓扑奇偶干涉和超流引起的连续质心动能移动，推导适用于可变相互作用强度的Tc(Φ)闭式表达式。

Result: LP响应由两部分组成：受ξ/R控制的离散拓扑奇偶效应，以及按1/R^2标度的连续动能项。随耦合增强、配对关联长度收缩至ξ≪R时，离散奇偶调制被指数抑制，但凝聚体仍需获得依赖磁通的宏观质心动量，因此Tc仍保持主要由超流宏观动能驱动的代数型LP调制。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸超导环的LP效应可分解为拓扑奇偶与质心动能机制；强耦合附近需注意平均场GL在相位涨落主导前的适用性界限。

Abstract: We analytically investigate the Little-Parks (LP) effect, the periodic oscillation of the superconducting transition temperature $T_c$ driven by an applied magnetic flux $\Phi$, from the weak-coupling Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) limit to the intermediate-coupling regime in a mesoscopic, quasi-one-dimensional ring. Utilizing a Ginzburg-Landau (GL) expansion with twisted boundary conditions, we correctly incorporate both the discrete finite-size topological parity interference and the continuous center-of-mass (CM) kinetic energy shift induced by the supercurrent. We derive a closed-form expression for $T_c(\Phi)$ valid within the GL framework across variable interaction strengths. We demonstrate that the LP response comprises two distinct physical contributions: a discrete topological parity effect governed by the correlation length ratio $\xi/R$, and a continuous kinetic energy term scaling as $1/R^2$. As the coupling increases and the spatial correlation length of the pairs contracts ($\xi \ll R$), the constituent pairs become geometrically blind to the global boundary constraint, leading to an exponential suppression of the discrete parity modulation. However, the requirement that the condensate acquires a flux-dependent macroscopic CM momentum persists. This ensures that the critical temperature $T_c$ maintains an algebraic LP modulation driven predominantly by the macroscopic kinetic energy of the supercurrent. We bound our theoretical approach by discussing the breakdown of mean-field GL validity before phase fluctuations dominate near the strong-coupling limit.

</details>


### [3] [Quantum geometry drives orbital paramagnetism in superconducting fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16387)
*Zi-Ting Sun,Ying-Ming Xie,Chunli Huang,Naoto Nagaosa*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 多分量超导体在Tc以上可由高斯涨落产生顺磁响应；其竞争来自Cooper对质心运动的抗磁贡献与分量间干涉的顺磁贡献，后者关联Cooper对量子几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导体通常通过Meissner效应排磁，且Tc以上涨落预期给出发散抗磁化率。本文探究多分量超导涨落是否可产生相反的顺磁响应。

Method: 采用高斯涨落理论研究Tc以上的一般多分量超导体，区分质心运动抗磁项与不同序参量分量干涉顺磁项，并将顺磁项与Cooper对量子几何联系起来；随后应用于具有两个手性配对模式的kagome超导模型，比较保持时间反演对称性与弱环流序破坏轨道时间反演对称性的情形。

Result: 当母态保持时间反演对称性时，顺磁贡献可超过常规抗磁贡献，出现净涨落顺磁；当弱环流序破坏轨道时间反演对称性时，两个手性模式简并解除，降温接近Tc时低能模式主导涨落，单分量物理接管并使抗磁性胜出。

Conclusion: 该工作给出超导体中涨落顺磁性的机制，并表明轨道磁响应可作为探测Cooper对量子几何的手段。

Abstract: Superconductors expel magnetic fields through the Meissner effect. Even slightly above the superconducting transition temperature, electrons can briefly form Cooper pairs, leading to an orbital diamagnetic susceptibility that diverges as the transition is approached. In this work, we use Gaussian fluctuation theory to study a generic multicomponent superconductor above its transition temperature and show that fluctuations can instead produce a paramagnetic response. We identify two competing contributions: the usual diamagnetic contribution from the center-of-mass motion of Cooper pairs and a paramagnetic contribution from interference between different components of the superconducting order parameter. The latter is related to pair quantum geometry, which describes how the internal components of the Cooper pair change with momentum. We then apply our theory to a model of a kagome superconductor with two chiral pairing modes. When the parent state preserves time-reversal symmetry, the paramagnetic contribution can outweigh the conventional diamagnetic contribution, producing net fluctuation paramagnetism. However, when the orbital time-reversal symmetry is broken, e.g., by forming a weak loop-current order, the degeneracy between the two chiral modes is then lifted. Upon cooling toward the superconducting transition, the lower-energy mode dominates the fluctuations, and diamagnetism wins as single-component physics takes over. Our results provide a mechanism for fluctuation paramagnetism in superconductors and show that the orbital magnetic response can probe the quantum geometry of Cooper pairs.

</details>


### [4] [Noise-Protected Superconducting Qudits from Higher-Harmonic Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16563)
*Hamed Vakili*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用约瑟夫森结电流-相位关系的二次谐波，可在三结超导环路中实现受保护的qutrit，且二次谐波符号可作为设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Qudit相比qubit可在每个电路元件中存储更多信息，但其噪声敏感性仍是实现容错量子计算的主要障碍，因此需要强抗弛豫和退相干的超导qutrit。

Method: 分析由三个约瑟夫森结构成的超导环路，利用电流-相位关系二次谐波调控能级与保护机制；分别针对正、负二次谐波设计qutrit，并提出以altermagnetic约瑟夫森结为候选平台，通过微观计算研究栅压对二次谐波符号的选择作用。

Result: 正二次谐波下，小不对称简单环路可实现可调、对称保护的qutrit，并具有精确电荷选择定则；负二次谐波下，引入弱对称破缺（如弱约瑟夫森二极管效应）可实现近电荷噪声不敏感、准粒子错误可纠正的保护qutrit。两种方案各有权衡且依赖材料，均需较大二次谐波；altermagnetic约瑟夫森结是候选平台。栅压可选择二次谐波符号，但栅压噪声会成为主要退相干源。

Conclusion: 二次谐波符号是保护型超导qudit的关键设计原则，为面向容错量子计算的材料与电路协同设计提供了新路径。

Abstract: Qudits, which generalize qubits to $d$ levels, store more information per circuit element than qubits. As with qubits, however, their sensitivity to noise must be overcome before they can be used for fault-tolerant quantum computing. Here we show that, by exploiting the second harmonic of the current--phase relation, a superconducting loop of three Josephson junctions can host qutrits strongly protected against relaxation, while dephasing limits their coherence. When the second harmonic is positive, a simple loop with a small asymmetry between the junctions provides a tunable, symmetry-protected qutrit. When it is negative, a protected qutrit can be realized by adding a small symmetry-breaking term, such as a weak Josephson diode effect. The two designs involve different trade-offs: the first has an exact charge selection rule, whereas the second is nearly insensitive to charge noise and its quasiparticle errors can be corrected. The preferable choice therefore depends on the materials used. Both require a sizable second harmonic to begin with, and we identify altermagnetic Josephson junctions as a candidate platform. Finally, microscopic calculations show that a gate voltage selects the sign of the second harmonic in these junctions, although gate-voltage noise then becomes the leading source of dephasing. These findings establish the sign of the second harmonic as a design principle for protected superconducting qudits.

</details>


### [5] [Nitride crystalline barrier epitaxial SIS Josephson junctions by MBE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14968)
*Manas Verma,Anand Ithepalli,Amit Rohan Rajapurohita,Eegene Clara Chung,David A. Garcia-Wetten,Naomi Pieczulewski,Danqing Wang,John G. Wright,Archit Shah,Jimy Encomendero,Rishabh Singh,Chandrashekar Savant,Hangwang Yang,Anthony Hyatt,Jaehong Choi,Thomas Varghese,Kushagra Aggarwal,Paul B. Welander,Apurva Mehta,David Schuster,Valla Fatemi,Gregory D. Fuchs,Michael C. Hamilton,Hong X. Tang,David A. Muller,Huili Grace Xing,Debdeep Jena*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作首次用MBE制备出功能性氮化物晶态势垒Josephson结，并在NbN/AlN/NbN体系中证实了相干SIS隧穿与欠阻尼Josephson行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶态隧穿势垒的SIS Josephson结长期难以通过MOCVD/MBE等外延技术实现，而这类外延材料与器件正是现代电子和光子系统的核心；实现该结构有望扩展超导量子电路与HEMT、激光器等有源器件单片集成的材料平台。

Method: 采用MBE外延生长NbN/AlN/NbN三层结构，其中AlN为约4.5 nm的晶态隧穿势垒，并测量其Josephson隧穿、电流-电压特性及磁场调制行为。

Result: 器件表现出相干Josephson隧穿，临界电流密度约17 A/cm²，IcRn约2.5 mV，超导能隙电压约3.7 mV，且能隙随温度呈BCS型演化；滞回I-V曲线和Fraunhofer型磁场电流调制证实了欠阻尼SIS Josephson行为。

Conclusion: 该结果突破了晶态势垒Josephson结的关键材料障碍，为合金及超晶格电极/势垒等更丰富的材料组合，以及超导量子电路与HEMT、激光器等有源电子/光子器件的单片集成提供了可能。

Abstract: Superconductor-insulator-superconductor Josephson junctions with crystalline tunnel barriers have remained elusive by epitaxial techniques such as MOCVD or MBE that produce the junction materials and devices powering today's electronic and photonic systems like transistors and lasers. Here, we overcome the key materials hurdle to realize the first functional nitride crystalline barrier Josephson junctions by MBE. Despite an AlN barrier thickness of approximately 4.5 nm, the NbN/AlN/NbN trilayers exhibit coherent Josephson tunneling with critical current densities of approximately 17 A/cm2, an IcRn product of approximately 2.5 mV, and a superconducting gap voltage of approximately 3.7 mV with a BCS-like gap evolution with temperature. Hysteretic current-voltage characteristics and Fraunhofer current modulation with magnetic field establish underdamped SIS Josephson behavior. The realization of crystalline barrier Josephson junctions by MBE opens the doors to a richer materials palette with alloys and superlattice electrodes and tunnel barriers, as well as potential monolithic integration of superconducting quantum circuits with active electronic and photonic devices such as HEMTs and lasers.

</details>


### [6] [Machine-accelerated extraction of experimental superconducting parameters for physical insight and prediction across thousands of materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15627)
*Mihir Manium,Benjamin Geisler,Peter J. Hirschfeld,Amalia I. Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: AutoSuperCon 从 4.7 万余篇 arXiv 预印本中用大语言模型提取并验证了覆盖 6793 种超导体的 8 类超导参数数据库，支持物理洞察与数据驱动发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习可预测超导候选材料，但除临界温度外缺乏经过验证的实验训练数据，限制了多参数预测与发现。

Method: 利用大语言模型从超过 47000 篇 arXiv 预印本中抽取 8 个超导参数，构建包含 20354 条记录、6793 种超导体的 AutoSuperCon 数据库，并与 NIMS SuperCon 参考数据验证（R²=0.96）。

Result: 数据库揭示：中位能隙比从常规超导体的 3.6 升至铜氧化物的 6.4；28% 的上临界场超过 Pauli 极限；临界温度受与超流密度成正比的上限约束。基于组分训练预测器，临界温度 R²_log=0.83、上临界场 R²_log=0.69，迁移学习可提升性能；在已知超导家族内，仅用组分即可筛选这两个参数。

Conclusion: AutoSuperCon 提供了经实验数据验证的多参数超导数据资源，兼具物理规律洞察和跨数千种超导体的数据驱动材料发现能力。

Abstract: New superconductors could unlock next-generation technologies from nuclear fusion to lossless electronics. Machine learning can predict candidate superconductors but lacks validated experimental training data beyond the critical temperature. Here we present AutoSuperCon, a database of eight superconducting parameters, containing 20354 entries for 6793 superconductors. The data were extracted from over 47000 arXiv preprints using a large language model and validated against reference datasets ($R^2 = 0.96$ with NIMS SuperCon). Using AutoSuperCon, we show that median gap ratios rise from 3.6 in conventional superconductors to 6.4 in cuprates, 28% of upper critical fields exceed the Pauli limit, and critical temperatures lie below a ceiling proportional to the superfluid density. Further, we train composition-based predictors for the critical temperature ($R^2_{\log}=0.83$) and the upper critical field ($R^2_{\log}=0.69$), boosted by transfer learning. Within known superconducting families, composition alone is sufficient to screen candidates for both parameters. AutoSuperCon thus enables physical insight and data-driven discovery across thousands of superconductors.

</details>


### [7] [Sign changing pairing in single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 revealed by nonmagnetic impurity bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16679)
*Huimin Zhang,Zhuozhi Ge,Michael Weinert,Lian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 单层FeSe/SrTiO3的配对对称性通过Fe空位缺陷诱导的束缚态被判定为符号改变型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层FeSe/SrTiO3高温超导发现后，其配对对称性究竟是常规符号保持s波还是非常规符号改变型存在广泛争议。

Method: 采用分子束外延生长单层FeSe/SrTiO3，并用扫描隧道显微镜/谱学探测Fe平面内非磁性Fe空位缺陷诱导的束缚态及其磁场响应。

Result: 观察到稳健的带内束缚态，表现强空间电子-空穴不对称性：空穴态为二重对称、电子态为四重对称；外加磁场下束缚态不发生能移或劈裂。

Conclusion: 该结果与符号改变型序参量一致，支持单层FeSe/SrTiO3为非常规符号改变配对，而非常规符号保持s波。

Abstract: The discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in single layer FeSe epitaxially grown on SrTiO3(001) substrates has instigated extensive debate over whether its pairing symmetry is conventional sign-preserving s-wave or unconventional sign-changing. Here we probe the pairing state for single layer FeSe/SrTiO3 grown by molecular beam epitaxy using scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy. We observe robust in-gap bound states induced by non-magnetic Fe-vacancy defects within the Fe-plane, which exhibit strong spatial electron-hole asymmetry with two-fold symmetry in hole states and four-fold in electron states. The bound states exhibit no energy shift or splitting under an applied magnetic field, consistent with a sign-changing order parameter.

</details>


### [8] [Symmetry-Enabled Rolling of Composite Bosons: A Route to Superconductivity from Strong Repulsion with $T_c$ Proportional to the Electron Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14615)
*Zhiyu Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 强库仑排斥可通过形成三电子一空穴（3e1h）玻色复合体并凝聚产生超导；作者提出“滚动”机制，使复合体无需高能中间态即可单粒子跳跃，从而在强束缚极限下保持有限质量并获得由电子带宽标度的Tc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受强库仑排斥体系中超导发现的启发，探讨库仑排斥能否产生Tc仅受电子带宽限制的超导。通常强束缚会使晶格上的复合体通过高阶过程运动、质量变重，超流刚度受限，导致Tc远低于带宽。

Method: 提出利用对称性实现“滚动”：在满足特定对称性的晶格中，3e1h复合体可通过单粒子跃迁运动且无高能中间态。推导滚动判据，构造所有14个支持滚动的极小晶格模型，并证明可滚动3e1h复合体可在基态稳定；进一步提出在双栅双层体系中实现，并用精确数值计算验证。

Result: 滚动使复合体在无限强束缚下仍具有有限质量，从而在可控强束缚区域实现Tc与电子带宽成比例。所构造的极小模型支持可滚动3e1h复合体基态；双栅双层方案中精确数值显示Tc可达电子跃迁能（hopping）的百分之几。

Conclusion: 滚动性（rollability）可作为在强排斥电子体系中寻找和设计超导体的指导原则，所构造的极小晶格模型可作为原型平台。

Abstract: Motivated by superconductors discovered in systems with a strong Coulomb repulsion, we ask whether such a repulsion can give rise to superconductivity with a transition temperature $T_c$ bounded only by the electron bandwidth. Coulomb repulsion can produce pairing as it binds electrons to holes. If each hole binds three electrons into a three-electron-one-hole (3e1h) composite, the condensation of these bosonic composites can give rise to superconductivity. However, the resulting $T_c$ falls far below the electron bandwidth in the controlled regime where the binding is much stronger than the carriers' kinetic energy. This is because, on a lattice, a tightly bound composite usually has to move through a higher-order process where the intermediate state costs an energy of order the binding energy, so that the strong binding makes the composite heavy, limiting the superfluid stiffness and hence $T_c$. Here, we show that symmetry offers a way around this tradeoff. In lattices satisfying certain symmetry requirements, a composite can move by single-particle hops without any costly intermediate state, a motion we call rolling. Such rolling keeps the mass of the composite finite even for an infinitely strong binding, so that a $T_c$ proportional to the electron bandwidth can be achieved in the controlled regime. We derive a criterion for rolling and apply it to construct all fourteen minimal lattice models that support such rolling. We show that rollable 3e1h composites can indeed be stabilized in the ground state. We then propose to realize such superconductivity in a dual-gate bilayer, and use exact numerics to show that $T_c$ can reach a few percent of the electron hopping. These results establish rollability as a guiding principle in the search for and design of superconductors in strongly repelling electron systems, with the minimal models constructed here as prototypes.

</details>


### [9] [Interplay of Pair Density Wave and Uniform Superconductivity on the Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15354)
*Ruiqing Fu,Xianxin Wu,Ziqiang Wang,Sen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在kagome晶格近p型van Hove填充下的单轨道t-W-J模型中，研究了均匀超导与配对密度波（PDW）之间的竞争、共存及相互重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  onsite吸引倾向于零动量BCS配对，而最近邻键吸引受kagome几何与子晶格干涉影响可促进有限动量配对，因此该模型可用于探讨均匀超导如何改变PDW序参量。

Method: 采用2×2超胞的自洽平均场计算，并结合Ginzburg--Landau分析，研究均匀超导分量与三个F_1 PDW分量之间的耦合与重构。

Result: 均匀超导分量会重构三个F_1 PDW分量的振幅和相位结构，产生纯键配对极限下不存在的超导态；这些态具有不同的电荷调制和准粒子谱，且手性PDW随 onsite吸引增强发生拓扑相变。

The resulting states exhibit distinct charge modulations and quasiparticle spectra, while the chiral PDW undergoes topological transitions as the onsite attraction increases. Abstract: We explore the pairing mechanism and possible topological properties of the unconventional superconductor UTe$_2$ through their influence on both the inductive and resistive channels using resonant cavity perturbation techniques. We measure a composite surface impedance for a variety of UTe$_2$ single crystals at microwave frequencies, where the inductive and resistive channels are comparable and can be treated on equal footing. By studying the composite electrodynamics for a large number of resonant modes, we are able to provide a comprehensive picture of the directional dependence of the excitations out of the ground state. We find similar power-law temperature dependencies at low-temperature for both the penetration depth and surface resistance, which in aggregate across modes, are quadratic and isotropic. ### [11] [Distilling an atomistic foundation model is all you need for large-scale molecular dynamics of semiconductor interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14948)

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明可将预训练原子基础模型的知识直接蒸馏到紧凑势函数中，并在异质半导体界面的大规模分子动力学中保留关键热学与力学响应。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进原子基础模型具有良好迁移性，但直接用于大规模分子动力学计算成本高；需研究能否将其知识蒸馏为紧凑势并保持异质界面的物理响应。

Result: 学生势相对教师模型将模拟速度和可访问体系规模提升数个数量级。79,920原子BAs/GaN界面的直接蒸馏保留了终止依赖热导和主要谱特征，热导与DFT训练对应模型最多相差6.6%；29,792原子Cu/金刚石在500 K加载下拉伸强度和加载功分别相差2.8%和1.5%；SiC/a-C/SiC探索性模拟保留了主要热瓶颈和退火响应方向。四模型委员会在10%选择预算下找回42-56%的最高误差教师构型并改善高误差力拟合，但未稳定改善下游性质。

Conclusion: 直接蒸馏可在紧凑原子间势中保留异质界面的关键热学和力学响应，为大规模、高通量异质界面模拟提供可行路径。

Abstract: State-of-the-art atomistic foundation models provide broadly transferable energy and force predictions, but their direct deployment in large-scale molecular dynamics remains computationally costly. Here we examine whether their knowledge can be distilled into compact potentials while retaining the physical responses of heterogeneous semiconductor interfaces. From 128 pretrained models spanning 22 model families, we select a fixed EquiformerV3 teacher and train atomic cluster expansion (ACE) and neuroevolution potential (NEP) students without target-specific teacher fine-tuning, with both simulation speed and accessible system size improved by several orders of magnitude relative to the teacher model. Matched training configurations and architectures enable direct comparisons with students trained on density-functional-theory (DFT) reference labels. For 79,920-atom BAs/GaN interfaces, direct distillation preserves termination-dependent thermal conductance and dominant spectral features, with conductance differing by at most 6.6% from DFT-trained counterparts. In 29,792-atom Cu/diamond specimens loaded at 500 K, tensile strength and loading work differ by 2.8% and 1.5%, respectively. Exploratory SiC/a-C/SiC simulations retain the principal thermal bottleneck and the direction of the annealing response. Furthermore, a four-model committee retrieves 42-56% of the highest-error teacher configurations with a 10% selection budget and improves high-error force fitting, but does not consistently improve downstream properties. These findings demonstrate that direct distillation can preserve key interfacial thermal and mechanical responses in compact interatomic potentials, opening a path towards large-scale, high-throughput simulations of heterogeneous interfaces.

</details>


### [12] [Plasma simulations with adaptive mesh and algorithm refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14820)
*Vladimir I. Kolobov,Robert R. Arslanbekov*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出PAMAR：将AMAR自适应网格与算法细化思想扩展到弱电离等离子体，使电子、离子、中性粒子和场可在同一Basilisk可执行程序中采用独立自适应网格求解器，并按单元自动选择动理学或流体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱电离等离子体中不同组分在质量、速度和时间尺度上相差多个数量级，单一网格或单一模型难以兼顾精度与成本；需要为各组分独立自适应离散并局部选择动理学/流体求解器。

Method: PAMAR允许电子、离子、中性组分和场具有独立相空间维数、细化准则和时间步，通过场求解器耦合。论文展示两个算例：氩瞬态电容耦合等离子体中使用电子和离子独立自适应四叉树(x,v)的两流动理学求解器；浮动电位球体周围使用(r,v,μ)八叉树求解器并与精确特征线法比较。两算例均逐单元选择模型：鞘层动理学/体区流体的混合离子求解器；损失锥层动理学、其余位置与总能量Fokker-Planck模型的混合电子求解器。

Result: 电容放电模拟无统计噪声地分辨三组电子及电子/离子动理学时间尺度，成本介于PIC与快速动理学模型之间；球体算例中逐组分网格以约十分之一均匀网格成本解析冷离子，并与精确解比较。混合电子求解器在界面严格守恒粒子，使用少1.6至5.3倍的网格单元。

Conclusion: PAMAR证明多组分独立自适应网格与逐单元动理学/流体模型选择可有效降低弱电离等离子体模拟成本并保持精度；下一步是面向更高维问题的并行隐式模拟。

Abstract: Adaptive Mesh and Algorithm Refinement (AMAR) combines adaptive Cartesian meshes with automatic selection of kinetic or fluid solvers. It was introduced for rarefied gas dynamics and later extended to gas mixtures, particle and lattice-Boltzmann solvers, computational electromagnetics, and multi-phase flows. In weakly ionized plasmas, the species differ by orders of magnitude in mass, velocity, and time scale. This paper describes PAMAR, a plasma extension of AMAR in which electrons, ions, neutral species, and fields can have independent adaptive-mesh solvers, each with its own phase-space dimensionality, refinement criteria, and time step, coupled through a field solver in one executable built on the Basilisk framework. We present two demonstrations. A transient capacitively coupled plasma in argon is simulated with two-stream kinetic solvers for electrons and ions on independent adaptive quadtrees in $(x,v)$. The simulations resolve three electron groups and the time scales of electron and ion kinetics without statistical noise, at a cost between particle-in-cell simulations and fast kinetic models. A sphere at floating potential in a collisionless or weakly collisional plasma is computed with octree solvers in $(r,v,\mu)$ for both species and compared with an exact method-of-characteristics solution. Per-species meshes resolve the cold ions at a tenth of the uniform-mesh cost. Both demonstrations also select the model cell by cell. In the capacitive discharge, a hybrid ion solver is kinetic in the sheaths and fluid in the bulk. Around the sphere, a hybrid electron solver confines the kinetic solver to the loss-cone layer and uses a Fokker-Planck model in position and total energy elsewhere. It conserves particles exactly across the interface and uses 1.6 to 5.3 times fewer cells. We discuss the next steps toward parallel, implicit simulations of higher-dimensional problems.

</details>


### [13] [A Diffusive T-Matrix Unifying the Electric and Magnetic Polarizability Tensors in Conducting Layered Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15090)
*Fraser Williams,Dhammika Jayalath,Clinton Fookes,Ajay Pandey*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出用过渡矩阵（T-matrix）统一描述导电介质中埋藏/水下目标的电磁感应与伽伐尼响应，克服磁极化率张量（MPT）忽略介质导电性的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPT假设周围介质不导电，但在海水极低频或黏土千赫兹频段、深度接近趋肤深度时失效；目标还会分流介质中的感应电流，产生MPT未包含的伽伐尼响应。

Method: 以T-matrix表征目标，保持与位置和传感器无关，并同时包含感应与伽伐尼响应；对任意形状和材料，每个介质与频率只需一次有限元求解。其感应块在弱导电介质中退化为MPT，伽伐尼块在静态极限下退化为电极化率张量。用全有限元解验证导电长方体（非磁性与磁导率情形）在分层海洋及黏土/基岩模型中的表现。

Result: 在偶极近似外推两个数量级时，T-matrix模型在两个数量级频率范围内、目标尺寸达趋肤深度0.7时误差不超过1.4%；将MPT直接用于导电介质时误差可达91%；两种响应之间的耦合块（MPT与电极化率张量均不含）贡献可达13%。

Conclusion: T-matrix为导电介质中目标电磁响应提供更准确、统一的模型，能正确纳入感应-伽伐尼耦合，优于仅使用MPT或电极化率张量的方法。

Abstract: Electromagnetic induction is used to detect and classify buried and submerged objects, such as unexploded ordnance in soil and on the seabed. A small object is commonly described by its magnetic polarizability tensor (MPT), which is independent of its position and of the sensor. The MPT assumes that the surrounding medium does not conduct. In seawater at extremely low frequency, or in clay at kilohertz frequencies, this assumption fails at depths comparable to the skin depth. The object then also diverts the current induced in the medium, a galvanic response that the MPT omits. We describe the object instead by its transition matrix (T-matrix), which keeps the independence from position and sensor and includes both responses. It is computed once per medium and frequency from a single finite element problem, for any shape and materials. Its inductive block reduces to the MPT when the medium conducts weakly, and its galvanic block reduces to the electric polarizability tensor in the static limit. We test the model against full finite element solutions for a conducting box, non-magnetic and permeable, in a layered sea and in clay over bedrock. Two orders beyond the dipole, it is accurate to within 1.4% over two decades of frequency and for sizes up to 0.7 of the depth, whereas the error of the MPT carried into the conducting medium reaches 91%. Blocks coupling the two responses, contained in neither tensor, contribute up to 13%.

</details>


### [14] [On the statistical reliability of chaotic simulations at reduced precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15344)
*Ross Ah-Weng,Peter V. Coveney*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文揭示低精度算术会使混沌系统模拟的集合统计量产生系统性偏差，并通过功能图与转移算子给出偏差、有效平均时长及精度降低效应的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度算术被用于加速科学计算和AI硬件，但小的算术误差不保证统计准确：有限表示限制空间分辨率，舍入会合并本应分离的混沌轨迹，使概率逐步集中于少数计算机状态，从而在周期性显现前扭曲统计平均，并可能因不相交计算盆地引入初始化偏差。

Method: 将有限精度混沌动力学建模为功能图，并用其转移算子联系统计偏差上界、准确平均持续时间以及降低精度的影响；在四个混沌映射上做穷举数值实验，有效位数取1–24位，其中24位端点匹配IEEE FP32，并比较随机舍入等情形。

Result: 实验显示初始准确的集合会漂移向错误统计量，较低精度通常使退化提前；随机舍入能显著延长部分有用平均窗口，但另一些情形仍严重偏置；更长的计算轨迹可能使推断统计更差，有限平均可比“无限时间”计算机平均好数个数量级。

Conclusion: 降低精度存在统计偏置推断风险，因而有必要从由较短轨迹构成的集合中收集统计量，而非依赖单条长轨迹的长时间平均。

Abstract: Reduced-precision arithmetic is increasingly used to accelerate scientific simulation, while AI workloads are driving its growing prominence in hardware design. Yet small arithmetic errors do not necessarily imply accurate statistics. Finite representation limits spatial resolution, while rounding can merge trajectories that should separate in chaotic systems, progressively concentrating probability on a shrinking collection of computer states. This redistribution can distort statistical averages well before periodicity becomes apparent; furthermore, disjoint computational basins can induce an initialisation bias, even when the intended equilibrium statistics are independent of the initial ensemble. We use functional graphs and their transfer operators to connect these pathologies to bounds on statistical bias, the duration of accurate averaging, and the effect of reducing precision. Exhaustive numerical experiments on four chaotic maps, with 1-24 significand bits and the 24-bit endpoint matching IEEE FP32, show how initially accurate ensembles drift towards incorrect statistics, with lower precision often bringing this deterioration forward. Stochastic rounding substantially extends some useful averaging windows but leaves others severely biased. Longer computed trajectories can therefore worsen inferred statistics, and a finite average can outperform the "infinite-time" computer average by orders of magnitude. This exposes a risk of statistically biased inference at reduced precision and provides a further motivation for collecting statistics from ensembles of shorter trajectories.

</details>


### [15] [A Magnetic Neuroevolution Potential for Large Scale Spin-Lattice Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15395)
*Rongkun Chen,Junhan Li,Feng Xiao,Shiqian Hu*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 开发磁性神经演化势，将磁自由度纳入势能描述，使能量由原子结构和磁构型共同决定，并同时输出原子力与磁能梯度；在CrN、Fe、FeGe的共线/非共线磁构型上验证，并进行声子计算和有限温度自旋-晶格动力学模拟。

Result: 模型在能量、原子力和维里预测上优于无磁描述符的标准神经演化势，平衡晶格常数与实验一致；声子计算反映磁序变化对晶格振动的影响，有限温度模拟揭示磁激发谱的温度依赖及自旋-晶格耦合对磁转变温度和尺寸收敛的影响；Fe中显式耦合使转变温度更接近实验，FeGe差异较小；单张消费级GPU可支持超过两百万原子的耦合动力学模拟。

Conclusion: 该框架兼具磁构型分辨能力与高计算效率，为研究大尺度磁性系统的有限温度动力学和自旋-晶格耦合提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Accurately describing magnetic order, lattice vibrations, and their coupling at finite temperatures within a unified framework remains a major challenge in atomistic simulations of magnetic materials. In this work, we develop a magnetic neuroevolution potential that explicitly incorporates magnetic degrees of freedom, establishing a potential-energy description jointly determined by atomic structures and magnetic configurations and enabling unified predictions of atomic forces and magnetic energy gradients. Using CrN, Fe, and FeGe with collinear or noncollinear magnetic configurations as representative systems, we demonstrate that the model outperforms standard neuroevolution potentials without magnetic descriptors in predicting energies, atomic forces, and virials, while yielding equilibrium lattice constants consistent with experimental values. Phonon calculations for different magnetic states show that the model captures lattice vibrational responses to changes in magnetic order. Finite-temperature spin-lattice dynamics simulations further reveal the temperature dependence of magnetic excitation spectra, while calculations of the average atomic magnetic moment versus temperature demonstrate the influence of spin-lattice coupling on magnetic transition temperatures and system-size convergence. For Fe, explicitly including spin-lattice coupling brings the predicted magnetic transition temperature closer to experiment, whereas the difference in FeGe is smaller. Performance benchmarks demonstrate that the method enables coupled dynamics simulations of over two million atoms on a single consumer-grade GPU. By combining magnetic-configuration resolution with high computational efficiency, the framework provides an efficient tool for investigating finite-temperature dynamics and spin-lattice coupling in large-scale magnetic systems.

</details>

*Marc Boul\'e*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放边界条件下静电场/静磁场数值仿真缺乏系统、可解析验证的基准，尤其涉及能量、力、力矩及电磁动量等指标。

Method: 构造单球和双球源配置：单球计算场能量并扩展到电磁动量；双球计算能量、力及适用时的力矩。为每个测试用例给出解析表达式，以有限元法为示例评估四种开放边界方法，并公开仿真文件以保证ONELAB可重复。

Conclusion: 球体解析测试用例可作为静电场和静磁场开放边界仿真的标准化基准，支持能量、力、力矩和电磁动量验证，并便于可重复计算物理与工程仿真。

Abstract: This paper presents a library of sphere-based test cases for verifying the correctness of open boundary simulations in electrostatics and magnetostatics for computational physics and engineering applications. While the energy in the fields of a single sphere is computed for various source configurations, the energy, force and torque (when applicable) are also computed for a pair of spheres. An extension to electromagnetic momentum is also given for the single sphere, demonstrating its use as a benchmarking metric. Analytical expressions are provided for each test case, from which the validity of a simulation environment can be assessed. The finite element method is used as the example context, and four different open boundary methods are evaluated. Once verified, the simulation is used to visualize the Poynting vector field associated with an electric charge placed near a permanent magnet, a configuration for which no analytical expression is available. Simulation files are made available and all numerical experiments are reproducible with the ONELAB open source software.

</details>


### [17] [Multi-domain AAA-least squares methods and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16631)
*Samuel J. Harris,Ryan A. Palmer*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出多域 AAA-最小二乘方法 nAAALS，用于求解多连通区域内外调和函数的有理逼近，并在涡动力学涡斑问题中验证其高效性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有 AAALS 方法难以直接处理多个相互耦合的域边界，因此需要一种能同时考虑各边界几何与全局多域配置、并满足跨边界耦合匹配条件的多域有理逼近方法。

Method: nAAALS 扩展 AAALS：用 AAA 算法逼近满足边界上 \tilde{S}(z)=\overline{z} 的函数，使极点分布反映各边界几何和全局多域结构；同时修改最小二乘框架以纳入各域边界上的耦合匹配条件。方法覆盖三类问题：外部无界域被多个单连通域穿孔、嵌套域问题、以及与 Runge-Kutta 耦合的准稳态时间推进问题。

Result: 在涡动力学中以有限面积不可压无粘旋转流涡斑为例，nAAALS 能复现已知涡斑平衡态，并计算新旧非平衡构型的演化；结果准确且计算成本低，在普通笔记本电脑上数秒内完成。

Conclusion: nAAALS 适用于稳态和准稳态多域问题，并有望推广到应用数学、数学生物学和流体力学等跨学科问题。

Abstract: A multi-domain AAA-least squares (nAAALS) method is developed for computing rational approximations of harmonic functions defined in the interior and exterior of multiply-connected domains. Three classes of problems are considered: i) multi-domain problems where an unbounded exterior is punctured by multiple singly-connected domains; ii) nested domain problems where one domain contains multiple punctured domains; and iii) quasi-steady time-stepping problems where the nAAALS algorithm is coupled with standard Runge-Kutta schemes to compute the coupled evolution of the multiple domain boundaries. The nAAALS method extends the original AAALS algorithm in two key ways. First, a function $\tilde{S}$ satisfying $\tilde{S}(z)=\overline{z}$ on each domain boundary is approximated using the AAA algorithm, producing simple poles distributed according to both the individual boundary geometries and the global multi-domain configuration. Second, the least-squares framework is modified to accommodate the coupled matching conditions across each domain boundary. The method is applied to problems in vortex dynamics to demonstrate its capability, focusing specifically on vortex patches as a working example: finite-area regions of incompressible, inviscid fluid in rotational flow. The nAAALS algorithm is used to (a) reproduce known vortex patch equilibria, and (b) compute the evolution of new and existing non-equilibrium configurations. The results are shown to be accurate at low computational cost, running in seconds on a standard laptop. Crucially, the nAAALS method is suitable for addressing a broader class of steady and quasi-steady multi-domain problems across disciplines, such as those arising in applied mathematics, mathematical biology, and fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [18] [Bayesian determination of structural ensembles from single-particle X-ray scattering images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16964)
*Steffen Schultze,Nicolai Kozlowski,Helmut Grubm\"uller*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种贝叶斯方法，可直接从全部散射图像的后验分布中采样加权电子密度系综，无需预先分类，用于单粒子X射线自由电子激光散射中的结构异质性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构系综重建依赖对散射图像的事先分类，易出错；对单个大分子而言，图像中相干散射光子太少，分类不可行。

Method: 基于贝叶斯后验概率分布，利用所有散射图像直接采样加权电子密度系综，避免图像分类步骤。

Result: 在低信噪比、高异质性条件下，用柔性小蛋白的合成散射数据和已发表的PR772病毒实验散射图像验证了该方法。

Conclusion: 该方法绕过易错且不可行的分类步骤，为单分子X射线散射研究结构异质性和分子运动提供了可行的贝叶斯框架。

Abstract: Single-particle X-ray scattering with free electron lasers records snapshots of individual molecules with femtosecond time resolution and is therefore ideally suited to study structural heterogeneity or molecular motions. Current approaches to determine structural ensembles rely on prior classification of the scattering images, which is error-prone and becomes infeasible for single macromolecules, whose images contain too few coherently scattered photons. Here, we present a Bayesian approach that samples weighted ensembles of electron densities directly from the posterior probability distribution given all scattering images, thereby avoiding classification altogether. We demonstrate our approach in the low signal-to-noise high heterogeneity regime using synthetic scattering data of a small and flexible protein, as well as on published experimental scattering images for the coliphage PR772 virus.

</details>

### [19] [Brute-force molecular dynamics simulation of aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15664)
*Elisa Petroni,Dario Baratella,Omar Abou El Kheir,Andrea Redaelli,Marco Bernasconi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用机器学习原子间势对非晶Ge2Sb2Te5进行约100 ns的分子动力学老化模拟，揭示结构弛豫导致Peierls畸变增强、带隙增大和原子迁移率降低，但同极键未在模拟时间内减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硫系合金如Ge2Sb2Te5用于相变电子存储器，但其非晶相老化会引起电阻升高，损害器件工作，因此需理解老化背后的结构弛豫机制。

Method: 采用机器学习原子间势进行分子动力学模拟：先在400 K平衡24 ns，再在略低于玻璃转变温度下老化约100 ns；并用密度泛函理论在小尺寸老化非晶模型上计算电子带隙。

Result: 老化过程中轴向键的Peierls畸变增强，与DFT计算得到的老化模型带隙展宽相关；原子迁移率显著降低，键寿命增加；但同极键数量未减少。

Conclusion: 非晶Ge2Sb2Te5的老化由结构弛豫主导，表现为Peierls畸变增强、带隙增大和动力学减慢；同极键的消除需远长于100 ns的时间尺度。

Abstract: Chalcogenide alloys such as the prototypical Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ compound are used in phase change electronic memories due to their ability to undergo a fast and reversible transition between the amorphous and crystalline phases upon heating. The amorphous phase is subject to aging that leads to an increase in the electrical resistance, which is detrimental to the operation of the devices. Using a machine learning interatomic potential, we have investigated the structural relaxations responsible for aging in amorphous Ge$_2$Sb$_2$Te$_5$ in molecular dynamics simulations at temperatures slightly below the glass transition. The aging simulation, lasting about 100 ns, follows an equilibration of 24 ns at 400 K. During aging we observed an enhancement of the Peierls distortion in axial bonds which is a typical feature of these alloys that we relate to the widening of the electronic band gap computed within Density Functional Theory in smaller aged amorphous models. We also observed a marked reduction of the atomic mobility with an increase of the bond lifetimes during aging. However, we do not see a reduction in homopolar bonds, which are expected to be absent in the ideal aged glass and which should be removed on a much longer time scale.

</details>


### [20] [Metric random tilings: exact entropy and the diffraction of counted disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16730)
*Xinyan Wu,Qingqing Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出“度量随机铺砌”，使铺砌的几何本身可计数，并在开普勒11种均匀网络中识别出零度量熵与正度量熵两类，给出蜂窝网络的熵值与衍射判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶体与准晶的随机铺砌理论只计数固定形状的排列方式，而化学设计与衍射测量所对应的度量/几何未被纳入精确计数框架；作者希望让边长的涨落与面闭合约束下的几何成为可计数变量。

Method: 定义周期性、拓扑锁定且边长在精确面闭合条件下涨落的度量随机铺砌；构建专用计数引擎枚举开普勒11种均匀网络，并用显式构造认证正度量熵载体。

Result: 11种网络分为7种零熵和4种正度量熵载体；蜂窝网络熵最大，在所考虑的边长取值域中均具广延熵，算术类为每铺砌 s=0.544 k_B（拟合区间0.53–0.58），另两类约0.2；衍射中平移扩展取值域使熵严格不变，而固定斑块尺寸下Bragg强度相干分数从9×10^-4变到0.58，并遵循振幅流的Debye–Waller规律。

Conclusion: 度量无序被纳入精确计数范式，并可通过“Bragg斑点外裹度量晕”在真实材料中识别。

Abstract: Tiling mathematics has twice become the language of a state of matter: crystals, then quasicrystals, whose thermodynamics are described by the random tiling of fixed shapes. There the counted variable is the arrangement rather than the geometry, although the metric is what chemical design sets and diffraction measures. Here we introduce metric random tilings, periodic networks of locked topology whose edge lengths fluctuate under exact face closure, so that the geometry itself is countable. Purpose-built counting engines enumerate Kepler's eleven uniform networks and divide them into seven zero-entropy ones and four carriers with positive metric entropy, each certified by an explicit construction. On the honeycomb, the densest carrier, all edge-length value domains considered carry extensive entropy, with the arithmetic class reaching $s = 0.544\,k_B$ per tile (fit band 0.53--0.58), the other two $\approx 0.2$. In diffraction, translational increase of the value domain leaves the entropy exactly invariant while the coherent fraction of the Bragg intensity at fixed patch size varies from $9\times10^{-4}$ to 0.58, following the Debye--Waller law of the amplitude flow. Metric disorder thus enters the exact-counting paradigm, and is identifiable in real materials by Bragg spots wrapped in metric halos.

</details>


### [21] [High-Fidelity In Silico Dataset of Ordered Binary and Ternary MXenes with Predictive Synthesis Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14687)
*Ahmed H. Biby,Charles B. Musgrave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用r2SCAN密度泛函理论构建了1040种有序二元和三元裸层MXene的高保真热力学数据集，并筛选出在特定合成条件下可能稳定的候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型MXene的靶向合成受限于其庞大的结构与组成空间，以及缺乏能够纳入合成条件的可靠稳定性描述符。

Method: 采用r2SCAN泛函的DFT计算1040种有序二元和三元裸层MXene，包括单过渡金属、面内有序双过渡金属、面内有序金属-双空位和面外有序双过渡金属MXene；计算形成与分解吉布斯能、分解产物、层对称群及高于凸包的能量。

Result: 所有MXene在平衡条件下均不可热力学稳定；115种以碳化物为主的MXene在500–2000 K高温下稳定，23种在10 GPa高压下稳定，Mn–Fe–C化学空间中有3种在实验可行的碳化学势范围内稳定。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了MXene稳定性趋势并识别了可合成候选物及其合成条件，为机器学习稳定性描述符开发和自主实验流程提供了数据基础。

Abstract: Despite their promise, the targeted synthesis of novel MXenes remains challenging due to their vast structural and compositional space and the lack of reliable stability descriptors that account for synthesis conditions. To address this gap, we utilized density functional theory (DFT) with the r$^2$SCAN functional to develop a high-fidelity dataset of 1,040 ordered binary and ternary bare delaminated MXenes, including mono-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered double-transition metal MXenes, in-plane ordered metal-divacancy MXenes, and out-of-plane ordered double-transition-metal MXenes. For each structure, we calculated formation and decomposition Gibbs energies, decomposition products, layer symmetry groups, and energies above the hull. We find that all MXenes are thermodynamically inaccessible under equilibrium conditions. However, 115 predominantly carbide MXenes become thermodynamically stabilized at elevated temperatures (500--2000 K), and 23 are stabilized under high pressure (10 GPa). Additionally, three MXenes in the Mn--Fe--C chemical space are stabilized within an experimentally feasible range of carbon chemical potentials. These findings reveal stability trends across the MXene space and identify synthesis strategies that can guide experimental efforts. Our dataset provides a robust foundation for developing machine-learned stability descriptors and for guiding experimental efforts toward novel MXene discovery. By identifying synthesizable candidates along with their synthesis conditions, this work bridges a critical gap in data-driven approaches to MXene discovery and lays the foundation for future autonomous experimental workflows.

</details>


### [22] [Improving Nonequilibrium Thermal Conduction in CaF2 by Breaking the Phonon Bottleneck Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14957)
*Mingde Qu,Minghuan Song,Yuxing Wang,Wudi Wang,Chenbo Zhang,Jiamin Quan,Jun Xu,Zhongwei Zhang,Jie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在CaF2中，热电子优先将能量传给输运和弛豫较差的LO声子，形成非平衡声子瓶颈；通过选择性激发TA声子促进LO声子弛豫并向携热声学支转移能量，可突破瓶颈并提升热传导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光光学材料散热不仅取决于热导率，还取决于吸收能量在声子模式间的再分配；CaF2中LO声子瓶颈会限制高效排热，因此需要主动调控声子布居以改善热管理。

Method: 结合电子-声子散射计算、声子玻尔兹曼输运分析和非平衡分子动力学模拟，识别热载流子能量优先沉积的声子模式，并基于三声子散射相空间分析设计选择性TA声子激发策略。

Result: LO声子主要接收热载流子能量并因输运与弛豫低效而累积；持续LO声子激发使热导率降低约12%，额外TA声子激发使热导率相较LO瓶颈状态提高约21%，并超过未激发值。

Conclusion: 通过选择性控制声子布居和支间能量再分配，可抵消声子瓶颈导致的热积累，为激光光学材料的主动热管理提供可行途径。

Abstract: Heat dissipation in laser optical materials depends not only on their thermal conductivity but also on how absorbed energy is redistributed among various phonon modes. In calcium fluoride (CaF2), rapid energy transfer from hot electrons to weakly propagating longitudinal optical (LO) phonons can create a nonequilibrium phonon bottleneck effect that limits efficient heat removal. Here, we propose selective phonon excitation as an effective strategy to break this bottleneck. Combining electron-phonon scattering calculations, phonon Boltzmann transport analysis, and nonequilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, we identify LO phonons as the primary modes that preferentially receive energy from hot carriers, leading to energy accumulation due to their inefficient transport and relaxation. Guided by three-phonon scattering phase-space analysis, selective excitation of transverse acoustic (TA) modes can accelerate LO relaxation and promote energy transfer toward heat-carrying acoustic modes. While sustained LO-phonon excitation reduces the thermal conductivity by approximately 12%, additional TA-mode excitation improves heat conduction by approximately 21% compared to the state limited by the LO-phonon bottleneck, surpassing the unexcited value. These findings demonstrate that controlling interbranch energy redistribution can counteract phonon-bottleneck-induced heat accumulation and reveal a working route for active thermal management in laser optical materials through selective control of phonon populations.

</details>


### [23] [Reversal of the Anomalous Hall Effect by Directional In-Plane Electric Field in Strained Bilayer MoO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14982)
*Wenpeng Wang,hang Shi,Xiangru Kong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状磁性材料具有可调谷和自旋自由度，有望用于多功能自旋电子器件，因此需要探索有效手段实现谷选择性自旋劈裂和可逆反常霍尔响应。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算，研究应变双层MoO中AC2和AC1堆叠构型的稳定性、能带特征及面内电场调控效应；重点分析沿a/b方向电场对X/Y谷自旋简并、Berry曲率和反常霍尔电导的影响。

Result: 在稳定AC2堆叠中，双轴应变诱导类狄拉克能带交叉；沿a或b方向电场分别选择性解除X或Y谷自旋简并，并伴随相反符号Berry曲率重分布，导致反常霍尔电导可逆反转。稳定AC1堆叠则表现应变驱动的金属-拓扑绝缘体转变、量子化自旋霍尔电导、谷极化和压磁效应。

Conclusion: 应变与电场协同可控制谷选择性自旋劈裂和反常霍尔响应，双层MoO是有前景的多功能自旋电子学和谷电子学平台。

Abstract: Layered magnetic materials with tunable valley and spin degrees of freedom hold promise for multifunctional spintronic devices. Here, we demonstrate that the orientation of an in-plane electric field enables selective switching of single-valley spin splitting and reversal of the anomalous Hall conductivity in strained bilayer MoO. In particular, electric fields along the a and b directions selectively lift the spin degeneracy at the X and Y valleys, respectively, while preserving it at the other valley. This valley-selective switching is accompanied by a redistribution of Berry curvature with opposite signs, giving rise to a reversible anomalous Hall response. These effects are realized in the energetically and dynamically stable AC2 stacking configuration, where biaxial strain induces Dirac-like band crossings that enable subsequent electric-field control. Meanwhile, the AC1 stacking configuration, which is also energetically and dynamically stable, exhibits rich strain-driven phenomena, including a transition from metal to topological insulator with quantized spin Hall conductivity, as well as valley polarization and piezomagnetism. Our findings reveal a combined effect of strain and electric fields for controlling valley-selective spin splitting and anomalous Hall responses, highlighting bilayer MoO as a promising platform for multifunctional spintronic and valleytronic devices.

</details>


### [24] [Depth determination from core-loss 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15037)
*Michael Deimetry*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于动量分辨芯损失四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM）数据的掺杂剂深度测定方法，可在低剂量条件下实现纳米至亚纳米级深度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统STEM微图仅给出样品投影信息，难以确定掺杂剂的三维分布；材料表征与开发需要超越投影微图，直接获取掺杂剂深度信息。

Abstract: Moving beyond specimen-projected micrographs in scanning transmission electron microscopy to determine the three-dimensional distribution of dopants is of vital importance for materials characterization and development. We present an approach for dopant depth determination using momentum-resolved core-loss four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy data. Within the Born approximation, Fourier transformation over probe position reveals fringes whose frequency encodes the dopant's distance from the focal plane. Two principal challenges arise in extending this approach to realistic experimental conditions. First, dynamical scattering degrades fringe contrast which can be partially mitigated by acquiring off major zone axes. Second, because core-loss doses are punishing, we use a Bayesian framework which, in simulation, achieves nanometre to sub-nanometre depth precision from only 100 to 1000 core-loss counts, depending on the signal-to-background.

</details>


### [25] [Defective $\alpha$-In$_2$Se$_3$ from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15060)
*Jorge Vega Bazantes,Timo Lebeda,Zhiqiang Mao,Jianwei Sun*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-In2Se3 是具有铁电应用前景的二维范德华材料，但本征缺陷会显著影响其结构和电子性质，因此需要澄清中性空位和反位缺陷的稳定性。

Method: 采用最先进的 meta-GGA 交换关联泛函进行密度泛函理论计算，复现 pristine 材料的结构参数，并在富 Se 和富 In 生长条件下计算中性空位与反位缺陷的形成能及结构弛豫。

Conclusion: 中层 Se 空位的高稳定性源于相邻 In 原子的局域结构弛豫，这为该缺陷的微观稳定性提供了解释，并表明缺陷工程对 α-In2Se3 的结构与电子性质具有重要影响。

Abstract: $\alpha$-In$_2$Se$_3$ is a two-dimensional van der Waals material with promising applications involving ferroelectricity. In this work, we employ density functional theory with a state-of-the-art meta-generalized gradient approximation (meta-GGA) exchange-correlation functional to investigate the electronic structure of bulk $\alpha$-In$_2$Se$_3$ and the formation of neutral point defects, including vacancies and antisites, under Se-rich and In-rich growth conditions. Our calculations reproduce the experimental structural parameters of the pristine material and reveal that the Se vacancy in the middle layer exhibits an unexpectedly low formation energy under both chemical environments. Structural analysis shows that the neighboring tetrahedrally coordinated In atom undergoes a substantial local relaxation along the three-fold axis, which preserves its $C_{3v}$ site symmetry even as its coordination is reduced from four to three, providing a microscopic explanation for the remarkable stability of this defect.

</details>


### [26] [High-Throughput Surface Screening Reveals Energetically Competitive Low-Symmetry Facets in Wurtzite Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15138)
*Gustavo Madrigal,Cibr\'an L\'opez,Jordi Llorca,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合自动化表面生成、机器学习原子间势筛选和DFT，系统评估纤锌矿GaN、LaN、ZnO的226个清洁化学计量表面，发现高指数棱柱面可与常规低指数面能级竞争，但形成能接近并不保证能带对齐相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤锌矿半导体的表面和界面决定其电子、传感及太阳能燃料转化性能；既有第一性原理研究多局限高对称低指数晶面，低对称高指数表面因计算成本和建模复杂性研究不足，而表面形成能由面内原子密度和断裂键强度决定，高Miller指数表面未必不具能量竞争力。

Abstract: The function of oxide materials, from solid-oxide fuel cells to solar thermochemical fuel production, depends on their oxygen vacancies. The formation energies span 8 eV and decrease down each group and, for most multivalent cations, with increasing oxidation state. Both trends follow the reducibility of the cation. An interpretable crystal feature model is fit to these energies; its inputs are properties of the pristine bulk, with the reduction potential alternatively taken from tabulated thermochemistry. Retaining only its two dominant descriptors, the crystal reduction potential and the band gap, the model reproduces the formation energies with a held-out mean absolute error of 0.31 eV, lower than that of its four-descriptor parent model for transition-metal perovskites. The bond-breaking and stability terms of the full Born-Haber fit prove statistically null for the binary oxides, and the largest residuals identify the hosts where electron accommodation departs from the cation-reduction picture that the descriptors encode. The dataset provides a benchmark for machine-learning interatomic potentials, and the model estimates vacancy formation energies of main-group oxides from bulk properties alone.

TL;DR: 该文系统研究了范德华铁磁体Fe3GeTe2的场依赖临界行为，揭示磁场方向、强磁晶各向异性及其他能量尺度如何调控临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临界行为分析常忽略磁场依赖性与各向异性效应；本文旨在阐明外加磁场B、强磁晶各向异性和竞争能量尺度对Fe3GeTe2临界行为的调制机制。

Method: 采用归一化斜率平均法（ANS），在最高7 T的不同场窗口中提取临界指数β、γ和δ，并分别分析B沿易轴（B∥c）和面内难轴（B⊥c）的临界行为。

Result: B∥c时，体系表现出由双交换与 itinerant RKKY 机制竞争主导的长程相互作用；随B增大该效应被抑制并趋近长程3D Ising普适类（β~0.330，γ~1.00）。B⊥c时，在整个测量场范围内出现γ<1的亚单位区间，归因于外场Zeeman能与强面外磁晶各向异性之间的强烈竞争。

Conclusion: 非平凡范德华磁体的临界行为本质上受磁场取向和磁晶各向异性强度调控，因此需要进行B依赖的临界行为研究，而不能仅依赖常规框架。

Abstract: We report a detailed investigation into the field-dependent critical behavior of the van der Waals ferromagnet $\mathrm{Fe_3GeTe_2}$ to elucidate how applied magnetic fields ($B$), strong magnetocrystalline anisotropy, and emerging other energy scales modulate its critical behavior. Utilizing the recently developed method, viz., the average of normalized slope (ANS), for analysis of critical behavior, we evaluate the critical exponents ($\beta$, $\gamma$, and $\delta$) across distinct field windows up to $\rm{7\,T}$. For $B$ applied along the crystal's easy axis ($B\parallel c$), the system exhibits long-range interactions dominated by competing double-exchange and itinerant Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) mechanisms, which is systematically suppressed with increasing $B$ to converge toward the long-range 3D Ising universality class ($\beta\sim0.330$, $\gamma\sim1.00$). Conversely, under an in-plane hard-axis configuration ($B\perp c$), the critical behavior study exhibits a sub-unity regime ($\gamma<1$) across the measured range of $B$. This behavior is attributed to the intense competitive interplay between the external Zeeman energy and strong out-of-plane magnetocrystalline anisotropy. Our findings demonstrate that critical behavior in nontrivial van der Waals magnets is fundamentally modulated by the orientation of $B$ and the strength of magnetocrystalline anisotropy, demonstrating the necessity of $B$-dependent critical behavior investigation over conventional frameworks.

</details>


### [31] [Materials research with a general-purpose agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15451)
*Paulo R. Pires,Th\'eo Cavignac,Tiago F. T. Cerqueira,Hai-Chen Wang,Silvana Botti,Miguel A. L. Marques*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建约1500万晶体DFT计算数据库（PBE/PBEsol/SCAN/TB-mBJ、最高150 GPa弛豫、介电张量、约14.5万电子-声子计算及超4亿机器学习势弛豫结构）；训练晶体结构生成模型、通用原子间势和十余种性质GNN；由凸包构建193,416种稳定化合物间174亿反应的图，并交由通用语言模型智能体无人值守使用。

Conclusion: 数据、模型与反应图的集成使通用智能体能够完成各组件单独无法完成的任务：从自然语言问题生成实验室可执行和可检验的材料实验方案。

Abstract: Over the last decade we have built Alexandria, an open database of about 15 million density-functional calculations of crystals. It includes PBE, PBEsol, SCAN and TB-mBJ results, structures relaxed at pressures up to 150 GPa, dielectric tensors and about 145,000 electron-phonon calculations, together with more than 400 million structures relaxed with machine-learned potentials. On these data we trained a generative model for crystal structures, a universal interatomic potential and graph neural networks for more than ten properties, from band gaps to superconducting critical temperatures. From the convex hull we computed a reaction graph of the 17.4 billion reactions between its 193,416 stable compounds. Here we give all of this to a general-purpose language-model agent. Working unattended, the agent screens the database for a solar absorber, drops a candidate whose synthesis releases too little energy, and writes a synthesis plan with precursors, crucible and atmosphere. Asked about the well-studied Ba-Zr-S system, it finds an unreported sulfide and designs the experiment that would confirm or rule it out. Asked for an air-stable alternative, it designs one and tests it against new structures from the generative model. With web search alone, the same agent proposes what the literature already discusses. Put together, the data, the models and the reaction graph let a general agent do what none of them does alone: go from a question in plain words to an experiment that a laboratory can run and test.

</details>


### [32] [Defect Topology Governs the Optical Response of Doped Amorphous Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15478)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用混合泛函密度泛函理论和线性响应计算，构建四种结构不同的连续随机网络，并进行替位硼和磷掺杂，结合Tauc分析与光学求和规则分析光学性质。

Result: 计算复现关键实验趋势；掺杂敏感性不仅取决于缺陷浓度，还取决于缺陷性质与空间组织。完全扩展的四配位构型对掺杂的介电扰动最强；存在预存缺陷态的网络的掺杂效应更多样。多数网络中硼的介电响应扰动强于磷；但含3配位和5配位缺陷且总缺陷分数7.4%的网络出现反转，对磷更敏感。Tauc和求和规则表明网络拓扑决定整体光学景观，掺杂主要影响带隙附近态并重新分配谱重。

Conclusion: 缺陷拓扑是连接非晶结构、掺杂活性与光学响应的微观控制参数，为理解和大非晶硅光学调控提供依据。

Abstract: The influence of defect topology and chemical doping on the optical response of amorphous silicon (a-Si) remains unresolved. In this study, hybrid-functional density functional theory and linear-response calculations are employed to examine four structurally distinct continuous random networks with substitutional boron and phosphorus doping. The calculated optical properties reproduce key experimental trends and demonstrate that dopant sensitivity is determined not only by defect concentration, but also by the character and spatial organization of defects. A configuration with fully extended 4-fold coordination shows the strongest dielectric perturbation to doping, while dopant effects in networks with pre-existing defect states are more varied. For most networks, the dielectric response is more strongly perturbed by boron than phosphorus. Notably, a network having both 3- and 5-fold defects with a total defect fraction of 7.4% reverses the typical boron-dominant response of other cases, becoming more sensitive to phosphorus. Tauc and optical sum-rule analyses further indicate that network topology defines the broader optical landscape, whereas doping primarily affects states near the gap and redistributes spectral weight. These findings establish defect topology as a microscopic control parameter that links amorphous structure, dopant activity, and optical response in amorphous silicon.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据极性模软化无法可靠推断应变BaSnO3的相稳定性，需要理解极性、旋转等晶格不稳定性之间的竞争及非谐耦合对结构弛豫的影响。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算双轴压应变下BaSnO3的晶格动力学，并进行双模能量面分析以研究极性序参量与旋转序参量的相互作用。

Result: 极性与旋转序参量主要呈竞争关系；A点和M点八面体旋转在区中心极性模之前失稳，并在中等应变下主导谐波响应，但其转变连续、无非谐晶格耦合且应变能量增益适中；大应变可诱导一阶转变为超四方极性态，其极化强度和铁电能量阱深度可与压应变BaTiO3相当。

Abstract: We use density functional theory to map the lattice-dynamical landscape of BaSnO3 under biaxial compressive strain and uncover the hierarchy of structural instabilities. Our results demonstrate that the phase stability of strained BaSnO3 cannot be inferred from the softening of the polar mode alone. Instead it is governed by the competition among lattice instabilities and their anharmonic coupling to structural relaxation. Two-mode energy-surface analyses show that the polar and rotational order parameters interact predominantly competitively. While A- and M-point octahedral rotations become unstable before the zone-center polar mode and dominate the harmonic response for moderate values of strain, they are related to a continuous transition without anharmonic lattice coupling and a moderate gain of energy with strain. Large strain may induce a first order transition to a supertetragonal polar state. Once this state is stabilized, the magnitude of polarization and ferroelectric energy well depth are comparable to BaTiO3 under compressive strain. Furthermore, we discuss how an electric field or electron doping may tip the balance between the different modes and favor of the supertetragonal structure over antipolar and rotational alternatives at a reduced critical strain.

</details>


### [34] [Cation-intermixing driven evolution from a narrow avoided crossing to an enlarged inverted gap in Ge$_2$Bi$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15631)

Abstract: Optically addressable electron spins are promising platforms for quantum technologies, with molecular systems offering chemical control over their spin and optical properties. Abstract: We demonstrate that optically driven chiral phonons induce an anomalous Hall response in a nonmagnetic, centrosymmetric material. Abstract: Negative thermal expansion (NTE) in framework materials arises from complex lattice dynamics that are challenging to capture using conventional theoretical approaches. Quasi-harmonic lattice-dynamical calculations identify low-frequency phonon modes with negative Gr\"uneisen parameters as inexpensive screening descriptors, while TDEP incorporates finite-temperature anharmonicity and ML-MD further reveals temperature-dependent structural distortions that drive the lattice contraction. The observations are further corroborated with X-ray diffraction (XRD) measurements. The concurrent increase in transverse displacement (uT) provides microscopic evidence for a quasi-rigid-unit-mode (QRUM) mechanism underlying the NTE behaviour at elevated temperatures. This is consistent with previous reports on other framework materials, including ZrW2O7, where collective polyhedral rotations and transverse atomic motions have been linked to NTE. Overall, our results demonstrate how relatively inexpensive lattice-dynamical descriptors can identify potential NTE materials, while higher-level anharmonic simulations can then resolve the underlying structural mechanisms at the atomic scale.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 DFT 哈密顿量模型能高效地从原子结构预测电子性质，但快速哈密顿量预测中的不确定性估计仍具挑战；需要可靠不确定性来筛选候选材料并优先进行第一性原理验证。

Method: 提出 EviDTB，采用证据深度回归估计预测不确定性，单次前向传播完成；与深度集成、Monte Carlo dropout 和均值-方差估计（MVE）进行对比。

Result: 在 5,478 个留出测试结构上，完整自旋-轨道耦合（SOC）哈密顿量矩阵元平均绝对误差为 0.622 meV；每原子平均不确定性与平均局部平方预测误差相关（Spearman ρ=0.812）；剔除不确定性最高的 15% 结构后，该误差的 99 分位数从 16.63 降至 3.72 eV²，降低 78%。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了实用的不确定性引导筛选手段，可优先选择候选结构进行第一性原理验证，有望加速目标电子性质材料的搜索。

Abstract: Universal density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonian models trained across broad chemical and structural spaces offer an efficient route from atomic structure to electronic properties. However, estimating prediction uncertainty alongside fast Hamiltonian predictions remains a challenge. In this work, we present EviDTB, an evidential deep regression approach for tight-binding DFT Hamiltonians that effectively estimates prediction uncertainty in a single forward pass. On 5,478 held-out test structures, EviDTB achieves a full spin--orbit-coupled (SOC) Hamiltonian matrix-element mean absolute error of 0.622meV. Its mean uncertainty per atom correlates with the mean local squared prediction error (Spearman $\rho=0.812$); removing the 15% most uncertain structures reduces the 99th percentile of this error by 78%, from 16.63 to 3.72eV$^2$. This score offers a practical way to prioritize candidates for first-principles verification. We hope that combining fast Hamiltonian prediction with uncertainty-guided screening will accelerate the search for materials with desirable electronic properties.

</details>

Result: NMC811/石墨在0.3C和1C下保持约99.7%初始放电容量，2C降至72.3%，3C降至63.5%；LCO/石墨在2C仅保持11.1%。NRA显示两种体系在2C时石墨负极锂累积最大，SEM观察到颗粒状表面沉积；XRD显示正极(003)和石墨(002)反射随倍率发生偏移。3C下锂累积较低与电荷吞吐量减少有关，而LCO/石墨3C条件受充电协议限制。

Abstract: Fast charging accelerates degradation in lithium-ion batteries, but the effects of cathode chemistry and charging protocol remain difficult to separate. Here, commercial NMC811/graphite (200 mAh) and LCO/graphite (45 mAh) coin cells were cycled for 100 cycles at 0.3C, 1C, 2C, and 3C, followed by scanning electron microscopy (SEM), X-ray diffraction (XRD), and nuclear reaction analysis (NRA). XRD showed rate-dependent shifts in cathode (003) and graphite (002) reflections, indicating structural changes during cycling. The lower lithium accumulation at 3C was associated with reduced charge throughput, while the LCO/graphite 3C condition was limited by its charging protocol. These findings highlight the importance of anode-side degradation and charging protocols in interpreting high-rate cycling performance of commercial lithium-ion batteries.

The alloy therefore offers a promising balance of processability, strength and oxidation resistance for high-temperature static and rotating components.or grain boundary phases. We show that the vibrational mode of TiO$_6$ octahedral rotation around negative-expansion axis can promote spontaneous polarization reduction with increasing temperature, making transformation of PbTiO$_3$ from tetragonal to cubic phase occur, via the projected amplitude, potential energy surface, and forces of individual atoms.

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 报道了金刚石中新发现的负电镓空位（GaV$^-$）的物理性质，其结合了NV$^-$与group-IV空位的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV$^-$易受电荷噪声影响且零声子线发射弱，而group-IV空位通常需要低温工作，因此需要兼具室温自旋相干、高发光效率和电荷噪声保护的新型量子缺陷。

Abstract: Solid-state quantum defects (point crystal imperfections with optically addressable spins) are among the most promising platforms for quantum information science due to their long-lived electron and nuclear spin lifetimes. The negatively-charged nitrogen-vacancy (NV$^-$) and negatively-charged group IV vacancies (G4V's) in diamond are some the most promising quantum defects but, the NV$^-$ is highly susceptible to charge noise and has low emission from its zero-phonon line while the G4V's require low operating temperatures. Here we report the physical properties of the newly discovered negatively-charged gallium-vacancy in diamond, GaV$^-$. We find that the GaV$^-$, created through FIB implantation and high-pressure high-temperature annealing, exhibits a combination of the best properties of both the NV$^-$ and G4V's: optically-detectable spin coherence at room temperature ($T_2(293K) = 1.06 \mu$s), a large Debye-Waller factor $\sim$0.4, a short radiative lifetime ($\sim$3.83 ns), strong temperature dependence of the zero-field splitting (16x that of the NV$^-$), and charge-noise-protected D$_{3d}$ symmetry. These features (along with improved performance at low temperature) position the newly discovered GaV$^-$ as a key resource for quantum sensing, networking, and computation.

</details>


### [48] [ORCHID-SAGE: Fully Reproducible CALPHAD Assessment from Evidence to Phase Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16308)
*Raymundo Arr\'oyave*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出ORCHID-SAGE标准，将CALPHAD数据库构建中的专家判断编码为可复现、可自动化的正式协议和构建记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相稳定性预测对材料设计至关重要，但CALPHAD热力学数据库构建依赖未记录的专家判断，导致数据库可被使用却难以复现、再生或更新。

Method: 将评估过程拆解为可记录的形式化协议，组织数据库构建的完整决策链；以Cr-Ni体系为例，依据记录由脚本在另一台机器重建数据库，并由仅通过MCP工作的AI智能体及仅用公开来源的重新拟合进行复现。

Result: 三种方式均得到相同预测，表明数据库可从其构建记录中可复现地再生，并可支持AI智能体自动化操作。

Conclusion: 数据库不应在发表后固定不变；在专家判断仍由人负责并留痕的前提下，AI智能体可结合SDLs等新证据持续更新数据库。

Abstract: Knowledge of phase stability is the first requirement for materials design, and predicting the conditions under which new materials can be synthesized has become a key capability of self-driving laboratories (SDLs). For inorganic materials, notably alloys, these predictions come from thermodynamic databases built with the CALPHAD method. Building a database, however, requires expert judgment at many steps, and because these decisions are rarely recorded, others can use a database but cannot regenerate or update it. Machines can already collect the data, build a first model with machine-learned interatomic potentials and refine it against experiments. The assessment itself, the sequence of judgments that turns these parts into a reliable database, is still carried out by human experts. We argue that it can be automated reproducibly only if the expert knowledge behind an assessment is encoded in formal protocols, so that an assessment delivers a record of its construction and not only its parameters. We introduce ORCHID-SAGE, a standard that organizes this record into reproducible and automatable protocols. In this first attempt, applied to Cr-Ni, the database was regenerated from its record by a script on a second machine, by an AI agent working only through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and by a refit from public sources only, with the same predictions. Recorded in this way, a database need no longer be fixed at publication but can be updated by AI agents as SDLs and other sources provide new evidence, while the judgment remains with people and on the record.

</details>

Abstract: Resistive random-access memory devices work under the principle of resistive switching from high resistive state to low resistive state under applied electric field. In this work, 2%Mn doped SrTiO3, devices were fabricated using PLD in which, the defects are engineered such that the conducting filament formation is expected to have two oppositely charged defects: a) presence of Mn3+ dopants, giving rise to a negatively charged Mn and Ti and b) single and double positive oxygen vacancies. This process of two filaments emanating from both the electrodes has shown enhancement in the resistive switching characteristics aiding in forming-free switching that exhibits over 102 as ON/OFF ratio with a memory retention beyond 103 seconds at a low read voltage of 0.1V. All devices demonstrated a stable bipolar resistive switching dominated by conduction filament formation resulting from a synergic effect of oxygen vacancies and the varying oxidation states of Mn based negative defects that propagates toward each other under bias. The formation of defect-based filaments around the bias electrode was evidently observed using XPS of the devices before and after switching. A dynamic tunnelling process is observed as an intermediate process before the conduction filament forms in the SET region and following the snapping in the RESET region. Conduction filaments being heavily doped localizes conduction pathways with a small finite tunnelling barrier gap that are instantaneously formed and ruptures during switching cycles. The dynamic tunnelling behavior gives rise to a tiny spike in current and is observed at the intermediate resistive states which are potential for multi-state usage of these devices. Overall, Mn-STO devices of varying thicknesses show promising retention features and defect engineering offers control over single and/or multi-states memory.

</details>

*Honghui Shang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用多参考神经变分辅助场量子蒙特卡洛研究二维Hubbard模型中条纹序与d波超导的竞争，发现半填充反相畴壁可产生本征局域d波配对，但仅抑制静态条纹序并不能形成跨条纹的相干配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Hubbard模型中条纹究竟竞争还是通过涨落促进d波超导仍无定论，尤其需要区分条纹涨落、畴壁局域配对与跨反铁磁畴的长程配对相干。

Abstract: Whether stripes compete with $d$-wave superconductivity or promote pairing through fluctuations remains unsettled in the two-dimensional Hubbard model. Using multi-reference neural variational auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (MR-nVAFQMC) at $U/t=8$, we superpose unpaired Slater determinants across stripe periods and domain-wall positions, reaching variational energies competitive with leading benchmarks on periodic lattices up to $32\times32$ sites. For cuprate next-nearest-neighbor hopping ($t'/t=-0.2$), diagonal hopping preserves electron-doped antiferromagnetism while kinetically frustrating hole motion into Yamada stripes with a dome of short-range connected $d$-wave pairing. At $1/8$ hole doping, hole delocalization across antiphase spin boundaries outweighs on-site repulsion, splitting the filled stripes of the pure model into half-filled walls with strong local pairing. Coherently delocalizing shifted walls strongly suppresses static charge modulation yet leaves short-range pairing unchanged, showing that local pairing is intrinsic to half-filled walls rather than driven by stripe fluctuations. Across intervening antiferromagnetic domains, however, pairing correlations become unresolved at one stripe period and beyond. Both parameter transfer to larger lattices without reoptimization and deep benchmark checkpoints retain stripe spin order without resolving long-range pairing, within uncertainties allowing weak superconductivity. Half-filled antiphase walls thus generate local $d$-wave pairs, whereas suppressing static stripe order alone yields no resolved coherence across stripes in the states studied.

</details>

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are increasingly used as computationally efficient surrogates for density functional theory (DFT) in high-throughput nanomaterials screening. Beyond the full site- and bond-resolved landscapes, we perform a controlled matched-reconstruction comparison in which 28 vacancy and divacancy configurations are scored by both engines using self-consistent chemical-potential reservoirs. In this dataset, SevenNet underestimates all 28 vacancy and divacancy formation energies, and the mean deficit increases with under-coordination within each defect family: 0.30-0.74 eV for monovacancies and 0.65-1.11 eV for divacancies as the number of broken edge C-H bonds increases. By contrast, the mean Stone-Wales energies differ by only 0.013 eV. SevenNet also preserves the ordering of three hydrogen-dependent reconstruction basins and reproduces $D_{6h}$-equivalent sites to within a few meV. The within-class symmetry spread therefore provides a practical diagnostic of basin-sensitive relaxations. Within the present coronene benchmark, these results support MLIP-based structure screening and preliminary ranking, while absolute vacancy energies and multi-basin edge configurations still require DFT validation.

Abstract: We investigate domain wall (DW) dynamics and morphology in amorphous Co$_{0.86}$Zr$_{0.14}$ thin films patterned with engineered, density-graded triangular-hole arrays. Using Kerr microscopy and high-resolution XMCD-PEEM imaging, we demonstrate that DW propagation is governed by the interplay between hole orientation, density gradients, and local magnetostatic interactions. In cooperative configurations, the density gradient reinforces the geometric ratchet effect, whereas in competing configurations, the two mechanisms oppose each other, leading to an inverse ratchet and enabling experimental access to backward DW propagation. Remarkably, density-graded arrays substantially reduce the stochastic variability of depinning fields observed in periodic arrays, resulting in highly reproducible DW motion. The depinning fields are described by a rigid DW model and an empirical scaling law incorporating both inter-hole spacing and array periodicity. These results demonstrate that engineered density gradients provide an effective route to deterministic control of DW propagation, with potential applications in spintronic and neuromorphic devices.

Abstract: Materials with large linear electro-optic (Pockels) effect have significant potential for fabricating energy-efficient photonic devices. Previous strategies have mostly relied on structural softness---associated with phase boundary, domain wall, or strain induced phase transition---to create large Pockels effect in ferroelectrics. In this Letter, we show that polarization reorientation in multiaxial ferroelectrics may create structural softness and thus induce large Pockels effect. By developing a phenomenological theory, we find that multiaxial ferroelectrics exhibit a hysteretic relationship between the azimuth angles of the polarization and the electric field, and a possible divergence of the linear electro-optic coefficient may occur at an appropriate polarization azimuth angle. This suggests a brand-new strategy for engineering large Pockels effect in multiaxial ferroelectrics. We perform first-principles simulations on several multiaxial ferroelectrics and predict that polarization reorientation can significantly enhance the electro-optic coefficients in monolayer SnSe and bulk KNbO$_3$. This validates the soundness of our strategy and emphasizes multiaxial ferroelectrics as promising platforms toward large Pockels effect.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML模型常依赖任意或通用采样构建的大数据集，易导致与预测任务最相关的化学/材料空间区域被过度或不足表示；核心挑战是在有限标注预算下高效筛选训练数据以提升预测性能。

Abstract: Machine-learning (ML) models for molecular and materials property prediction are often trained on large datasets curated through arbitrary or generic sampling strategies. Such approaches can lead to the over- or under-representation of regions of chemical and materials space that are most relevant to the prediction task. An important challenge is therefore how to efficiently curate training data to maximize predictive performance while minimising the number of labelled examples required. Here, we systematically compare training data acquisition strategies for molecular and materials property prediction, ranging from random sampling to various active learning approaches. We evaluate their performance across different model classes, representations, datasets, and target properties. Our results show that the effectiveness of data acquisition strategies depend strongly on the interplay between the model, dataset, and prediction task, although uncertainty-based sampling generally outperforms alternative strategies. Based on these findings, we derive guidelines for selecting data acquisition strategies under a given labelling budget.

</details>

Motivation: 圆偏振声子的光学激发可产生非平衡现象，但此前基于泵浦-探测偏振旋转的实验是否能证明存在可观“真实”磁化存在争议，原因包括非平衡法拉第效应解释和虚假非线性光学贡献等混杂因素。

Result: 观测到驱动SiC中巨大的内部偏振旋转；若依赖不同假设，该信号可对应高达μ0M=0.3 T或低至4 μT的磁化。但外部杂散磁场测量明确给出μ0M<0.001 T，说明SiC中的磁化可忽略。

Abstract: The optical excitation of circular phonons has opened up non-equilibrium phenomena with no equilibrium counterparts, ranging from the generation of synthetic magnetic fields to the control of topological order or the manipulation of ferroaxial states. In this area, significant controversy has arisen on whether sizeable "true" magnetizations accompany circular phonons. Several confounding factors cloud the interpretation of the experiments, which are based on probing polarization rotation in the driven material. These range from the interpretation of the Faraday effect in non-equilibrium conditions to spurious nonlinear optical contributions in pump probe experiments. Here we resolve these ambiguities by measuring both polarization rotation inside the driven material and the stray magnetic field generated outside it with a proximate Faraday detector. For a 5 MV/cm mid-infrared drive, we observe a colossal polarization rotation inside a driven SiC crystal, similar to what was reported in previous work. Depending on the assumptions made, this could correspond to magnetizations as high as $\mu_0 M = 0.3 T$ or as low as 4 $\mu T$. However, when measuring the magnetic stray field outside the driven sample we unambiguously determine that the magnetization in SiC is negligibly small ($\mu_0 M \lt 0.001 T$).

</details>

Abstract: Ceramic materials that maintain structural integrity and resist thermal-shock under extreme conditions are critical for many applications. Here we show that spark plasma sintered composites containing equal weight fractions of hexagonal silicon carbide (6H-SiC) and boron nitride (h-BN) exhibit exceptional stability under extreme high-temperature oxidizing conditions and unusually stable temperature-independent thermal conductivity across a broad temperature range. Under high-velocity oxygen-fuel torch exposure in air, it withstands prolonged heating and thermal-cycling at surface temperatures reaching >1500 {\deg}C, whereas pure 6H-SiC and h-BN rapidly fractures or erodes. Microstructural evaluation shows the formation of a multi-phase surface silica layer that further protects the material from oxidation. Finite-element analysis heat-flux map shows that intrinsic porosity mitigates thermal stress, while ReaxFF molecular dynamics simulations reveal thermally driven interfacial reconstruction and the formation of a Si-N/C-B-rich bonding network, enhancing the interfacial cohesion at elevated temperatures. This lightweight, easily machinable ceramic composite will be a promising structural material in extreme environment applications.
