### [59] [Correlation-Consistent Gaussian Basis Sets for Periodic Solids. I. Hydrogen and p-block Elements from Boron to Chlorine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14768)
*Jincheng Yu,Hong-Zhou Ye*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 作者提出了面向固体的相关一致高斯基组 cc-pVXZ-s（X=D,T,Q），覆盖氢及硼至氯的 p 区元素，并验证其在周期体系中的精度、可迁移性与数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子 cc-pVXZ 基组难以直接用于固体计算，常出现过扩散函数和数值不稳定问题，因此需要为周期计算构建系统可收敛且兼容全电子与赝势/ECP 的相关一致基组。

Method: 将 Dunning 分子 cc-pVXZ 的构造与改进的 material-constrained atomic optimization（MCAO）算法结合；MCAO 对标准原子优化进行正则化，在参考固体中抑制过度弥散的函数并保证数值稳定性。基组同时覆盖全电子势及常用赝势/ECP，并通过分子 cc-pVXZ 与周期平面波计算进行基准测试。

Result: 基准表明 cc-pVXZ-s 在平均场和相关波函数计算中具有良好精度、可迁移性和数值稳定性，并可可靠外推至 CBS 极限。应用全电子 cc-pVXZ-s 对 7 种体相半导体计算 CBS 收敛的晶格常数、体模量和内聚能；在最高至 CCSD(T) 的相关波函数方法层级下，体模量和内聚能系统收敛至实验值，而晶格常数存在小幅系统性高估。

Abstract: We present the cc-pVXZ-s series (X = D, T, and Q) of correlation-consistent Gaussian basis sets for solids, covering hydrogen and the p-block elements from boron to chlorine. The series includes basis sets of consistent quality for the all-electron potential and for various pseudopotentials and effective core potentials commonly used in periodic calculations. Our design combines Dunning's construction of the molecular cc-pVXZ series with a revised material-constrained atomic optimization (MCAO) algorithm for improved numerical performance. MCAO regularizes standard atomic optimization to suppress overly diffuse basis functions and ensure numerical stability in a set of reference solids. Benchmarks against standard cc-pVXZ calculations for molecules and plane-wave calculations for periodic solids establish the accuracy, transferability, and numerical stability of cc-pVXZ-s in both mean-field and correlated wavefunction calculations and confirm reliable extrapolation to the complete-basis-set (CBS) limit. As an application, we use all-electron cc-pVXZ-s to compute basis-set-converged lattice constants, bulk moduli, and cohesive energies for 7 bulk semiconductors. Using a hierarchy of correlated wavefunction methods up to local coupled cluster with singles, doubles, and perturbative triples [CCSD(T)], we find systematic convergence toward experimental bulk moduli and cohesive energies, while a small systematic overestimation of lattice constants suggests potential limitations in the approximate finite-size treatment. This work provides a foundation for the further development of systematically convergent Gaussian basis sets for periodic calculations.

</details>

Motivation: 现代MLP虽能高精度大尺度模拟，但扩展到电化学/电催化时需考虑电极电势和电场对原子相互作用的影响，现有方法仍不成熟，需概念性进展以支持有限温度、施加电势下的可靠模拟。

Method: 综述将当前发展分为三类：1）描述极化、极化率和Born有效电荷的线性响应模型，将传统MLP扩展到有限电场；2）直接学习场依赖势能面的方法；3）结合经典模型与机器学习的混合方法。

Abstract: Modern machine learning potentials (MLPs) enable large-scale atomistic simulations at unprecedented accuracy. The incorporation of explicit physical interactions such as long-range electrostatics has continuously broadened their applicability to increasingly complex systems in chemistry and materials science. Extending MLPs to electrochemistry and electrocatalysis, however, is still challenging as it requires the consideration of electrode potentials and electric fields in the atomic interactions. Such approaches have emerged only recently, and although the field is evolving rapidly, further conceptual advances are still needed for carrying out reliable simulations at finite temperature under applied potential. In this review, we provide a comprehensive overview about recent progress in the atomistic simulation of electrified interfaces using MLPs. Current developments fall into three different categories. The first comprises linear response models that describe polarisation, polarisability, and Born effective charges, extending conventional MLPs to finite electric fields. The second includes methods that directly learn a field-dependent potential energy surface. The third consists of a series of hybrid approaches combining classical models with machine learning. Although still under active development, these methods represent a major step toward more realistic simulations approaching the quality of electronic structure calculations. Finally, we discuss open challenges and possible future directions for advancing MLP-based simulations aiming for a bottom-up understanding of atomistic processes at electrified interfaces.

Upon impact with the surrounding supersaturated liquid, this microjet generates intense localized shear, thereby inducing crystallization of the model inorganic salt, potassium permanganate. Under identical laser energies and supersaturation levels, crystallization was not observed in single-bubble experiments, in which no microjet was present. We show that the crystallization probability increases with microjet velocity. Boundary integral simulations reveal how experimental parameters such as confinement, bubble spacing, and laser energy govern microjet formation and velocity. We quantify the relationship between jet velocity and the observed enhancement in crystal nucleation kinetics by incorporating shear into the kinetic prefactor in classical nucleation theory. Conclusion: 该研究为DRM机制提供了单颗粒定量约束，表明颗粒水平偏置可解释DRM效率差；并指出该偏置为磁矿物作为天然手性剂提供了定量基础，可能与前生命地球生物分子同手性起源有关。

Abstract: Detrital remanent magnetization (DRM), the alignment of ferromagnetic grains with the geomagnetic field during sedimentation, is the primary mechanism of paleomagnetic remanence acquisition in sedimentary rocks, yet its microphysical mechanism remains contested. Measured DRM intensities consistently fall below theoretical predictions-the long-standing "DRM efficiency gap"-and no single-particle resolving observations have been available to test DRM acquisition models directly. We compare these measurements with the results of bulk magnetometry and a Langevin-based kinetic model. We observe a statistically significant mean alignment of individual detrital particle moments with the applied field, $10\%$ when averaged as unit vectors and $24\%$ when weighted by moment magnitude, with the vector sum of all particles reproducing the direction of the bulk DRM, which is $\sim1.8\%$ of saturation remanence. Particle-level analysis shows that alignment rises with particle moment, a trend reproduced by our kinetic model in which floc-hosted magnetite rotates toward the field until the surrounding sediment locks it in place. Even where post-depositional disturbance is minimal and the measured DRM reaches the upper end of reported efficiencies, it remains well below its theoretical maximum, limited in comparable measure by incomplete particle-level alignment and sub-saturation within-particle magnetization. Beyond its implications for the DRM mechanism, this strong particle-level bias provides a quantitative foundation for magnetic minerals as natural chiral agents, a proposed pathway for the emergence of biomolecular homochirality on the prebiotic Earth.

<details>

Conclusion: EVE与HADES构成可公开获取、代码透明的实用预测框架，可显著提升非理想液体混合物扩散系数的预测可靠性。

Abstract: Experimental data on mutual diffusion coefficients in liquid mixtures are scarce, and existing prediction methods are unreliable for non-ideal mixtures. Its inputs are molecular descriptors derived from SMILES and activity-coefficient information from HANNA, a machine-learning model for activity coefficients. EVE predicts Maxwell--Stefan diffusion coefficients; the corresponding Fickian coefficients follow from the thermodynamic factor, likewise obtained from HANNA. Trained and evaluated on 2106 experimental data points for 123 binary mixtures, EVE outperforms all available semi-empirical models, halving the mean absolute relative error for mixtures without experimental infinite-dilution diffusion data. Combined with HADES, EVE yields the complete set of self- and mutual diffusion coefficients of a binary mixture from SMILES and pure-component viscosities alone. Abstract: The prospect of exothermic reverse intersystem crossing (rISC) in organic chromophores with inverted singlet-triplet (INVEST) excited states has stimulated considerable interest in next-generation emitters. However, realizing efficient spin conversion remains challenging because energetic inversion does not necessarily imply the appreciable spin-orbit coupling (SOC) required for rISC. Here, we investigate direct ${\rm S}_1-{\rm T}_1$ SOC in 85 INVEST candidates comprising 32 azaphenalenes, 7 non-alternant hydrocarbons, and 46 boron-nitrogen-substituted polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (BNPAHs), all previously predicted to exhibit negative singlet-triplet gaps using a correlated wavefunction method at reliably optimized molecular geometries. While the azaphenalenes and non-alternant hydrocarbons exhibit negligible direct SOC, 28 of the 46 BNPAHs display finite coupling. Analysis of molecular geometries and natural transition orbitals suggests that SOC variations depend on molecular topology, orbital orientation, and electronic-state symmetry rather than nonplanarity or excited-state character alone. We estimate direct-rISC rates with a Marcus model, identifying selected BNPAHs with rates approaching $10^7\,{\rm s}^{-1}$.

Result: 在大量子区域（平均约440个分子、表面体积比小）中，偶极重正化储层如预期诱导量子子系统呈现Grand Canonical统计；在较小量子区域（平均约190个分子、表面体积条件较差）中，该储层相对于未重正化柔性TIP3P显著提高了与QM区域的分子一致性。

Abstract: Open-boundary Quantum Mechanics/Molecular Mechanics (QM/MM) simulation provides a direct way to test whether a quantum subsystem and its surrounding classical reservoir are physically consistent. Here we use this framework to replace a standard flexible TIP3P reservoir by a dipole-renormalized (DR) Amber-compatible three-site water model optimized against a full \textit{ab initio} trajectory of liquid water. The renormalization targets the molecular dipole distribution and adjusts the atomic charges, bonded, and Lennard-Jones interaction parameters through a stochastic optimization procedure. In the case of a large QM region ($\langle N\rangle\approx 440$), where the surface-to-volume ratio is small, the DR reservoir induces, as expected, Grand Canonical statistics for the quantum subsystem. In smaller subsystems ($\langle N\rangle\approx 190$), where the surface-to-volume condition is deliberately less favorable, the same reservoir shows substantially improved molecular consistency with the QM region relative to the non-renormalized flexible TIP3P model. These results show that quantum information at molecular dipole level is crucial for the microscopic physics of liquid water. When this concept is transferred into a classical reservoir, it can be used to extend the reliable regime of adaptive open-QM/MM water simulations even beyond the physically dictated Grand Canonical conditions. \end{abstract

### [66] [HARMONY: an efficient and accurate deep learning framework for prediction of experimental vibrational spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16342)
*Xinyu Lu,Bowen Li,Yi Rong,Qingling Zhou,Xingbo Tang,Menghan Li,Yuqiang Li,Guokun Liu,Tong Zhu,Bin Ren,Hao Ma*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: HARMONY 是一个两阶段深度学习框架，以谐振光谱作为物理锚点：等变神经网络快速预测原子力与光谱张量以构建谐振光谱，HarmoNet 结合分子拓扑与测量元数据预测实验红外/拉曼光谱。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验振动光谱预测既需要高效分子计算，也需要从理论振动到实测信号的可靠映射；同时该映射还可服务于逆推分子结构。

Method: 第一阶段用等变神经网络直接预测原子力和光谱张量，快速构建谐振光谱；第二阶段 HarmoNet 将谐振光谱、分子拓扑和测量元数据融合，预测实验红外与拉曼光谱。框架还通过伪实验预训练和候选结构光谱验证改进逆推分子推断，并与 Vib2Mol 集成为通用智能体工具。

Result: 红外和拉曼平均余弦相似度分别为 0.912 和 0.907，单分子平均推理时间 2.12 s，约为所评估 DFT 流程的 600 倍加速。给定分子式时，Vib2Mol–HARMONY Agent 在 NIST 红外数据上 Top-1 召回率 70.53%，在 SDBS 联合红外/拉曼数据上 83.33%。用 19 个氨基酸对照和 8 个肽微调后，15 个留出肽的平均拉曼余弦相似度从 0.669 提升至 0.896。

Abstract: Predicting experimental vibrational spectra requires both efficient molecular calculations and a reliable mapping from theoretical vibrations to measured signals. Here we introduce \textbf{HARMONY}, a two-stage deep-learning framework that bridges this gap using harmonic spectra as physical anchors. Equivariant neural networks directly predict atomic forces and spectroscopic tensors for rapid harmonic-spectrum construction, while HarmoNet combines these spectra with molecular topology and measurement metadata to predict experimental infrared and Raman spectra. HARMONY achieves mean cosine similarities of 0.912 for infrared and 0.907 for Raman spectra, with a mean inference time of 2.12~s per molecule, approximately 600-fold faster than the evaluated DFT workflow. Beyond forward prediction, HARMONY improves inverse molecular inference through pseudo-experimental pretraining and spectral verification of candidate structures. Integrating HARMONY and Vib2Mol as scientific tools within a general-purpose agent enables adaptive candidate generation and verification. With molecular formulas provided, the resulting Vib2Mol--HARMONY Agent achieves Top-1 recall of 70.53\% on NIST using infrared spectra and 83.33\% on SDBS using combined infrared and Raman spectra. Fine-tuning with 19 amino-acid controls and eight peptides further increases mean Raman cosine similarity from 0.669 to 0.896 on 15 held-out peptides. HARMONY establishes harmonic anchoring as a practical connection between molecular physics, experimental spectroscopy and adaptive molecular structure elucidation.

</details>


### [67] [Stable ring-polymer molecular dynamics without Fourier transforms: a linear-scaling bead-space Cayley propagator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16396)
*Yuhao Chen,Yongle Li,Xiao He*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在珠子坐标中实现自由环聚合物 Cayley 步的 RPMD 积分器：每步只需解一个循环三对角系统，避免逐模态 Fourier 变换，并保持与正常模态 Cayley 映射等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPMD 在低温下需要大量珠子，刚性环聚合物弹簧会严重限制积分步长；现有 Cayley 修正虽能解除该限制，但按模态逐项实现，每步都要对所有自由度做 Fourier 变换，开销大。

Method: 作者证明珠子坐标下的 Cayley 步可化为对步中点的单个循环三对角求解，其矩阵仅依赖珠子数 P、温度和时间步长，与原子质量无关；采用 Thomas 递推加秩二 Woodbury 修正，无需选主元，每自由度约 18P 次运算，条件数上界为 1+(ω_PΔt)^2，且逆矩阵随珠子间距几何衰减，因此该步在珠子索引上局部。

Result: 在 PyRPMD 中实现后，该传播子对多达 2048 个珠子与正常模态 Cayley 的复现精度达 1.5×10^-15，成本随 P 线性增长；对直接 RPMD（dRPMD），已发表低温设置下时间步稳定性上限提高 23--29 倍；在 20 K、3072 个珠子时，其环聚合物能量守恒与约十倍大时间步的 velocity Verlet 相当，而控制虚假共振的低 lying 内模态基本不变。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了高效、稳定、可扩展的珠子空间 Cayley 传播子，可显著提升低温 RPMD/dRPMD 的积分步长与计算效率，同时保持正常模态 Cayley 的能量与分布性质。

Abstract: Ring-polymer molecular dynamics (RPMD) builds zero-point energy and tunnelling into classical trajectories and is widely used for bimolecular rate coefficients. At low temperature it requires many beads, and the stiff ring-polymer springs then limit the integrator. The Cayley modification of the free ring-polymer step removes this limit, but it has so far been formulated mode by mode, which requires Fourier transforms of every degree of freedom at every step. Here we show that, in bead coordinates, the Cayley step reduces to one cyclic tridiagonal solve for the midpoint of the step, with a matrix that depends only on the number of beads $P$, the temperature and the time step, and not on the atomic masses. The bead-space step is exactly the normal-mode Cayley map: it conserves the free ring-polymer energy, preserves the free ring-polymer Boltzmann distribution, and is stable for any $P$ whenever $\Omega\Delta t<2$ for the highest physical frequency $\Omega$. A Thomas recursion with a rank-two Woodbury correction solves it in about $18P$ operations per degree of freedom without pivoting, with a condition number bounded by $1+(\omega_P\Delta t)^2$, and the inverse matrix decays geometrically with bead separation, so the step is local in bead index. Implemented in the open-source PyRPMD package, the propagator reproduces normal-mode Cayley to $1.5\times10^{-15}$ for up to 2048 beads and its cost grows linearly with $P$. For direct RPMD (dRPMD), which has so far integrated the springs explicitly, these properties raise the stability limit on the time step 23--29-fold in published low-temperature settings; at \SI{20}{K} with 3072 beads, the Cayley step conserves the ring-polymer energy as well as velocity Verlet does with a time step about ten times larger, while leaving the low-lying internal modes that govern spurious resonances essentially unchanged.

</details>

*Edward G. Hohenstein,Melisa Alkan,Xiaojie Wu,Robert M. Parrish*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始网格基交换虽适合大体系，但绝对和相对误差较大；需要一种既高效又高精度、易实现且可微的交换方案。

Method: 采用对称重叠拟合的网格基交换，在正交化原子轨道基中拟合，并结合基于原子密度叠加差分的dSCF交换能分区，形成补偿型网格交换；方法保持解析可微，并已提供于cuEST v0.3库。

Result: 相较原始网格基交换，绝对和相对误差降低数个数量级；对高质量和/或增广基组，相比GPU积分直接方法具有效率优势；方法易于实现。

Conclusion: 该补偿型网格交换方法适用于大规模量子化学计算，在精度、可微性和效率之间取得良好平衡，并通过cuEST v0.3库方便用户集成。

Abstract: A compensated grid-based approach is developed for hybrid exchange in large-scale molecular systems. The method uses a symmetrically overlap-fitted grid-based exchange approach, with fitting performed in the orthonormalized atomic orbital basis. This fitting is augmented with a superposition-of-atomic-densities difference-density (dSCF) partitioning of the exchange energy, yielding the finished compensated grid-based exchange approach. The complete method is found to have orders of magnitude reduction of absolute and relative error properties versus raw grid-based exchange, and remains analytically differentiable. The method is easy to implement and provides efficiency gains over GPU integral-direct techniques for high quality and/or augmented basis sets. Library functions to help users implement the method in their own quantum chemistry codes are provided in the recently-shipped cuEST v0.3 library.

</details>


### [69] [Dynamics and Statistical Mechanics of Born-Oppenheimer Auxiliary Velocities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16605)
*Paula Sierra,Benjamin Rotenberg,Sara Bonella*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文用Mass-Zero约束动力学把Born-Oppenheimer动力学中辅助变量的时间导数（如电流）作为显式动力学变量处理，并推导其平衡统计性质的不变测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在BO动力学中，电子密度、诱导极化、电极电荷等辅助变量满足稳态条件，但其时间导数（如电流）是物理可观测量，需要一致地纳入统计力学描述。

Method: 采用Mass-Zero约束动力学，将辅助变量的时间导数解释为显式动力学变量，并推导控制其平衡涨落的不变测度；在恒电势分子动力学中展示该框架。

Result: 电极电荷速度能够重现BO电流涨落以及频率依赖的导纳。

Conclusion: Mass-Zero约束动力学为BO辅助变量导数提供严格的统计力学框架，可用于计算电流涨落和频率依赖响应等物理量。

Abstract: In Born-Oppenheimer (BO) dynamics, auxiliary variables such as electron densities, induced polarization and electrode charges follow stationarity conditions, yet their time derivatives, such as currents, are physical observables. Using Mass-Zero constrained dynamics, we interpret these derivatives as explicit dynamical variables and derive the invariant measure giving their equilibrium statistical properties. We illustrate the framework in constant-potential molecular dynamics, where the electrode-charge velocities reproduce BO current fluctuations and the frequency-dependent admittance.

</details>


### [70] [Library of peptides: Analysis of a complex reactive mixture with the use of statistical physics and inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17130)
*Laura Messio,Taleen Peker,Roba Moumn\'e,H\'el\`ene Berthoumieux*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个连接统计物理与分析化学的反卷积框架，可从部分重叠光谱数据中解析复杂自组装混合物的全组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组装混合物物种数量巨大且分析信号严重重叠；传统色谱和光谱反卷积忽略反应网络施加的浓度相关性，难以准确表征。

Method: 借鉴蛋白质折叠的Potts模型，将每种化合物的化学势表示为少量本征构建块能量及序列中相邻构建块间配对耦合项的函数；结合质量作用定律预测整个反应网络的平衡浓度，并通过拟合预测光谱信号与实验部分数据推断模型参数。

Result: 在以81种肽构建的动态组合库（DCL）中，该方法仅用13个重叠峰即解析出完整混合物组成，并发现侧向耦合主导组装能量学。

Conclusion: 该策略为从部分数据解析自组装体系提供了通用途径，并可自然扩展到基于靶标驱动的高亲和力结合物识别。

Abstract: Self-assembled mixtures enable the exploration of vast chemical diversity, yet characterizing the hundreds of coexisting species they contain remains a major challenge. Standard analytical techniques such as chromatography and spectroscopy produce overlapping signals that cannot be resolved by conventional deconvolution methods, which neglect the concentration correlations imposed by the reactive network. Here, we introduce a framework that bridges statistical physics and analytical chemistry to deconvolute complex self-assembled mixtures from partial spectral data. Inspired by Potts models of protein folding, we express the chemical potential of each compound as a function of a reduced set of parameters-intrinsic building-block energies and pairwise coupling terms between neighboring building blocks in the sequence. Coupling this model with the law of mass action yields equilibrium concentrations across the entire reactive network. The model parameters can be infered by fitting predicted spectroscopic signals to experimentally measured partial data. We demonstrate this approach using dynamic combinatorial libraries (DCLs) of peptides as a model system. Applied to a DCL of 81 peptides, our method resolves the full mixture composition from just 13 overlapping peaks, revealing that lateral couplings dominate the assembly energetics. Beyond DCLs, this strategy offers a general route for analyzing self-assembled systems from partial data and extends naturally to target-driven identification of high-affinity binders.

</details>


### [71] [Quantum Boltzmann Equation for Static Correlation with Cumulant Kernel Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17245)
*Romit Chakraborty*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于BBGKY-Wigner-Weyl映射的量子Boltzmann方程，用参数自由的累积量核在零电子温度下高效解析强静态相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式两体累积量方法存在标度瓶颈，且需在动力学弛豫中保持纯N-可表示性并准确处理强静态相关。

Method: 将BBGKY层级通过Wigner-Weyl同构映射为离散原子轨道矩阵力学；把两体累积量投影到最低酉不变量，用单一Coulomb-exchange收缩计算感受率加窗的轨道散射标度；通过Markovian碰撞弛豫使累积量弛豫到双曲正切吸引子，得到参数自由的QBE Cumulant-Kernel以正则化导数尖点且不引入热展宽。

Result: 在H3交换反应、D4h方形H4解离、BeH2非绝热避免交叉、H2O对称键断裂、N2三键解离、MR-MGN-BE17解离测试集及苯炔单重双自由基异构体等案例中，该动力学弛豫在零电子温度下解析强静态相关。

Conclusion: 该QBE累积量核在保持N-可表示性的同时克服显式两体累积量标度瓶颈，为强静态相关提供参数自由、可扩展的动力学弛豫框架。

Abstract: We present a Quantum Boltzmann Equation for static correlation by mapping the Bogoliubov-Born-Green-Kirkwood-Yvon hierarchy to discrete atomic-orbital matrix mechanics via the Wigner-Weyl isomorphism to evaluate scattering of the correlated two-body cumulant. This Quantum Boltzmann Equation converges to the active-space static correlation limit and preserves pure $N$-representability constraints during the kinetic relaxation. To resolve scaling bottlenecks of explicit two-body cumulant methods, we project the two-body cumulant onto its lowest unitary invariant and evaluate a susceptibility-windowed orbital scattering scale via a single Coulomb-exchange contraction. Relaxing onto a hyperbolic-tangent cumulant attractor through Markovian collisional relaxation yields a parameter-free Quantum Boltzmann Equation Cumulant-Kernel that regularizes derivative cusps without thermal smearing. Using the collinear $\mathrm{H}_3$ exchange reaction, the symmetric $D_{4h}$ square $\mathrm{H}_4$ dissociation, the non-adiabatic avoided crossing in $\mathrm{BeH}_2$, the symmetric bond cleavage of $\mathrm{H}_2\mathrm{O}$, the triple-bond dissociation of $\mathrm{N}_2$, the 11-molecule MR-MGN-BE17 dissociation test set, and the singlet diradical isomers of benzyne as case studies, we show that this kinetic relaxation resolves strong static correlation at zero electronic temperature.

</details>


### [72] [Active-space construction in single-fragment coupled-cluster embedding for chemical reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17278)
*Ardavan Farahvash,Timothy C. Berkelbach*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文基准测试单片段量子嵌入：将CCSD(T)相关限制在选定片段的轨道上，其余轨道冻结在Hartree-Fock水平，并评估不同片段活性空间构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全体系CCSD(T)计算成本高，需发展片段量子嵌入以在保持精度的同时降低相关计算规模，并系统比较活性空间构造对反应能和反应剖面的影响。

Method: 比较多种片段活性空间构造，并用统一片段投影算符关联部分轨道构造；在60个有机反应能和三个反应剖面上测试。重点评估基于密度矩阵嵌入理论的regional embedding，以及片段中心投影原子轨道和由片段MP2密度矩阵本征矢定义的片段自然轨道，并考察复合MP2修正。

Result: regional embedding同时用于占据和虚拟活性轨道时准确且稳健；片段中心投影原子轨道和片段自然轨道在虚拟空间中也表现良好，但后者虚拟空间通常更大。复合MP2修正带来最大改进：结合片段自然轨道时，相对全体系CCSD(T)的平均反应能误差低于0.3 kcal/mol，所有个体误差低于1 kcal/mol。反应剖面显示准确端点能量可能掩盖显著势垒误差。

Conclusion: 单片段量子嵌入的性能强烈依赖活性空间构造及被省略相关的近似；regional embedding与复合MP2修正是有效策略，但仅看反应能不足以评估反应剖面精度，需关注势垒和省略相关的影响。

Abstract: We benchmark single-fragment quantum embedding, in which electron correlation---at the level of coupled-cluster theory with single, double, and perturbative triple excitations [CCSD(T)]---is restricted to orbitals associated with a chosen molecular fragment, and the remaining orbitals are frozen at the Hartree-Fock level. We compare several methods for constructing fragment active spaces, and we test their performance on 60 organic reaction energies and three reaction profiles. A common fragment projection operator is used to relate several of the tested orbital constructions. Regional embedding, based on density matrix embedding theory, is shown to give accurate and robust performance when used for both the occupied and virtual active orbitals. For virtual orbitals, fragment-centered projected atomic orbitals and fragment natural orbitals (defined by eigenvectors of a fragment MP2 density matrix) also perform well, but the latter typically retains larger virtual spaces. Composite MP2 corrections yield the largest improvement: combined with fragment natural orbitals, they give average reaction energy errors below 0.3 kcal/mol relative to full-system CCSD(T), with all individual errors below 1 kcal/mol. Reaction profiles show that accurate endpoint energies can conceal substantial barrier errors, emphasizing the importance of approximating omitted correlation.

</details>

### [73] [Susceptibility Bottlenecks in Equilibrium Propagation Using Oscillator Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14727)
*Malihe Farasat,Oshada Rathnayake,Nikhil Shukla*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文研究振荡器伊辛机（OIM）中平衡传播（EP）的学习信号传输，指出隐藏层响应可分解为输出端施加的 nudging force 与跨层 susceptibility；二次谐波注入和耦合形式分别通过相位二值化与能景混合曲率影响二者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EP 依赖自由平衡态与弱扰动平衡态之间的位移进行物理学习，因此需要理解振荡器动力学如何决定扰动/学习信号能否从输出层传到隐藏层；仅实现目标伊辛哈密顿量未必足以保证有效学习。

Method: 采用双分区 OIM，其相互作用可在相位差耦合与相位和耦合之间插值，并保证所有耦合形式在二值锁相态下实现同一伊辛哈密顿量；将隐藏层响应对输出端 nudging force 与跨层 susceptibility 进行分解，并结合数值模拟分析分类性能。

Result: 二次谐波注入主要通过相位二值化影响 nudging force；耦合形式通过能景的混合曲率控制跨层 susceptibility。在等比例混合时，二值流形上的混合曲率消失，显著抑制隐藏层学习信号；数值模拟验证了这些机制及其对分类性能的影响。

Conclusion: 实现期望的能景本身不足以确保有效的物理学习；系统还必须支持学习所需扰动的传输。

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) enables learning in physical systems by exploiting the displacement between free and weakly perturbed equilibrium states. Here, we investigate how the underlying oscillator dynamics govern the transmission of learning signals in oscillator Ising machines (OIMs). We show that the hidden-layer response can be decomposed into two distinct factors: the nudging force applied at the output and the susceptibility governing its transmission to the hidden layer. Using a bipartite OIM with interactions interpolating between phase-difference and phase-sum coupling, we investigate these factors while ensuring that all coupling forms realize the same Ising Hamiltonian at binary phase-locked states. We demonstrate that second-harmonic injection primarily influences the nudging force through phase binarization, whereas the coupling form controls the cross-layer susceptibility through the mixed curvature of the energy landscape. At equal mixing, the mixed curvature vanishes on the binary manifold, strongly suppressing hidden-layer learning signals. Numerical simulations validate these mechanisms and reveal their consequences for classification performance. Our findings establish that realizing a desired energy landscape alone is insufficient to ensure effective physical learning; the system must also support the transmission of perturbations required for learning.

</details>


### [74] [Beyond Ground States: Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians for p-Bit Invertible Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14744)
*Tian Feng,Xutang Fan,Yiyun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文将 p-bit 可逆逻辑的哈密顿量设计视为逆统计力学问题，学习在合法赋值上接近均匀的 Gibbs 能量，并在有限时间采样下优于传统手工编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 p-bit 构造关注基态成员与较大最小能隙，但有限时间采样器会经过激发态，因此仅最大化能隙未必带来更快的可验证求解。

Method: 在能隙下限约束下，用最高四阶相互作用的固定池做约束最大似然拟合，配合剪枝与重拟合，使每个 clamp case 的合法态 Gibbs 概率趋近均匀；所有能量在每个 case 上精确审计，并用九种调优采样器重启后达到验证解的时间评分。

Result: 在欠定模式下，学习乘法器从 4x4 到 7x7 仅需手工编码 0.55、0.44、0.52、0.46 的 updates；学习加法器在 8 bit 反向模式为 0.71 updates，24/32 bit 约四分之一，32 bit 工作量为 0.55。5x5 等条件下，似然拟合相对能隙最大化拟合仅需 0.55 updates 和 0.58 work，并优于手工编码。FPGA 上整数化后与软件 bit-exact，学习乘法器 4x4 周期为手工的 0.41，7x7 为 0.057，LUT 最多为其 2.4 倍。

Conclusion: 该方法只需电路的合法赋值集合；在欠定模式中可显著减少采样更新与工作量，但在确定模式下手工编码工作量更少，且加法器从 12 bit 起手工编码 updates 更少。

Abstract: Probabilistic-bit (p-bit) invertible logic encodes a Boolean relation as an energy whose ground states are its legal assignments, computing in any direction by sampling. Traditional constructions target ground-state membership and a wide minimum energy gap, yet finite-time samplers traverse excited states. We cast Hamiltonian design as inverse statistical mechanics: fit an energy with interactions up to order four whose Gibbs law approaches uniformity on each clamp case's legal states, under a gap floor keeping every legal state below every illegal one. Constrained maximum likelihood with pruning and refitting fits terms from a fixed pool, and every delivered energy is audited exactly on all cases. Each energy is scored by time to a verified solution under restarts with its best of nine tuned samplers. In underdetermined modes, learned multipliers factor in 0.55, 0.44, 0.52 and 0.46 [0.38, 0.56] of the hand-written encodings' updates from 4x4 to 7x7, faster within every family below 7x7 at no resolved work penalty; learned adders solve the reverse mode in 0.71 of the updates at 8 bits and about a quarter at 24 and 32 bits, at 0.55 of the work at 32 bits. At 5x5 under equal pool, budget and floor, a gap-maximizing fit reaches 0.79 [0.73, 0.88] in updates and 1.26 in work against the hand-written encoding, while likelihood fitting takes 0.55 [0.46, 0.58] and 0.58 [0.48, 0.61] of the gap fit's updates and work. Rounding coefficients to the narrowest exact integer words tested, we run both energies at each pair's common word on one FPGA color-sweep SSA core, bit-exact with software: learned multipliers take 0.41 of the hand-written encoding's cycles at 4x4 and 0.057 [0.045, 0.068] at 7x7, at up to 2.4 times its lookup tables. The method requires only a circuit's legal assignments; in determined modes, hand-written encodings take less work, and from 12 bits on the adder fewer updates.

</details>


### [75] [Zeroth persistent homology of ground-state correlations at clean and disordered quantum transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14797)
*Francisco Belch\'i,Nahuel Statuto*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文利用交错基态关联的零维持久同调构建无阈值伪临界判据，并与传统响应比较，揭示其在干净临界、混合阶转变及无序重入区的能力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统响应量通常依赖阈值，且难以统一刻画无序诱导的无限无序不动点、重入有序及强无序重整化群团簇结构，因此需要检验持久同调能否提供稳定、无阈值的层次化诊断。

Method: 结合精确自由费米子解与密度矩阵重整化群，计算交错基态关联的零维持久同调；用条形码记录单链接层次合并尺度，以持久景观速度最大值定义无阈值伪临界点，并用标记合并树与强无序重整化群团簇比较。

Result: 持久景观速度最大值能以与传统响应相当的精度定位干净临界点和混合阶转变线；在重入区，全局速度误判进入点，仅将过滤限制在有序尺度可恢复进入点，且所测估计量均不能定位退出点；边界条件强制的畴壁抑制无序平均重入瓣；对单个无序实现，标记合并树可恢复多数强无序重整化群团簇，包括格点受限层次遗漏的非连续团簇，且无序越强一致性越高。

Conclusion: 零维持久同调提供了稳定、无阈值的关联层次，其信息源于完整关联矩阵；它能有效补充传统响应并捕捉强无序团簇结构，但对重入区的进入/退出定位仍存在明显限制。

Abstract: The antiferromagnetic Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields exhibits a continuous critical point, a mixed-order transition line and, with quenched disorder, an infinite-disorder fixed point and reentrant order. Using exact free-fermion solutions and the density-matrix renormalization group, we examine what the zeroth persistent homology of staggered ground-state correlations adds to conventional responses. Its barcode records the merge scales of the single-linkage hierarchy, and the maximum of its persistence-landscape velocity defines a threshold-free pseudocritical point. This point locates the clean critical point and the mixed-order line with an accuracy comparable to conventional responses. In the reentrant regime, the global velocity misplaces the entry, which is recovered only by restricting the filtration to the ordering scale, and none of the tested estimators locates the exit. A domain wall forced by the boundary conditions suppresses the disorder-averaged reentrant lobe. For individual disordered realizations, the labelled merge tree recovers most strong-disorder renormalization-group clusters, including noncontiguous ones missed by lattice-restricted hierarchies, with increasing agreement at stronger disorder. This information resides in the full correlation matrix, while zeroth homology provides a stable, threshold-free hierarchy.

</details>


### [76] [Direct imaging of Su-Schrieffer-Heeger edge modes in a disordered suspended mechanical oscillator chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14826)
*Defeng Lu,Rohan Modi,Nicolas Delnour,Michael Hilke*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文报道了一个可重构的宏观机械SSH模型，并实验观测到由拓扑终止支持的边界局域中隙模式及质量无序效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典机械系统中实现并直接成像SSH拓扑模型，以研究拓扑简正模式和 disorder 对边界态的影响。

Method: 构建由交替质量块和相同压缩弹簧耦合的悬浮链；以相邻质量块纵向相对位移为键坐标，通过机械动力学矩阵的平移与重标度将N块链映射为(N-1)格点SSH链；利用视频追踪重建键分辨谱和模式分布，并与理想有限SSH链预测比较。

Result: 拓扑终止支持局域于边界的带隙内模式，并呈现有效偶数长度SSH链预期的交替子晶格结构；质量变化会劈裂边缘模频率，并产生相对于无无序预测的非对称模式分布。

Conclusion: 该可直接成像平台可用于研究经典机械系统中的拓扑简正模式及无序效应。

Abstract: We report a reconfigurable macroscopic mechanical realization of the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model: a suspended chain of alternating masses coupled by identical compression springs. The relevant SSH degree of freedom is the relative longitudinal displacement between neighboring masses. In this bond-coordinate basis, applying a constant shift and rescaling of the mechanical dynamical matrix maps a chain of $N$ blocks onto an (N-1)-site SSH chain. Using video tracking, we reconstruct bond-resolved spectra and mode profiles and compare them with predictions for an ideal finite SSH chain. The topological termination supports mid-gap modes localized near the boundaries, with the alternating-sublattice structure expected for an effective even-length SSH chain. Mass variations split the edge-mode frequencies and produce asymmetric mode profiles relative to the non-disordered predictions. This directly imageable platform enables studies of topological normal modes and disorder effects in a classical mechanical system.

</details>


### [77] [Physical interpretation of spin-glass models with correlated disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16765)
*Hidetoshi Nishimori*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文讨论具有特定相关键无序的Ising自旋玻璃模型，通过规范变换把铁磁序与Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃相联系起来，并用于研究温度混沌和磁场混沌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用铁磁序与自旋玻璃相之间的对应关系，为理解自旋玻璃相中的温度混沌、磁场混沌等非平凡性质提供新视角。

Method: 从对称±J Edwards-Anderson模型中抽取一个平衡自旋构型作为参考构型，再作规范变换使该参考构型变为全上态，从而构造相关无序键；该构造类似Mattis模型但角色反转。推广模型中引入参数γ，规范等价类的概率仅由γ决定，而抽取参考构型时的逆温度只决定类内分布；磁化定义为与参考构型的重叠。

Result: 相关无序模型的阻挫统计与对称±J模型完全相同；其铁磁相本质上是“伪装”的自旋玻璃相。通过该对应，对其铁磁相的研究给出了Edwards-Anderson模型自旋玻璃相中温度混沌和磁场混沌的意外结果。

Conclusion: 相关无序模型把铁磁相与自旋玻璃相通过规范变换联系起来，为自旋玻璃问题提供了新视角，并成为产生高度非平凡结果的沃土。

Abstract: Ising spin-glass models with a specific type of correlated bond disorder have recently been used to relate properties of spin-glass phases, such as temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos, to ferromagnetic order. This correlated disorder has a simple physical interpretation, which underlies the sampling procedure introduced by Oshima, Arai, and Hukushima for numerical simulations: the bond configuration is obtained from the Edwards-Anderson model with the symmetric $\pm J$ distribution by drawing one of its equilibrium spin configurations and writing the model in the gauge in which this reference configuration becomes the all-up state. The construction is analogous to the Mattis model, with the roles of the ferromagnet and the spin glass reversed: whereas the Mattis model is a ferromagnet in disguise, the ferromagnetic phase of the correlated model is essentially a spin-glass phase in disguise. The frustration statistics of the correlated-disorder model are exactly those of the symmetric $\pm J$ model, and, in a generalization with an additional parameter $\gamma$, the probabilities of the gauge equivalence classes are determined by $\gamma$ alone, whereas the inverse temperature at which the reference configuration is drawn determines only the distribution within each class. The magnetization of this model is the overlap with the reference configuration. Through this correspondence, studies of its ferromagnetic phase have led to unexpected results on temperature chaos and magnetic-field chaos in the spin-glass phase of the Edwards-Anderson model. Models with correlated disorder have thus provided a new perspective on spin glasses and have proved fertile ground for highly nontrivial results on the spin-glass problem.

</details>


### [78] [Low-temperature vibrational anomalies and thermal transport in polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16848)
*V. Sokolenko,A. I. Krivchikov,A. Je\.zowski,V. Boiko,O. A. Korolyuk,D. Szewczyk*

Method: 对芘、并四苯、苝、并五苯、蒄和碗烯六种多环芳烃晶体开展低温热容、低频拉曼光谱和热导率测量，并进行系列比较。

Result: 对具有清晰极大值的化合物，热容过量的上升侧符合无形状参数的raised-cosine标度函数，而衰减侧依赖化合物；可分辨的晶格拉曼带数目和宽度随系列变化，但受谱背景和分辨率限制；热导率覆盖从晶体型到玻璃型行为；五种平面化合物中分辨出激活贡献，而碗烯在当前测量灵敏度下无需该贡献。

Conclusion: 玻璃样热学和振动特征并非非晶材料所独有，而与分子结构和晶体堆积差异有关；但这些结果本身不能将堆积方式孤立为唯一因果变量。

Abstract: The boson peak is an excess of low-frequency vibrational states over the Debye prediction and is commonly associated with structural disorder. Similar heat-capacity anomalies also occur in ordered molecular crystals, but their dependence on molecular structure and their relation to heat transport remain incompletely understood. Here we compare six polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) crystals -- pyrene, tetracene, perylene, pentacene, coronene and corannulene -- using low-temperature heat capacity, low-frequency Raman spectroscopy and thermal conductivity measurements. For compounds with well-resolved maxima, the rising side of the heat-capacity excess is consistent with a raised-cosine scaling function with no adjustable shape parameter, whereas the decay is compound-dependent. The number and widths of resolved lattice-mode Raman bands vary across the series, although their assignment is constrained by spectral background and resolution. Thermal conductivity ranges from crystal-like to glass-like behaviour. An activated contribution is resolved in the five planar compounds but is not required to describe the corannulene data within the sensitivity of the present measurements. These results show that glass-like thermal and vibrational signatures are not unique to amorphous materials and are associated with differences in molecular structure and crystal packing; they do not, by themselves, isolate packing as the sole causal variable.

</details>


### [79] [Cup and cap synchronization: higher-order node dynamics from topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16979)
*Runyue Wang,Yuanzhao Zhang,Ginestra Bianconi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出 Cup and Cap Synchronization 统一框架，结合代数拓扑与非线性动力学，揭示高阶节点同步的临界行为以及四体相互作用对同步转变的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高阶节点同步模型较为分散，缺乏能够涵盖既有模型并揭示三角形以上高阶相互作用作用的统一理论框架。

Method: 结合代数拓扑与非线性动力学，构建 Cup and Cap Synchronization 框架，将底层高阶网络的拓扑组织自然纳入高阶节点动力学，并与两类高阶 Kuramoto 类模型进行比较。

Result: 该框架可再现两类现有模型的临界普适类，并展现此前模型未捕捉的现象；四体相互作用可根本改变同步转变的临界性质，产生仅含边和三角形相互作用时不可见的集体现象。

Conclusion: 需要超越常见三体项；Cup and Cap Synchronization 为理解高阶网络集体动力学提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: Higher-order node-based synchronization provides distinct mechanisms for discontinuous synchronization transitions. However, a comprehensive theoretical framework capable of encompassing existing models and revealing the role of higher-order interactions beyond triangles is still missing. Here, combining algebraic topology and nonlinear dynamics, we introduce Cup and Cap Synchronization, a unifying theoretical framework for higher-order node-based dynamics that naturally incorporates the topological organization of the underlying higher-order network. We show that Cup and Cap Synchronization can display critical behavior belonging to the universality classes of two existing higher-order Kuramoto-like models, whose distinct critical behaviors were thought to originate from different mechanisms. Remarkably, we demonstrate that Cup and Cap Synchronization displays additional phenomenology not captured by the previously proposed models of higher-order node-based synchronization. Most relevantly, the inclusion of four-body interactions can fundamentally alter the critical properties of the synchronization transition, giving rise to collective phenomena that cannot be observed in models including only interactions mediated by edges and triangles. These findings reveal the importance of going beyond the commonly studied three-body terms and establish Cup and Cap Synchronization as a unifying theoretical framework for understanding collective dynamics in higher-order networks.

</details>


### [80] [Statistics of Fock-space correlation lengths in the many-body localised phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17306)
*Bibek Saha,David E. Logan,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文针对多体局域相中Fock空间本征态振幅的强非均匀性，建立基于两点Fock空间关联函数的定量微观理论，解释典型关联与平均关联的定性差异，并推导多分形奇异谱及量子多分形的反常对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域相中长程多体共振导致Fock空间本征态振幅高度非均匀；需要定量理解典型与平均关联行为差异的微观起源及其与Fock空间几何的关系。

Method: 构建两点Fock空间关联函数理论；对无序倾斜场Ising链进行精确对角化；分析Fock空间关联长度分布、稀有共振节点及高维超立方Fock空间图几何；并推导本征态奇异谱。

Result: 精确对角化显示：典型关联严格指数衰减，而平均关联呈亚指数衰减并随系统尺寸系统性变化，源于稀有共振节点；其背后是Fock空间关联长度分布，方差随系统尺寸倒数标度；该分布与高维Fock空间几何共同导致典型与平均行为的定性差异。

Conclusion: 该理论将MBL本征态Fock空间非均匀性、典型/平均关联差异及量子多分形反常对称性破缺统一为Fock空间几何与关联长度分布的内禀结果，并给出奇异谱的理论推导。

Abstract: The presence of long-ranged, many-body resonances in the many-body localised phase of disordered quantum systems is manifest in strong inhomogeneities of eigenstate amplitudes on the Fock space. We develop a quantitative, microscopic theory for this, centred on two-point Fock-space correlation functions. Exact diagonalisation of the disordered tilted-field Ising chain uncovers a stark contrast: typical correlations decay perfectly exponentially, whereas average correlations exhibit subexponential decay with systematic system-size dependence driven by rare resonant nodes. We show that this behaviour stems from an underlying distribution of Fock-space correlation lengths with a variance that scales inversely with system size. Crucially, the interplay of this distribution with the high-dimensional geometry of the hypercubic Fock-space graph, leads to the qualitative difference between the typical and average behaviours. The theory also allows us to derive the singularity spectrum of the many-body localised eigenstates, and to establish theoretically the anomalous symmetry violation of quantum multifractality as an inherent consequence of the Fock-space geometry.

</details>

### [81] [Quantum Hall Fabry-P\'erot Interferometry in Monolayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14633)
*Himadri Chakraborti,Charles L. Tschirhart,James Fang,Saisab Bhowmik,Chengrui Yu,Shujian Cao,William Caro,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jie Shan,Kin Fai Mak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在单层WSe2中首次实现栅极定义的量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪，观察到稳健的Aharonov-Bohm边缘干涉，并提取弱体-边耦合与边缘速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范德华材料中的多自旋/谷自由度使量子霍尔干涉解释复杂；单层WSe2因强自旋轨道耦合和自旋-谷锁定具有本征单分量朗道能级，适合干净、味分辨的量子霍尔干涉研究。

Method: 制备单层WSe2栅极定义的量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪，通过单器件连续调节腔尺寸，测量Aharonov-Bohm干涉，并结合温度与偏压依赖测量及静电估计分析体-边耦合和边缘速度。

Result: 观察到量子霍尔边缘通道的稳健AB干涉；AB区间占优与静电估计的弱体-边耦合Δ≈0.034一致；温度与偏压测量给出一致的边缘速度v≈(4.9±0.2)×10^4 m/s。

Conclusion: 单层WSe2被确立为干净、味分辨的量子霍尔干涉平台，为研究单分量分数量子霍尔态及任意子统计的干涉探测奠定基础。

Abstract: Electronic Fabry-P\'erot interferometers provide a powerful probe of coherent quantum Hall edge transport, yet the presence of multiple spin and valley degrees of freedom in existing van der Waals material platforms can complicate their interpretation. Monolayer WSe$_2$ offers a distinct opportunity because strong spin-orbit coupling and spin-valley locking lift these degeneracies, producing intrinsically single-component Landau levels. Here we realize the first gate-defined quantum Hall Fabry-P\'erot interferometer in monolayer WSe$_2$. By continuously tuning the cavity size in a single device, we observe robust Aharonov-Bohm (AB) interference of quantum Hall edge channels across the interferometer; the observed predominance in the AB regime is consistent with a weak bulk-edge coupling ($\Delta \approx 0.034$) estimated by electrostatics. Moreover, temperature and bias dependence measurements consistently yield an edge velocity $v \approx (4.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^4\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$. Our results establish monolayer WSe$_2$ as a clean, flavor-resolved platform for quantum Hall interferometry and provide a foundation for future studies of single-component fractional quantum Hall states and interferometric probes of anyonic statistics in transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [82] [A discrete realization of the boundary sine-Gordon model and the Schmid transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14637)
*Nicolas Paris,Oscar Bouverot-Dupuis,Christophe Mora*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文通过可精确映射到边界sine-Gordon模型的离散模型和高效团簇Monte Carlo，确认了Schmid转变的存在与性质，并验证了Schmid-Bulgadaev图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界sine-Gordon模型（等价于电阻分流约瑟夫森结）的相图及Schmid转变此前存在争议，需要无歧义的大尺度数值证据。

Method: 构造一个精确复现bsG模型性质的离散模型，利用团簇Monte Carlo算法高效模拟，遍历广泛参数空间并计算弱隧穿与强隧穿区间的完整普适交叉；同时建立模型自对偶性并分析临界固定点结构。

Result: 明确识别出表征Schmid转变的垂直临界固定点线，高精度恢复共形场论预测，并厘清Schmid转变与Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless转变的本质区别。

Conclusion: 结果确定性地确立了Schmid转变的存在及其性质，并支持Schmid-Bulgadaev场景的有效性。

Abstract: The phase diagram of the boundary sine-Gordon model (bsG), or equivalently of the resistively shunted Josephson junction, has been the subject of recent debate. We introduce a discrete model that reproduces exactly the properties of the bsG model and can be simulated extremely efficiently using cluster Monte Carlo algorithms. This enables us to explore a broad range of parameters and compute the complete universal crossover between the weak- and strong-tunneling regimes. We establish the self-duality of the model and unambiguously identify the vertical line of critical fixed points characteristic of the Schmid transition, recovering the conformal field theory predictions along this line with high accuracy. We further clarify the essential distinctions between the Schmid and Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions. Our results definitively establish the existence and nature of the Schmid transition and demonstrate the validity of the Schmid-Bulgadaev scenario.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索莫尔图案参数对磁自由度量子涨落的可调性，并利用真空涨落作为工作介质实现可控的磁-机械响应。

Method: 采用Bistritzer-MacDonald方法，以莫尔Ladder为例计算磁振子真空能和磁振子卡西米尔力随莫尔图案参数的变化，并据此设计原型发动机。

Result: 磁振子真空能与磁振子卡西米尔力成为莫尔图案参数的函数；可实现以真空涨落为工作介质的原型发动机，并产生新型磁-机械效应。

Conclusion: 该效应预计是莫尔结构的普适性质，与所涉及激发态的物理本质无关，且方法可直接推广到其他莫尔结构。

Abstract: We prove that the quantum fluctuations of the magnetic degrees of freedom of moir\'e structures can be tuned by adjusting the moir\'e lattice. Using the Bistritzer-MacDonald approach, we explore the consequences of this fact for the magnonic vacuum energy and the magnonic Casimir force, which become functions of the moir\'e pattern parameters. This allows a prototypical engine to be run with the vacuum fluctuations as a working agent and, in that way, to furnish a novel type of magneto-mechanical effect. Our calculations focus on the moir\'e Ladder as an example but can be generalized to any moir\'e structure in a straightforward fashion. We anticipate that our results belong to a generic property of moir\'e structures, regardless of the physical nature of the excitations at hand.

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of weakly nonlinear electric and thermoelectric transport through a T-shaped double quantum dot coupled to normal and superconducting leads. Nonlinear screening is treated within the characteristic-potential formalism, in which an internal electrostatic potential accounts for nonequilibrium charge redistribution on the lead-coupled central dot. The side-coupled dot induces the Fano-Andreev effect, which, together with nonlinear screening, significantly modifies the transport properties. Under voltage bias, the screening-induced level renormalization produces a pronounced bias asymmetry in the Andreev differential conductance. In response to a pure thermal bias, increasing the interdot coupling weakens the level renormalization and suppresses the quasiparticle thermoelectric response, leading to reduced thermovoltage and nonlinear Seebeck coefficient. When voltage and thermal biases are applied simultaneously, a finite subgap cross thermoelectric response emerges beyond the linear regime. The sign of this response is governed by the competition between the energy-dependent transport contribution, modified by the Fano-Andreev effect, and the nonlinear screening contribution, thereby determining whether a thermal bias enhances or suppresses the low-bias Andreev current.

</details>


### [85] [Momentum-Space Mismatch Enables Non-Hermitian Valley Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15036)

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出利用能带接触节点与耗散库之间的动量空间失配，诱导节点选择性非厄米趋肤效应，从而实现近100%可调且鲁棒的谷极化输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谷过滤器多依赖特定材料或晶格几何，缺乏普适的微观机制；作者寻找一种共同要素来实现谷选择性输运。

Method: 将系统的能带接触节点与耗散库耦合，使动量空间失配编码于推迟自能中，产生节点依赖的耗散和准粒子寿命差异；有效非厄米哈密顿量在两个节点具有相反的点隙谱绕数，形成节点分辨的非厄米趋肤效应和强不对称耗散。

Result: 在一个一维二聚体链以及具有真实狄拉克谷的二维π通量矩形和蜂窝晶格中，输运计算均得到近100%的谷极化；该效应可栅极调控，并对无序和边缘缺陷保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 动量空间失配提供了一条实现非厄米谷过滤的通用路径，可将节点选择性非厄米趋肤效应转化为高效谷极化电流。

Abstract: Valley-selective transport is central to valleytronics, but existing valley filters are largely tailored to specific materials or lattice geometries, motivating the search for a common microscopic ingredient. Here we identify the momentum-space mismatch between the band-touching nodes of a system and its dissipative reservoir as a route to node-selective non-Hermitian skin effects (NHSE). When the two are coupled, the mismatch is encoded in the retarded self-energy, producing node-dependent dissipation and contrasting quasiparticle lifetimes. The resulting effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian exhibits opposite point-gap spectral winding at the two nodes, giving rise to a node-resolved NHSE with strongly asymmetric dissipation rates: one node decays rapidly into the reservoir while the other survives over the device, yielding a nearly valley-polarized outgoing current. We demonstrate this mechanism in a minimal one-dimensional dimer chain and in two-dimensional systems hosting genuine Dirac valleys, including $\pi$-flux rectangular and honeycomb lattices. In all cases, transport calculations yield nearly 100\% valley polarization, while remaining gate-tunable and robust against disorder and edge imperfections. Our results show that momentum-space mismatch provides a route to non-Hermitian valley filtering.

</details>

*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*
