<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为若所有共振线宽相同，损耗无法区分相反手性；作者希望说明非厄米损耗结合相干交换可筛选磁振子手性，并厘清该效应来自极点约束还是制备、本征矢或物理库约束。

Method: 构建被动共线模式网络，引入空间关联损耗与相干交换；用非厄米共线自旋-空间小群组织手性及复频率上的允许极点配对；并以等谱正常对照和第二种实现（在对角边缘分离共轭手性扇区）进行验证。

Result: 初始无极化混合态达到26.9%极化，剩余布居29.9%，而等谱正常对照无极化；联合自旋与晶体旋转可反转信号并交换更强损耗下的例外轮廓；第二实现虽总绕数为零，仍在相反对角边缘分离共轭手性扇区。

Conclusion: 损耗可在同线宽条件下选择磁振子手性；两个读出量可区分对极点的约束与对制备、本征矢及物理库的约束，为无源非厄米手性调控提供判据。

Abstract: Loss can select one magnon chirality even when all resonances have the same linewidth. We construct a passive collinear mode network in which spatially correlated loss and coherent exchange give opposite angular momenta different survival probabilities. An initially unpolarized mixture reaches 26.9\% polarization with 29.9\% of the population remaining; an isospectral normal control gives none. A combined spin and crystal rotation reverses the signal and exchanges the exceptional contours reached at stronger loss. We organize the allowed pole pairings by the action of a non-Hermitian collinear spin-space little group on chirality and complex frequency. A second realization separates conjugate chirality sectors at opposite diagonal edges despite zero total winding. The two readouts distinguish constraints on poles from constraints on preparations, eigenvectors, and physical reservoirs.

</details>


### [87] [Magnetically Compensated Spin-Polarized Edge States in Coplanar Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15134)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 边界对称性可从磁补偿、自旋简并层中释放自旋极化边缘态，并把磁补偿与边缘自旋织构及电学自旋读出联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁补偿层通常自旋简并且无净自旋极化，但边界能否释放自旋极化态、其对称性规则及可测电响应尚不明确。

Method: 对共面交换磁体建立对称性判据：允许的偶宇称边缘自旋空间等于允许的均匀磁化空间；用六个最大扇区分类边缘织构，并在同一交换模型的正交切面及FeSe自旋涡旋、NiI$_2$周期四螺旋Wannier模型中实现奇/偶扇区和半无限边界。

Result: 对称性保护的补偿使边缘选择垂直于磁矩平面的奇自旋织构；奇、偶扇区均可实现；半无限边界呈互补电响应：奇边缘为垂直自旋积累，偶边缘为面内自旋流响应。

Conclusion: 边界对称性将磁补偿与平衡态边缘自旋织构及电学自旋读出相连，为无净磁化磁体的自旋极化边缘态设计提供对称性判据。

Abstract: A boundary can release spin-polarized electronic states from a magnetically compensated, spin-degenerate layer. We establish the symmetry criterion governing this release in coplanar exchange magnets: the allowed even-parity edge-spin space equals the allowed uniform-magnetization space. Symmetry-protected compensation therefore selects an odd spin texture perpendicular to the moment plane. Six maximal sectors organize the possible edge textures. Orthogonal cuts of one exchange model realize odd and even sectors, while FeSe spin-vortex and NiI$_2$ period-four spiral Wannier models provide material realizations. Their semi-infinite boundaries exhibit complementary electrical responses: perpendicular spin accumulation at the odd edge and in-plane spin-current response at the even edge. Boundary symmetry thus connects magnetic compensation to equilibrium spin textures and electrical spin readout.

</details>


### [88] [Quantum-Metric-Induced Distinct Optoelectronic Responses in Triple $M$ Valleys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15145)
*Yongliang Song,Shuxian Sun,Zhichao Zhou,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出利用量子度规诱导的光电响应区分并操控三重M谷，实现非线性谷霍尔效应与光激发谷极化霍尔输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谷电子学主要关注性质对比鲜明的K±谷，而M谷缺乏可靠操控方法，导致大量M谷材料未被利用。

Method: 通过紧束缚计算调节电场方向，选择任意一对M谷产生的相反非线性霍尔电流；利用线偏振光选择性激发单个M谷；并用第一性原理计算在反铁磁MoI3双层中验证相关响应。

Result: 三种M谷可通过量子度规诱导的光电响应区分；调节电场方向可实现非线性谷霍尔效应；线偏振光可驱动光激发谷极化霍尔输运；MoI3双层表现出显著且可输运测量的非线性谷霍尔电导。

Conclusion: 研究将谷电子学从传统二元K±谷拓展至三重M谷，为设计具有多量子态和高可调性的先进光电器件提供了途径。

Abstract: While $K_{\pm}$ valleys with contrasting optoelectronic properties have been the main focus of valleytronics, the lack of reliable manipulation methods for $M$ valleys leaves numerous $M$-valley materials untapped. Here, we discover that three flavors of $M$ valleys become distinguishable via quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses. The tight-binding calculations demonstrate that by tuning the electric-field orientation, opposite nonlinear Hall currents from any pair of $M$ valleys can be chosen to realize the nonlinear valley Hall effect. Moreover, linearly polarized light allows for preferential excitation of individual $M$ valleys, driving photoexcited valley-polarized Hall transport. Using first-principles calculations, we further validate the quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses in antiferromagnetic $\mathrm{MoI}_3$ bilayers, which exhibit a significant nonlinear valley Hall conductivity amenable to transport measurements. Our findings extend the scope of valleytronics from conventional binary $K_{\pm}$ valleys to triple $M$ valleys and pave the way for designing advanced optoelectronic devices with multiple quantum states of high tunability.

</details>


### [89] [Interface-Driven Remote-Surface States in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15192)
*Pierre A. Pantaleon,Alejandro Jimeno-Pozo,Francisco Guinea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 铑菱形多层石墨烯中，表面低能态可通过逐层机制将波函数权重传递至对侧表面，并解释远程表面调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文关注铑菱形多层石墨烯两个外表面上的低能态，以及这些态如何随层数增加扩展到对侧表面并导致实验观测到的远程表面调制。

Method: 作者提出逐层耦合机制，分析外层空位、圆形空洞和直锯齿边缘所束缚的亚晶格极化零模的波函数权重分布，并研究周期性莫尔界面下各动量谐波的独立作用。

Result: 发现波函数在连续层上扩展面积增大，归一化权重随体系尺寸向远程表面转移；同一关系支配空洞和锯齿边缘零模的扩展。在莫尔界面处，该机制分别作用于每个动量谐波，使低能带的层极化依赖布洛赫动量和调制周期。

Conclusion: 该工作为近期多层石墨烯实验中观察到的远程表面调制提供了内在的单粒子解释。

Abstract: Rhombohedral multilayer graphene hosts low-energy states on its two outer surfaces. We identify a layer-by-layer mechanism through which a low-energy state generated at one surface acquires weight on successive layers up to the opposite surface. For an outer-layer vacancy, the wavefunction spreads over a larger area on successive layers, shifting normalized weight toward the remote surface as the system size increases. The same relation governs the extension of sublattice-polarized zero modes bound to circular voids and straight zigzag terminations. At periodic moire interfaces, it acts separately on each momentum harmonic, making the layer polarization of the low-energy bands depend on Bloch momentum and modulation period. This provides an intrinsic single-particle explanation for the remote-surface modulations observed in recent multilayer graphene experiments.

</details>


### [90] [Chirality-Odd Readout of Coherence-Mediated Magnon Spin Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15214)
*Yanmeng Lei,Ji Zou,Sanchar Sharma,Tao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 结构手性可通过电学自旋注入-探测构型中的界面读出场符号反转来识别，而在注入端不改变自旋流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁体中结构手性普遍存在，但其对绝缘体自旋输运的影响仍缺乏系统理解，仅有少数实验初步观察。

Method: 理论预测铁磁与反铁磁螺旋中的手性响应，分析螺旋螺旋对称性对马格农自旋矩阵的限制，并考虑电子-马格农散射的注入与探测界面效应。

Result: 螺旋对称性使马格农自旋矩阵对角元为零，本征模线偏振且无自旋，输运由不同模式叠加介导；反转手性同时翻转沿螺旋轴的马格农自旋与注入量子相干，注入自旋流不变；但在探测界面电子-马格农散射振幅反转，使马格农积累读出的电压符号相反，与多铁BiFeO3中电场切换下第一谐波电压反转一致。

Conclusion: 结构手性是控制马格农相干性及其电学读出的内禀参数，并主要通过探测界面表现为可测的手性响应。

Abstract: Structural chirality is ubiquitous in magnets, yet its impact on spin transport in insulators remains largely unexplored, with only a few recent experiments offering the first glimpses. Here we predict the chiral response in the electric spin injection-detection configuration in both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic spirals. The screw symmetry of the spiral forbids any nonzero diagonal element of the magnon spin matrix: every eigenmode is linearly polarized and carries no spin, so spin transport is mediated entirely by superposition between distinct modes. Reversing chirality flips the magnon spin along the spiral axis and the injected quantum coherence in lockstep, leaving the associated injected spin current invariant. Spiral chirality acts instead at the detection interface, where electron--magnon scattering amplitude is reversed and causes the magnon accumulation to be read out as a voltage of opposite sign. This interfacial readout is consistent with reversed first-harmonic voltage under electric-field switching in multiferroic BiFeO$_3$, establishing structural chirality as an intrinsic control parameter for magnon coherence and its electrical readout.

</details>


### [91] [Steering Skyrmion Diffusion with Chiral Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15253)
*Palina Shaban,Ulrich Pototschnig,Gulnaz Rakhmanova,Mikhail Lemeshko,Ragheed Alhyder*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 手性分子层通过改变界面磁性相互作用，使磁斯格明子受到与手性相关的力，并显著改变其偏转与扩散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索吸附于磁性薄膜上的手性分子如何通过其手性与磁化耦合，进而调控磁斯格明子的热运动。

Method: 建立理论模型：一层取向倾斜的分子按其手性改变界面磁性相互作用；结合随机微磁模拟和集体坐标计算，分析斯格明子在边界、覆盖梯度和周期图案中的响应。

Result: 均匀分子层不改变斯格明子位置，而边界和覆盖梯度会产生力；靠近手性区域时斯格明子发生横向偏转，且偏转方向在对映异构体间反转。周期图案中，沿势变化方向扩散被抑制，垂直方向扩散增强；净扩散由陀螺响应与耗散响应的平衡决定，并在一维景观中精确展示。

Conclusion: 分子取向、空间图案和斯格明子性质共同决定其运动；手性分子层可作为调控磁斯格明子输运的直接手段，并提供手性相互作用的可观测特征。

Abstract: Chiral molecules adsorbed on a magnetic film interact with its magnetization and can thereby alter the thermal motion of magnetic skyrmions. Here we develop a theoretical model in which an aligned layer of tilted molecules modifies the interfacial magnetic interactions according to its handedness. Within this model a skyrmion responds only to spatial variations of the coupling, so a uniform layer leaves its position unaffected, whereas boundaries and coverage gradients exert forces on it. Near a chiral region, these forces deflect the skyrmion transversely in a direction that reverses between enantiomers, which provides a direct signature of the chiral interaction. In extended periodic patterns, the same forces reshape the long-time diffusion, suppressing it in the direction in which the potential varies and enhancing it in the perpendicular direction. The net effect on the mean diffusion is set by the balance between the gyrotropic and dissipative responses of the skyrmion, as we show exactly for one-dimensional landscapes. Stochastic micromagnetic simulations and collective-coordinate calculations confirm these predictions and show how molecular orientation, spatial patterning, and skyrmion properties together determine the motion.

</details>


### [92] [Directed Domain Wall Motion by Radio-Frequency Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15305)
*Lukas Fischer,Rouven Dreyer,Simon Arnold,Jae-Chun Jeon,Georg Woltersdorf,Stuart S. P. Parkin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究射频电流如何通过自旋轨道矩激发磁畴壁振荡，并借助对称性破缺将振荡整流为定向运动甚至磁翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直流自旋轨道矩驱动畴壁运动已较成熟，但射频电流与畴壁的耦合机制及其在对称周期驱动下能否产生定向运动仍不清楚。

Method: 利用时间分辨与静态磁光克尔显微镜，研究射频自旋轨道矩驱动的畴壁动力学，并引入横向磁场或初始电流脉冲打破对称性。

Result: 射频电流可在远低于铁磁共振频率处高效激发低频畴壁振荡；横向磁场使振荡整流为自推进，初始电流脉冲可实现持续畴壁运动；在均匀磁态中，脉冲生成边缘畴并经射频驱动扩展，可实现高效射频辅助磁翻转。

Conclusion: 研究建立了射频驱动畴壁振荡经对称性破缺整流为定向运动的通用框架，为高频畴壁控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Magnetic domain walls (DWs) are central to emerging spintronic technologies for memory, logic and neuromorphic computing, where their controlled displacement is a key functionality. While current-induced DW motion driven by spin-orbit torques is well established under direct currents, it remains unclear how radio-frequency (RF) currents couple to DWs and whether they can generate directed motion under a symmetric periodic drive. Here we show that RF currents drive DW oscillations, which can be rectified into directed motion through symmetry breaking. Using time-resolved and static magneto-optical Kerr microscopy, we demonstrate that RF spin-orbit torques efficiently excite low-frequency DW oscillations well below the ferromagnetic resonance frequency. Whereas these oscillations remain symmetric in the absence of perturbations, symmetry breaking by a transverse magnetic field produces self-propulsion, while symmetry breaking by an initial current pulse gives rise to sustained DW motion. We further show that in initially uniform magnetic states, RF-driven expansion of a pulse-generated edge domain ultimately results in efficient RF-assisted magnetic switching. Together, these results establish a general framework for understanding how RF-driven DW oscillations are rectified into directed motion, providing new opportunities for their high-frequency control.

</details>


### [93] [Matrix-aware 2D field-effect biosensing of cardiac troponin I using complementary ion-sensitive transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15347)
*Subhabrata Das,Bharti,Babulu Pradhan,Abhishek R Nath,Kashish Sapra,Jaydeep sharma,Vijayender Bhalla,Satyaprakash Sahoo,Manish Mathew,Md. Ehesan Ali,Kaushik Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究构建了MoS2 FET与石墨烯ISFET双传感器平台，用于复杂生物基质中肌钙蛋白I（Trop-I）的高灵敏检测，并利用机器学习辅助信号融合补偿血清离子强度导致的信号衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球主要死亡原因，亟需在床旁对心肌损伤进行快速、灵敏且可靠的检测；但FET生物传感器在高离子强度血清中信号显著衰减，限制实际应用。

Method: 将适配体功能化MoS2二维FET用于Trop-I高灵敏识别，并以石墨烯基ISFET作为辅助传感器表征样品离子环境；通过机器学习辅助整合两种传感器响应，补偿血清诱导的信号畸变，并恢复2D-FET转移特性中的关键电学指标（Ids_max、SS、gm）。

Result: MoS2 FET对Trop-I的检测限达0.001 ng mL-1；但血清高离子强度使场效应响应约衰减80%。ISFET辅助与机器学习融合可校正基质效应，并恢复受血清影响的关键电学参数。

Conclusion: 该双传感器架构结合分子识别、高分析灵敏度和基质感知的电信号校正，为复杂生物流体中更可靠的FET生物标志物检测提供路径，并有望通过引入靶标特异性适配体实现心脏生物标志物多重检测。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases remain a leading cause of mortality worldwide, highlighting the need for rapid, sensitive and reliable detection of cardiac injury at the point of care. Here, we develop a dual-sensor platform that combines an aptamer-functionalized molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) field-effect transistor (2D-FET) with a graphene-based ion-sensitive field-effect transistor (ISFET) for the detection of cardiac troponin I (Trop-I) in complex biological matrices. The atomically thin MoS2 channel enables highly sensitive detection of Trop-I, achieving a limit of detection of 0.001 ng mL-1. However, the high ionic strength of serum substantially attenuates the field-effect response, resulting in approximately 80% degradation of the sensor response. To address this matrix effect, the ISFET is employed as a secondary sensor to characterize the ionic environment of the sample. Machine-learning-assisted integration of the 2D-FET and ISFET responses is investigated as a strategy for compensating serum-induced signal distortion and recovering key electrical figures of merit extracted from the 2D-FET transfer characteristics, including maximum on-state current (Ids_max), subthreshold slope (SS) and transconductance (gm). This dual-sensor architecture combines molecular recognition, high analytical sensitivity and matrix-aware electrical signal correction, providing a potential pathway towards more reliable FET-based biomarker detection in complex biological fluids. The approach may further enable multiplexed detection of cardiac biomarkers through incorporation of target-specific aptamers, offering potential for broader assessment of cardiac stress and myocardial injury risk.

</details>


### [94] [Nonlocal multipolar spin accumulation in altermagnets beyond d-wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15438)
*J. W. Gonzalez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究自旋守恒的g波和i波交变磁体中，均匀纵向电导没有自旋奇分量时，如何通过点注入器的非局域自旋积累探测费米速度的高阶自旋奇矩，并用玻尔兹曼理论与量子输运模拟给出角谐波、径向衰减和幅度标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀纵向电导在自旋守恒的g波和i波交变磁体中缺少自旋奇分量，因此需要非局域、空间分辨的探测手段来揭示自旋奇速度矩及其对称性特征。

Method: 采用点注入器测量非局域自旋积累，结合玻尔兹曼理论预测首个非零自旋奇速度矩的阶数m=2,4,6对主导角谐波、渐近径向依赖r^{2-m}和固定距离与注入电流下幅度标度τ^{m-3}的影响；并用量子输运模拟和双接触条带中的无序实现进行验证。

Result: 对g波序，第四速度矩产生第四角谐波并具有r^{-2}渐近尾。量子输运模拟无需幅度拟合即支持有限几何玻尔兹曼理论预测的径向分布；在所分析窗口内，三种对称性的量子幅度比基于Born散射时间的玻尔兹曼预测高15-65%。在双接触条带中，用预设径向窗口在1800个独立无序实现中以6.8σ显著性检测到g波第四谐波，且其幅度随交变磁劈裂减小而降低。

Conclusion: 非局域自旋积累可有效探测交变磁体中的高阶自旋奇速度矩；g波和i波交变磁体的角谐波与径向标度可由速度矩阶数决定，量子输运结果与玻尔兹曼理论径向轮廓一致但幅度更大，并给出了g波第四谐波的显著实验可观测信号。

Abstract: In spin-conserving models of $g$- and $i$-wave altermagnets, the uniform longitudinal conductivity has no spin-odd component. A point injector reveals higher moments of the Fermi velocity through the nonlocal spin accumulation. Boltzmann theory predicts that the rank $m=2,4,6$ of the first nonzero spin-odd velocity moment sets the leading angular harmonic, its asymptotic radial dependence $r^{2-m}$, and its amplitude scaling $\tau^{m-3}$ at fixed distance and injected current. For $g$-wave order, the fourth moment produces a fourth angular harmonic with an asymptotic $r^{-2}$ tail. Quantum transport simulations support the radial profiles predicted by finite-geometry Boltzmann theory without an amplitude fit. Across the three symmetries, the quantum amplitudes exceed the Boltzmann predictions based on the Born scattering time by $15-65$\% in the windows analyzed. In a two-contact ribbon, we detect the $g$-wave fourth harmonic at $6.8\,\sigma$ in an independent set of 1800 disorder realizations using a preselected radial window. Its amplitude decreases when the altermagnetic splitting is reduced.

</details>


### [95] [Symmetry-Enabled Ferroelectric Valley Transduction in Rhombohedral Bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15494)
*Katsunori Wakabayashi,Souren Adhikary,Tomoaki Kameda,Kazuhito Tsukagoshi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在菱面体双层WSe2中，铁电极化通过K谷与Γ谷不同的层间杂化实现谷选择性能移，并给出可测的微分跨导签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电极化可与双层中的层自由度耦合，但要获得谷选择性响应，两个谷必须具有不同的层间特征；菱面体双层WSe2的三重旋转对称恰好满足：K谷层间隧穿被禁戒，Γ谷仍强杂化。

Method: 基于对称性分析与有限场拟合/计算，比较是否施加K谷禁戒规则；计算铁电层不对称引起的K边移动及K–Γ能谷景观；分析极化反转下的微分跨导g_d、补偿场零点以及谷依赖屏蔽修正。

Result: 不施加规则时K耦合拟合与零一致；K边移动达\polshiftdft meV，为单谷由层不对称可获得的最大值。极化反转使谷景观在位移场中镜像，两种堆垛在零场具有等大反号g_d；理想对称极限下每个堆垛在其补偿场处g_d为零。谷依赖屏蔽仅使该零点在读取点偏移\gdnulllow\%，器件不对称限制精度。

Conclusion: 将已知堆垛选择规则与铁电谷转导联系起来，给出定量谷移动和可直接输运验证的签名。

Abstract: Ferroelectric polarization couples to the layer degree of freedom in a bilayer, but a valley-selective energy response requires the valleys to carry different interlayer character. Rhombohedral bilayer WSe$_2$ meets that condition by symmetry: three-fold rotation forbids interlayer tunneling at the $K$ valley, while strong hybridization remains at $\Gamma$. The resulting contrast converts the band-effective ferroelectric layer asymmetry into a shift of the $K$ edge, and so displaces the $K$--$\Gamma$ landscape: a finite-field fit that does not impose the rule leaves the $K$ coupling consistent with zero, and the shift reaches \polshiftdft~meV, the most that asymmetry can give a single valley. Reversing the polarization mirrors the valley landscape in displacement field, so the two registries have equal and opposite differential transconductance $g_d$ at zero field. In the ideal symmetric limit a second parity holds about each registry's own compensation field and places a zero of $g_d$ there. The valley-dependent screening calculated here displaces that zero by only \gdnulllow\% at the read point, so device asymmetry limits the accuracy. The result links a known stacking selection rule to ferroelectric valley transduction, with a quantitative valley shift and a directly testable transport signature.

</details>


### [96] [Filling and Sealing Carbon Nanotubes by Local Anodic Oxidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15567)
*Ruiqing Zang,Jianan Xiao,Jacob Bair,Ian Wang,Chuyue Peng,Matthias Kuehne*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: AFM局域阳极氧化切割碳纳米管可意外实现一步“切割-填充-密封”，形成在干燥Ar中稳定数周的含水封闭碳管段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳纳米管是研究极端受限物质的重要纳米流体通道和纳米孔，但多数合成碳管端部封闭；研究内部流体/离子传输需开口与填充，研究封装物种还需密封以防逃逸。此前LAO切割是否导致开口、填充或密封尚不明确。

Method: 采用AFM电容耦合局域阳极氧化在预定位置切割单根碳纳米管，并用空间分辨显微拉曼光谱表征；结合干燥Ar气氛放置和高真空140°C退火检验封装物种稳定性与释放行为。

Result: LAO切割后碳管段意外呈填充且密封状态，在干燥Ar中可稳定数周；最内壁径向呼吸类模式蓝移和展宽与水的填充一致，表明AFM针尖-基底间凝聚的水弯月面是填充来源。140°C高真空退火可使部分而非全部管段释放封装物种，说明存在热可克服势垒而非端帽重新形成。

Conclusion: 该一步切割-填充-密封能力可用于直径分辨的纳米受限水及水溶液研究，并为真空分析技术提供仄升尺度样品递送途径。

Abstract: Carbon nanotubes (CNTs) are prime nanofluidic conduits and nanopores for studying matter under extreme confinement. Since most synthesis routes yield CNTs with closed ends, opening and filling strategies are required to access their interior for fluid and ion transport, whereas studies of the encapsulated species themselves may additionally require sealing to prevent their escape in dry or vacuum environments. Capacitively coupled local anodic oxidation (LAO) in an atomic force microscope (AFM) has been shown to cut individual CNTs at predefined positions, but whether cutting induces opening, filling, or sealing has remained unclear. Using spatially resolved micro-Raman spectroscopy, we find that LAO cutting surprisingly produces filled CNT segments that remain sealed under dry Ar atmosphere for weeks. The magnitudes of the blueshift and broadening of the innermost-wall radial breathing-like mode are consistent with water filling, pointing to the water meniscus condensed between the AFM tip and the substrate as the source of CNT filling during cutting. High-vacuum annealing at 140 {\deg}C releases the encapsulated species from some, but not all, segments, indicating a thermally surmountable barrier rather than re-formed end caps. This one-step cut-fill-seal capability offers a route to diameter-resolved studies of nanoconfined water and aqueous solutions, and to zeptoliter-scale sample delivery for vacuum-based analytical techniques.

</details>


### [97] [Single Spin Hall Conversion in the Hall Response of Magnetic-Insulator/Heavy-Metal Bilayers Identified by Odd Spin-Hall-Angle Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15571)
*Akashdeep Akashdeep,Ewiese Mohammad Ababneh,Botros Gerges Mikhail Fenhas,Duc Minh Tran,Mathias Kl\"aui,Steven S. -L. Zhang,Vivek Amin,Gerhard Jakob,Timo Kuschel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过区分不同磁场窗口分离YIG/Ta与YIG/Pt双层的Hall电压，发现存在奇于自旋Hall角的Hall响应，并用界面自旋流漂移扩散模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为磁绝缘体/重金属双层的Hall响应源于两次连续自旋Hall转换，因而应偶于自旋Hall角；作者希望厘清磁化驱动、磁场驱动及普通Hall效应的各自贡献。

Method: 利用不同磁场窗口将Hall电压分解为磁化驱动进动、磁场驱动进动和普通Hall效应三部分，并比较具有相反自旋Hall角的Ta与Pt重金属层；采用包含界面生成自旋流的漂移扩散模型进行描述。

Result: 磁场驱动分量在Ta与Pt之间反号，揭示出奇于自旋Hall角的Hall响应；磁化驱动分量除最薄重金属层外也在Ta与Pt间反号。模型表明磁场驱动分量主要由单次自旋Hall转换贡献，磁化驱动分量也与单次转换一致；若用有限虚自旋混合电导解释则需异常大的数值，因而该机制不太可能。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了对磁绝缘体/重金属双层Hall响应的更一般理解，表明其不能仅用两次连续自旋Hall转换导致的偶响应图像描述。

Abstract: The Hall response of magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers is usually ascribed to two successive spin Hall conversions and is therefore expected to be even in the spin Hall angle. Using distinct magnetic-field windows, we separate the Hall voltage of yttrium iron garnet bilayers with Ta and Pt of opposite spin Hall angle into contributions from magnetization- and magnetic-field-driven precession as well as from ordinary Hall effect. The field-driven component reverses sign between Ta and Pt, revealing a Hall response odd-in-spin-Hall-angle. The magnetization-driven component also has opposite signs for Ta and Pt, except for the thinnest heavy-metal layers. A drift-diffusion model with interface-generated spin currents describes this behavior: the field-driven component is predominantly carried by a single spin Hall conversion, and the magnetization-driven component is consistent with one as well. Other potential explanations, based on a description with a finite imaginary spin-mixing conductance rather than interface-generated spin currents, require unusually large values of this conductance, making this mechanism unlikely. Our findings advance the understanding of Hall responses in magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers toward a more general description.

</details>


### [98] [Non-Bloch Topology of Frustrated Non-Hermitian Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15863)
*Fan Yang,Jiong-Hao Wang,Hui Liu,Emil J. Bergholtz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文构建了一类具有严格平带的二维阻挫非厄米晶格模型，并用非布洛赫能带理论揭示平带趋肤效应、奇异性和非布洛赫拓扑边界对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫晶格中的严格平带与紧致局域态是研究平带拓扑的理想平台，但非厄米趋肤效应会显著改变能带结构，且二维以上非厄米平带拓扑仍缺乏理解。

Method: 构造可自然用非布洛赫能带理论描述的二维阻挫非厄米平带模型，以非厄米呼吸Kagome和棋盘格模型为例；分析平带由其他色散带诱导的基无关趋肤效应、能带交叉处本征矢不连续性及边界条件敏感性；通过紧致局域趋肤模的宏观求和构造边界趋肤模，并将其拓扑起源追溯到非布洛赫环面上的两个线性独立不可收缩环路态；提出基于零能角模双正交极化的不变量，并打破严格平带性实现近平坦非布洛赫陈带。

Result: 发现平带可获得由其他能带诱导的基无关趋肤效应；在呼吸Kagome模型中，该效应恰在平带进入色散带周期边界谱所张区域时出现。平带在能带交叉处本征矢具有不可移动不连续时变为奇异，且该奇异性依赖边界条件、不受平带性保护。体边对应表现为体内奇异能带接触与边界稳健平带趋肤模，后者可由紧致局域趋肤模宏观求和构造，其拓扑起源对应非布洛赫环面上的两个不可收缩环路态；双正交极化不变量可刻画该对应关系。

Conclusion: 工作建立了二维非厄米严格平带的非布洛赫理论框架，揭示了平带趋肤效应与奇异性的边界敏感性及非布洛赫拓扑起源，并推广到近平坦非布洛赫陈带，为研究非厄米平带拓扑和体边对应提供了通用方法。

Abstract: Frustrated lattices with exactly flat bands and compact localized states provide an ideal setting to uncover the topology of flat bands, leading to intriguing physics in two dimensions. Extending from Hermitian to non-Hermitian systems, however, band structures are drastically changed by the non-Hermitian skin effect, which is less well understood beyond one dimension. Here, we construct a broad class of two-dimensional frustrated non-Hermitian lattice models with exactly flat bands which can be naturally described in a non-Bloch band theory. Using non-Hermitian breathing Kagome and checkerboard models as illustrations, we reveal how flat bands acquire a basis-independent skin effect induced by the other bands; in the breathing Kagome model it appears exactly when the flat band enters the area spanned by the periodic-boundary spectra of the dispersive bands. A flat band becomes singular when its eigenvectors hold immovable discontinuities at band crossings. Such singularity in non-Hermitian systems is sensitive to boundary conditions and unprotected by band flatness. Non-Bloch topology underlying is uncovered in a bulk-boundary correspondence with the formation of a singular band touching in the bulk and a robust flat band skin mode on the boundary. We present a geometric approach to construct this boundary skin mode from a macroscopic sum of compact localized skin modes and trace its topological origin to two linearly independent noncontractible loop states on a non-Bloch torus. To characterize the correspondence, an invariant is proposed based on biorthogonal polarization of the zero-energy corner mode. Breaking exact flatness, we further realize nearly flat non-Bloch Chern bands.

</details>


### [99] [Emergence of Phonon Hydrodynamics as a Non-Hermitian Symmetry-Breaking Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16161)
*Xin Qian,Guanda Quan,Ning Wang,Yaoming Chu,Run Hu,Te-Huan Liu,Xiangfan Xu,Jianming Cai,Ronggui Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文将声子流体动力学的起始与边界表述为非厄米动力学转变，并基于Peierls-Boltzmann方程给出可预测的定量判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子流体动力学可实现超越经典扩散极限的集体热流，但如何定量判定其出现条件与边界仍是凝聚态物理中的长期难题。

Method: 从Peierls-Boltzmann输运方程出发，推导温度场与声子漂移场耦合动力学的有效薛定谔方程，将微观声子散射性质转化为波状热输运的预测边界；并用从头算声子性质作为输入。

Result: 一对奇异点定量定义流体动力学窗口的上下边界，给出优于Guyer-Krumhansl定性散射率不等式的临界设计准则；解析判据表明仅当有效热扩散率超过声子动量扩散率时才能存在有限流体动力学窗口；理论准确复现实验观测的温变和长度依赖超快第二声动力学。

Conclusion: 建立了基于对称性的集体能量输运理解框架，并为识别支持低维材料和功能器件中波状热输运的材料、温度和特征长度尺度提供定量设计原则。

Abstract: Phonon hydrodynamics enables collective heat flow beyond the classical diffusive limit and offers a promising route to controlling thermal transport in low-dimensional materials and nanoscale devices. However, quantitative criteria for determining when this hydrodynamic state emerges and where its boundaries lie remain a longstanding challenge in condensed matter physics. Here, we show that the onset of phonon hydrodynamics can be formulated as a non-Hermitian dynamical transition that converts microscopic phonon scattering properties into predictive boundaries for wavelike heat transport. Starting from the Peierls-Boltzmann transport equation, we derive an effective Schrodinger equation for the coupled dynamics of temperature and phonon drift fields, allowing the hydrodynamic regime to be identified directly from material transport properties. Within this framework, a pair of exceptional points quantitatively define the lower and upper boundaries of the hydrodynamic window, providing a critical design criterion beyond the qualitative scattering-rate inequalities proposed by Guyer and Krumhansl six decades ago. Furthermore, we derive an analytical criterion for the existence of hydrodynamic transport, showing that a finite hydrodynamic window can be supported only when the effective thermal diffusivity exceeds the phononic momentum diffusivity. Using ab initio phonon properties as inputs, our theory accurately captures the experimentally observed temperature- and length-dependent ultrafast second-sound dynamics. These results establish a symmetry-based framework for understanding collective energy transport and provide quantitative design principles for identifying the materials, temperatures, and characteristic length scales that support wavelike heat transport in low-dimensional materials and functional devices.

</details>


### [100] [Controlled Growth and Disorder Mitigation of GaAs1-xSbx/InAs Core-Shell Quantum Wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16203)
*Ran Zhuo,Huading Song,Yanlin Wu,Wenlong Yang,Haiyan Shi,Xiyu Hou,Sijun Chen,Zhichuan Wang,Donghai Li,Jinjin Bai,Runan Shang,Hao Zhang,Dong Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过将Sb引入GaAs1-xSbx/InAs核壳纳米线以降低晶格失配，显著改善晶体质量、迁移率和栅极调控能力，并观察到Aharonov-Bohm-like振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GaAs-InAs核壳纳米线存在较大晶格失配，导致缺陷和器件性能受限；作者希望通过GaAs1-xSbx合金核引入Sb来减小核壳晶格失配。

Method: 生长GaAs1-xSbx/InAs核壳纳米线并研究其输运性质；将Sb组分x从0.15增至0.35，表征InAs壳层中的堆垛层错等晶体缺陷密度、场效应迁移率、栅压夹断行为，并进行磁电导测量。

Result: x从0.15增至0.35时，InAs壳层中的堆垛层错等晶体缺陷密度减半；场效应迁移率由3850 cm2/Vs提高到4800 cm2/Vs；纳米线更易被栅压夹断，电场调控增强；磁电导呈现Aharonov-Bohm-like振荡，表明InAs壳层为相位相干的管状导电通道。

Conclusion: 晶格失配是核壳纳米线中的关键无序来源；通过Sb合金化降低失配可为低无序核壳量子器件提供实用途径。

Abstract: We report the growth and transport properties of GaAs1-xSbx/InAs core-shell nanowires. Compared with the well-studied GaAs-InAs system, incorporating Sb substantially reduces the lattice mismatch between the core and shell. Accordingly, both the nanowire crystal quality and device performance improved significantly. Increasing x from 0.15 to 0.35, the density of crystalline defects, such as stacking faults in the InAs shell, decreases by half. Moreover, the field-effect mobility increases from 3850 cm2/Vs to 4800 cm2/Vs, consistent with the reduction of the lattice mismatch. The incorporation of Sb makes the nanowire easier to pinch off via the gate voltage, enhancing the electric field tunability over the nanowire. Magneto-conductance measurements reveal Aharonov-Bohm-like oscillations, suggesting a phase-coherent tubular InAs shell. Our results identify lattice mismatch as a critical disorder source in core-shell nanowires and provide a practical route toward low-disorder core-shell quantum devices.

</details>


### [101] [High-Quality InSb Nanowires Grown by Molecular-Beam Epitaxy via Precise Catalyst Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16234)
*Wenlong Yang,Jiaye Xu,Shuai Yang,Wenyu Song,Haiyan Shi,Donghai Li,Xiyu Hou,Sijun Chen,Ke He,Hao Zhang,Dong Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文报道了无外来催化剂的自催化MBE生长InSb纳米线，并证明其具有高迁移率和相干量子输运能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InSb纳米线是拓扑量子计算的重要候选材料，但现有器件多用MOCVD生长，难以实现超导体原位生长；MBE生长又受参数窗口窄、重复性差及外来催化剂引入深能级杂质限制。

Method: 先生长GaAs纳米线茎，再原位将Ga催化剂液滴转化为In液滴，通过顶部In液滴在GaAs茎上实现InSb轴向异质外延生长；低通量抑制径向生长。

Result: InSb纳米线长度可随生长时间从0.8微米线性调至2.6微米，直径近似不变；为纯闪锌矿结构，无孪晶和堆垛层错；电子迁移率约19000 cm² V⁻¹ s⁻¹；观察到准弹道输运、量子点和Kondo关联。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了衬底无关、自催化的高质量InSb纳米线可控制备路线，为高性能InSb电子与量子器件建立了材料平台。

Abstract: Semiconductor InSb nanowires are a major material candidate for topological quantum computation. The nanowires in current devices were mostly grown using metal organic chemical vapor deposition, incompatible with in situ growth of superconductor, a crucial requirement for low-disorder devices. Growth of InSb nanowires using molecular-beam epitaxy (MBE) can address this issue. So far, studies on MBE-grown InSb remain limited due to an extremely narrow parameter window for the growth and reproducibility. Moreover, the use of foreign catalysts can introduce deep-level impurities. Here, we report a self-catalyzed MBE growth of InSb nanowires without those foreign catalysts. We first grow GaAs nanowire stems, and then convert Ga catalyst droplets into In droplets in situ. Axial heteroepitaxial growth of InSb can be realized through the In droplets on top of GaAs stems. We further find that a low flux suppresses radial growth, enabling linear length tuning from 0.8 to 2.6 micrometer with growth time, while diameter stays nearly constant. These InSb nanowires have a pure zinc-blende structure free of twins, stacking faults. Transport measurement reveals an electron mobility near 19000 cm2 V-1s-1. Moreover, the observation of quasi-ballistic transport, quantum dots, and Kondo correlations demonstrates the coherent quantum transport capability of the MBE-grown InSb nanowires. Our work provides a substrate-independent, self-catalyzed route for the controllable synthesis of high-quality InSb nanowires, establishing a robust material platform for high-performance InSb-based electronic and quantum devices.

</details>


### [102] [Ferroelectric control of exciton photoluminescence in rhombohedrally stacked bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16475)
*Wenlong Zhong,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Xiaoxiang Xi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用近零扭转、菱方堆叠的人工WSe2双层，通过激子光致发光和共振拉曼实现了滑动铁电极化的可逆电学读取与切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动铁电可提供非易失、电可编程的光电功能，但莫尔重构和畴平均常掩盖激子发光的确定性调控，因此需要一种能直接解析铁电WSe2电子结构的光学读出方式。

Method: 构建近零扭转、菱方堆叠的WSe2双层，施加面外电场，测量激子光致发光（包括动量间接QK激子和动量直接KK层间激子）及共振拉曼散射随极化切换的滞后响应。

Result: QK激子发光呈现Stark斜率反转和能量突变，可分辨由两次能带重排和一次铁电切换分隔的四阶段过程；极化反转选择性开启与层内激子杂化的KK层间激子支，产生历史依赖发光；共振拉曼强度滞后并与极化相关层间电场对应。

Conclusion: 研究建立了基于激子的铁电WSe2电子结构定量表征方法，并展示了电可编程激子/光电功能的实现途径。

Abstract: Sliding ferroelectricity offers a non-volatile route to electrically programmable electronic and optical functionality, but deterministic control of excitonic photoluminescence is often obscured by moir\'{e} reconstruction and domain averaging. Here we study an artificially assembled WSe$_2$ bilayer with near-zero twist in rhombohedral stacking and observe reversible, hysteretic electrical switching through excitonic photoluminescence. Under an out-of-plane electric field, momentum-indirect QK exciton emission exhibits Stark-slope reversals and abrupt energy jumps, resolving a four-stage sequence separated by two band realignments and one ferroelectric switching event. Polarization reversal also selects the momentum-direct KK interlayer-exciton branch that hybridizes with an intralayer exciton, producing history-dependent photoluminescence. Ferroelectric modulation of resonant Raman scattering provides a complementary readout, with hysteretic intensities correlated with the polarization-dependent interlayer field. These results establish an exciton-based quantification of the electronic structure in ferroelectric WSe$_2$ and open a route to electrically programmable optoelectronic functionality.

</details>


### [103] [Micromagnetic modelling and simulation of inertial magnetization dynamics in ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16567)
*Massimiliano d'Aquino*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 综述铁磁纳米结构中惯性磁化动力学的理论建模与微磁模拟，重点讨论太赫兹章动、惯性自旋波以及保持约束和能量性质的数值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚皮秒磁化动力学可表现出超越传统Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert描述的惯性效应，并产生太赫兹章动；在理论预测和实验观测之后，理解并控制超快自旋动力学需要系统研究磁性惯性。

Method: 从惯性Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程的机械类比出发，引入空间非均匀微磁公式，讨论磁化长度守恒、角动量弛豫和广义自由能平衡；综述线性惯性自旋波，包括GHz进动与THz章动分支、惯性红移、谱线宽、交换/静磁相互作用、限域、曲率和扭转效应，以及大波数下的有限传播速度；并介绍频域和时域微磁模拟及保持连续动力学约束和能量性质的几何中点积分器。

Result: 阐明了惯性自旋波中GHz进动与THz章动分支共存、进动模惯性红移、谱线宽和有限自旋波传播速度等特征；数值方法可保持基本约束和能量性质，并用于超短章动自旋波激发传播和超快惯性磁化翻转。

Conclusion: 微磁建模将惯性Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert理论与实验可及的纳米尺度磁化动力学联系起来，为理解和控制超快自旋动力学提供了理论框架和仿真工具。

Abstract: Magnetization dynamics at sub-picosecond time scales can exhibit inertial effects beyond the conventional Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert description, giving rise to nutational motion at terahertz frequencies. Following its theoretical prediction and experimental observation in ferromagnets, magnetic inertia has attracted increasing interest for understanding and controlling ultrafast spin dynamics. In this topical review, we discuss the theoretical modelling and micromagnetic simulation of inertial magnetization dynamics in ferromagnetic nanostructures. Starting from the mechanical analogy underlying the inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation, we introduce its spatially inhomogeneous micromagnetic formulation and discuss its main properties, including magnetization-length conservation, angular-momentum relaxation, and the generalized free-energy balance. We then review the linear dynamics of inertial spin waves, highlighting the coexistence of GHz precessional and THz nutational branches, the inertial redshift of precessional modes, spectral linewidths, and the effects of exchange, magnetostatic interactions, confinement, curvature and torsion. Particular attention is devoted to the large-wavenumber regime, where magnetic inertia leads to a finite limiting spin-wave propagation velocity. Finally, we address frequency- and time-domain micromagnetic simulation techniques for spatially nonuniform inertial dynamics, including geometric midpoint integrators preserving the fundamental constraints and energy properties of the continuous dynamics. Applications to the excitation and propagation of ultrashort nutational spin waves and ultrafast inertial magnetization switching illustrate the role of micromagnetic modelling in connecting inertial Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert theory with experimentally accessible nanoscale magnetization dynamics.

</details>


### [104] [A superconducting quantum point contact in Ge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16705)
*Anna Tacchini,Inga Seidler,Isabell Grandt-Ionita,Thibault Thuegaz,Stephen W. Bedell,Fabrizio Nichele*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Ge/SiGe中实现栅压可调的超导量子点接触，并利用高度不对称SQUID对其少模超导输运进行相位敏感表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由少量弹道模式承载超电流的超导量子点接触，有助于理解介观弱链接中的相干输运，并建立正常态输运模式与超导响应之间的联系。

Method: 将Ge/SiGe量子点接触嵌入高度不对称超导SQUID中，通过测量电流-相位关系（CPR）和开关电流实现相位敏感研究，并在微波辐照下考察光子辅助输运。

Result: 观察到电导量子化与开关电流量子化直接对应；在第一电导平台起始附近实现有效透射的强栅压调控；微波下低透明度区出现光子辅助多重Andreev反射，高透明度少模区出现Shapiro replicas。

Conclusion: Ge/SiGe超导量子点接触可作为可调少模超导弱链接平台，将正常态弹道输运模式与超导电流响应联系起来，并支持微波调控的相位敏感少模超导输运研究。

Abstract: Hybrid superconducting-semiconducting circuits provide a versatile platform for investigating coherent transport in mesoscopic weak links. Superconducting quantum point contacts (SQPCs) are particularly interesting in this context, as their supercurrent is carried by a discrete number of ballistic transport modes. Here, we investigate a gate-tunable superconducting quantum point contact realized in Ge/SiGe. By incorporating the QPC into a highly asymmetric superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID), we gain access to its current-phase relation (CPR) and switching current, enabling phase-sensitive studies of few-mode superconducting transport. We observe a direct correspondence between conductance quantization and switching-current quantization, linking the normal-state transport modes to the superconducting response. In addition, we demonstrate strong gate tunability of the effective transmission near the onset of the first conductance plateau. Under microwave irradiation, the QPC exhibits photon-assisted multiple Andreev reflections at lower transparencies and Shapiro replicas in the high-transparency, few-mode regime.

</details>


### [105] [Interfacial Effects of Thermal Reorganization on Charge and Spin Transport in Organic Spin Valves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16852)
*Zihao Liu,Yunzhe Ke,Hajar Komekoz,Shuai Gu,Rico Ehrler,Chen Wang,Olav Hellwig,Frank Ortmann,Dietrich R. T. Zahn,Carsten Deibel,Michael Sommer,Shuaishuai Ding,Georgeta Salvan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究考察原位热退火对NDI基有机自旋阀完整器件电学与磁输运性能的影响，并比较直接蒸镀Co/Au与转移石墨烯/Co/Au两种顶电极；退火虽提升体相有序和有效垂直电导，却破坏可重复磁电阻响应，凸显界面主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机自旋阀的磁电阻响应高度依赖铁磁电极/有机半导体界面（spinterface），界面形貌、化学相互作用、能级对齐和自旋极化变化会导致器件性能重复性差，因此需要理解热退火对完整器件界面与自旋输运的影响。

Method: 制备完整的NDI基有机自旋阀，对比直接蒸镀Co/Au和转移石墨烯/Co/Au两种顶电极配置；比较as-cast与原位退火器件的电输运和磁输运性能，并结合伴随薄膜的结构表征及电子-only器件提取的有效垂直电导进行分析。

Result: 两种as-cast器件均表现出清晰磁电阻响应，转移电极器件的绝对响应更强；原位退火后两类器件电阻均显著增加，伴随薄膜结构有序度提升且电子-only器件有效垂直电导约增加36倍，但完整器件中未解析出可重复的磁电阻响应。

Conclusion: 单纯改善体相有序不足以保证自旋阀性能提升；退火诱导的界面接触、接触均匀性和局域电流分布变化可在完整器件响应中占主导，强调spinterface稳定性和界面工程对实现可靠、热稳定有机自旋阀操作的关键作用。

Abstract: Organic semiconductors are attractive for spintronic applications owing to their weak spin-orbit coupling, small hyperfine interactions, mechanical flexibility, and tunable electronic properties. Organic spin valves (OSVs) provide a representative platform for investigating spin-dependent transport. However, the magnetoresistance (MR) response is highly sensitive to the ferromagnetic electrode/organic semiconductor interface (spinterface). Variations in interfacial morphology, chemical interaction, energy-level alignment, and spin polarization can strongly affect spin injection, transport, and detection, making reproducible OSV performance difficult to achieve. Here, we investigate how in situ thermal annealing affects the electrical and magnetotransport properties of complete NDI-based OSVs. Two top-electrode configurations are compared: directly evaporated Co/Au and transferred Gr (graphene)/Co/Au. Both as-cast devices exhibit clear MR responses, with a stronger absolute response observed for the transferred-electrode device. After in situ annealing, the resistance of both device types increases substantially, and no reproducible MR response is resolved, despite the enhanced structural order observed in companion films and an approximately 36-fold increase in the effective vertical conductivity extracted from electron-only devices. This contrasting behavior indicates that improved bulk order alone is insufficient to ensure enhanced spin-valve performance. Instead, annealing-induced changes in interfacial contact, contact homogeneity, and local current distribution can become dominant in determining the complete-device response. These results emphasize the critical role of spinterface stability and interface engineering in achieving reliable and thermally robust OSV operation.

</details>


### [106] [Squeezed terahertz light from nonlinear diamagnetism in ultrastrongly coupled two-dimensional electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16924)
*Dasom Kim,Junichiro Kono,Stephen Hughes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 二维电子气太赫兹腔中的内禀能带非抛物性可把原本线性Hopfield描述的体系转变为量子Kerr振荡器，从而产生太赫兹光压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维电子气太赫兹腔是超强光-物质耦合的成熟平台，但其集体响应通常由具有精确经典对应的线性Hopfield模型描述，忽略了能带非抛物性带来的非线性量子效应。

Method: 作者采用非旋转波理论，并结合与超强耦合一致的输入-输出理论、从头算腔浴模型以及电子加热的自洽模型，分析能带非抛物性诱导的Kerr非线性及输出压缩。

Result: 预测现有GaAs样品在弱限制、品质因数约100的腔中可实现1.8–2.4 dB输出压缩；若适度提升迁移率或腔频率，压缩可达约9 dB。载流子加热通过材料品质因数限制性能，并给出最优腔品质因数与超强耦合强度成反比的设计规则。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，二维电子气太赫兹腔中的能带非抛物性可作为量子Kerr非线性资源用于太赫兹压缩，并提供受载流子加热约束的器件设计准则。

Abstract: Two-dimensional electron gases in terahertz cavities are among the most mature platforms for ultrastrong light--matter coupling, yet their collective response is captured by the linear Hopfield model, which has an exact classical correspondence. We show that the intrinsic band nonparabolicity of the electrons, recently revealed through nonlinear diamagnetism, turns this system into a quantum Kerr oscillator that squeezes terahertz light. Using a non-rotating-wave theory with ultrastrong-coupling-consistent input--output, an ab initio cavity bath, and a self-consistent model of electron heating, we predict output squeezing of 1.8--2.4\,dB with present GaAs samples in weakly confined cavities with quality factors of order $10^2$, and up to $\approx9$\,dB with modest improvements in mobility or cavity frequency. Carrier heating sets limits through a material figure of merit, which yields a simple design rule that the optimal cavity quality factor scales inversely with the ultrastrong-coupling strength.

</details>


### [107] [Achiral-Chiral Dissociation and Structural Zero of the Current in the Spinterface Model of CISS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16998)
*Seif Alwan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将 spinterface CISS 模型的量子非平衡格林函数方法推广到自旋分辨的引线展宽，研究自旋输运中手性/非手性分量的解离、非普适最优工作点以及精确结构零点的非共线变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）的输运理论需要处理自旋分辨耗散，并区分由界面磁矩驱动与Rashba自旋翻转驱动的自旋流分量；作者希望建立可描述二者解离及结构零点的输运平台。

Method: 在Alwan2026QSpinterface的量子非平衡格林函数框架中引入自旋分辨的引线展宽，将自旋流分解为非手性部分（界面磁矩驱动）和手性部分（Rashba自旋翻转驱动），并分析共线及非共线铁磁引线下的耦合、偏压与结构零点。

Result: 随引线自旋不对称增强，非手性分量线性增长，手性分量按几何平均抑制，二者解离且在不同耦合尺度达峰；耦合/偏压最优仅为参考构型工作点。共线引线下在 λ_R sin(φ/2)=t_mol 出现结构零点，手性信号跨链长、频率分辨与扭转角均以机器精度塌缩，源于秩一奇异键矩阵的零本征矢将电子囚禁于不自旋跳跃的自旋叠加暗态。非共线铁磁引线以受控方式破缺该零点：残余横向极化通常线性，源于边界单次相干自旋翻转；在调谐抵消角处两边界贡献相消干涉而变为二次，标度指数由1突跳到2。

Conclusion: spinterface模型构成具有双轴解离结构、非标度最优工作点以及精确结构零点的输运平台；其非共线变形呈现清晰的线性加二次层级。

Abstract: We study the transport sector of the spinterface model of chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) under spin-resolved dissipation, extending the quantum non-equilibrium Green's function formulation of Ref.~\cite{Alwan2026QSpinterface} to spin-resolved lead broadenings. The spin current decomposes into an achiral part, driven by the interfacial moment, and a chiral part, driven by the Rashba spin flip. Under increasing lead spin asymmetry, the achiral part grows linearly while the chiral part follows the geometric-mean suppression, so the two sectors dissociate; they also peak at different coupling scales along the lead broadening. The coupling and bias optima are operating points of the reference configuration, not universal scaling laws. A structural zero appears at $\lambda_R\sin(\phi/2)=t_{\mathrm{mol}}$ in the collinear-lead configuration, where the chiral signal collapses to machine precision universally across chain lengths, frequency resolutions, and twist angles; the zero originates from the rank-one singular bond matrix, whose null eigenvector traps an electron in a spin-superposition dark state that does not hop. With non-collinear ferromagnetic leads, the zero is broken in a controlled way: the residual is generically linear in the transverse polarization, arising from a single coherent spin flip at a boundary, and becomes quadratic at a tuned cancellation angle where two boundary contributions interfere destructively, with the scaling exponent jumping sharply from one to two. These results establish the spinterface model as a transport platform with a two-axis dissociation structure, non-scaling operating optima, and an exact structural zero whose non-collinear deformation is a clean linear-plus-quadratic hierarchy.

</details>


### [108] [Reflection Zeros Set the Scattering Invariant of Finite Majorana Wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17001)
*Alex Krasnok*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文澄清了有限马约拉纳器件中零能电子反射符号 Q 的物理含义：Q 计数无反射态，并可由此从零偏压电导读取拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳模式可能用于受拓扑保护的量子信息存储，但其输运特征是零偏压电导峰，普通束缚态也可产生类似峰，因此马约拉纳模式是否已被观测仍有争议；长线中零能电子反射符号 Q 可标记拓扑相，但有限器件中 Q 能证明什么尚不清楚。

Method: 通过分析零能电子反射符号 Q 与复能量平面下半平面中无反射态数目的关系，并将其与有限超导线的马约拉纳分裂、接触耦合及零温零偏压电导联系起来。

Result: 证明 Q 计数无反射态：当下半复能量平面中存在奇数个无反射态时 Q=-1；在有限线中这意味着两个马约拉纳模式的分裂小于它们与接触的耦合。对于等耦合接触，Q 的每次符号变化（包括体拓扑转变）都是例外点，此时两个无反射态合并且透射最大平坦。零温下，Q=-1 当且仅当接触处测得的零偏压电导超过 e^2/h。

Conclusion: 这些结果明确了输运实验能对马约拉纳器件证明什么，并给出了从电导读取拓扑不变量的方法。

Abstract: Majorana modes, zero-energy excitations of superconducting wires that are their own antiparticles, could store quantum information protected from local noise. In transport experiments, however, ordinary bound states mimic their signature, a conductance peak at zero voltage, and whether Majorana modes have been observed remains debated. A sign, $Q$, of the electron reflection at zero energy marks the topological phase of a long wire, but what $Q$ certifies in a finite device has been unclear. Here we show for the first time that $Q$ counts reflectionless states, waves that enter through one contact and leave entirely through the other: $Q=-1$ when an odd number of them lie in the lower half of the complex energy plane. In a finite wire this means that the two Majorana modes are split by less than their coupling to the contacts. With equal contacts, every sign change of $Q$, including the bulk topological transition, is an exceptional point at which two such states merge and transmission is maximally flat. At zero temperature, $Q=-1$ exactly when the zero-bias conductance measured at a contact exceeds $e^2/h$. These results show what transport can certify about a Majorana device and how to read the invariant from conductance.

</details>


### [109] [Acoustically driven motion of single molecules at hBN surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17112)
*Hande N. A\c{c}{\i}kg\"oz,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Peter G. Steeneken,Sabina Caneva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于表面声波（SAW）与六方氮化硼（hBN）的无接触驱动平台，可在hBN表面实现DNA分子的可调谐定向输运，并揭示SAW功率对输运偏置与轨迹线性的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单分子成像与传感已能观测界面生物分子动力学，但如何在表面主动输运、定位并控制分子运动仍具挑战，限制了单分子器件的发展；现有驱动方式常依赖分子的电学、介电或磁学性质。

Method: 构建SAW-hBN集成平台：hBN提供原子级平整表面以限制DNA并保持其面内迁移率，SAW提供无接触、主动且可引导的输运；通过轨迹分析和跳跃分析评估不同SAW功率下的DNA运动。

Result: 提高SAW功率会增加沿声流方向的位移偏置，使输运速度提高且轨迹更趋线性，从而实现hBN表面上DNA分子的可调谐定向输运。

Conclusion: SAW可调控受限DNA在hBN表面的定向输运，为纳流系统中的片上生物分子操控提供新途径，并对先进单分子测序及生物分子操控与分析技术具有重要意义。

Abstract: Developments in single-molecule imaging and sensing techniques have enabled direct observation of biomolecular dynamics at interfaces, which has important implications for the fields of membrane biophysics, nanofluidic sensing and transport. However, integrating active surface transport, localization and control over molecule motion remains challenging, limiting further progress in the realization of single-molecule devices. Here, we introduce an integrated surface acoustic wave (SAW)-hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) platform as a contactless actuation platform for directional transport of biomolecules over hBN surfaces. hBN provides an atomically flat surface on which DNA can be confined and still retain in-plane mobility, whereas SAW actuation enables active and guided transport without requiring specific electrical, dielectric or magnetic properties of the biomolecules. Trajectory- and jump-based analyses show that increasing the SAW power leads to higher displacement bias in the streaming direction, resulting in transport velocities along increasingly more linearized trajectories. We thus demonstrate that SAWs can induce tunable directional transport of DNA molecules confined on hBN surfaces, providing a new approach for the on-chip manipulation of biomolecules in nanofluidic systems. Our findings have important implications for the development of advanced single-molecule sequencing platforms and other technologies for biomolecular manipulation and analysis.

</details>


### [110] [Exact characterization of three-terminal fermion counting under one-body scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17122)
*Zhanning Wang,Yongtao Li,Rub\'en Seoane Souto,Jos\'e C. Abadillo-Uriel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 将两端非相互作用费米子的广义二项统计推广到三端/三通道散射，给出出射费米子统计的精确边界，并据此在冷原子散射实验中识别相互作用贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子散射是探测散射区及出射粒子量子动力学的基本手段；两端非相互作用费米子统计由Pauli原理给出广义二项分布，偏离该分布是相互作用的标志，因此需要推广到多端情形以识别相互作用。

Method: 研究耦合三个出射端（或通道）的散射区，推导非相互作用费米子出射统计的精确界限；在两费米子、三端情形下，得到涉及六个可测概率的四个不等式，用于检验观测统计能否由非相互作用散射体解释。

Result: 四个不等式提供了非相互作用散射体的可检验判据；将其应用于近期冷原子散射实验，识别出相互作用对出射粒子有显著贡献的参数区间。

Conclusion: 该工作为从介观导体到冷原子光阱等纳米尺度系统中识别费米子相互作用提供了判据和可能性。

Abstract: Quantum scattering is a fundamental probe of the scattering region and the quantum dynamics of outgoing particles. In two terminals, the statistics of non-interacting fermions is described by a generalized binomial distribution, that arises from the Pauli exclusion principle, while deviations are a signature of interactions. In this work, we extend this result to a scattering region coupled to three outgoing terminals (or channels), obtaining exact bounds for the statistics of the outgoing fermions. In particular, for two fermions and three terminals, four inequalities involving six measured probabilities determine whether a non-interacting scatterer can explain the observed statistics. Applying these inequalities to recent scattering experiments in cold atoms, we are able to identify a regime where interactions have a significant contribution on the outgoing particles. Therefore, our work opens up the possibility of identifying fermion interactions at the nanoscale in systems that range from mesoscopic conductors to optical traps in cold atoms.

</details>

### [111] [High-pressure behavior of the low-frequency mode of water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17096)
*Maria Rescigno,Guillaume Radtke,Umbertoluca Ranieri,Richard Gaal,Luigi Paolasini,Andrea Piovano,Ferdinando Formisano,Paola Giura,Livia Eleonora Bove*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 通过高压高分辨非弹性X射线散射研究液态水低频THz模，发现其接近临界阻尼且随压力显著硬化，不能用单一横声模解释；与冰VI对比表明该模是多支弱色散模式叠加的混合激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液态水约10 meV的低频模的微观起源长期存在争议，主要解释包括横声类激发、横光类模式或模-模耦合机制，需要实验厘清。

Method: 在室温、0.1–1 GPa压力范围内，对H2O和D2O进行能量分辨率1.6 meV的高分辨非弹性X射线散射（IXS），并与Ice VI的IXS测量和从头算模拟进行比较。

Result: 低频模在整个压力范围内保持强阻尼、接近临界阻尼；其频率随压力明显硬化，硬化程度远超横声模预期。Ice VI中同一能区存在一簇弱色散模式，提示液态水低频模具有多分支叠加的混合特征。

Conclusion: 液态水低频THz模并非单一明确激发，而是多个弱色散分支叠加形成的混合模式，这为理解压缩下水的异常THz动力学提供了统一的微观图像。

Abstract: The low-frequency (LF) mode observed in the THz spectrum of liquid water near 10 meV has been known for decades, yet its microscopic origin remains debated. Leading interpretations assign it to a transverse acoustic-like excitation, a transverse optic-like mode, or a mode-mode coupling mechanism. We address this question by probing the collective dynamics of H$_2$O and D$_2$O at room temperature and pressures from 0.1 to 1 GPa using high-resolution (1.6 meV) inelastic X-ray scattering (IXS). The LF mode remains heavily damped, close to critical damping, throughout the explored range, while its frequency hardens markedly with pressure, much more than expected for a transverse acoustic mode. Comparison with IXS measurements and \textit{ab initio} simulations of crystalline Ice VI reveals a cluster of weakly dispersive modes in the same energy range, suggesting that the low-frequency mode in liquid water reflects a mixed character arising from the superposition of several branches rather than a single well-defined excitation, providing a coherent microscopic picture of water's anomalous THz dynamics under compression.

</details>


### [112] [Carbon-made Lobachevsky surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14602)
*R. Gabbrielli,P. Pais,P. Castorina,T. P. C. Klaver,D. Legut,O. De Luca,R. G. Agostino,A. Iorio*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文以“平衡高斯—博内定理”统一回顾并扩展碳基常负高斯曲率曲面（Lobachevsky平面片段）的构造，连接拓扑、缺陷、弹性变形六边形与边界，并讨论其在模拟引力与海水淡化等方向的动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳制常负曲率曲面可在实验室实现Lobachevsky平面片段；其动机包括模拟引力中椭圆、Beltrami与双曲伪球对应Bañados–Teitelboim–Zanelli黑洞三种静态相，以及水淡化等应用。

Method: 围绕平衡高斯—博内定理：由拓扑固定的曲率在向列/ disclination缺陷、弹性变形六边形和边界之间分配；用SL(2,R)的椭圆、抛物、双曲单参数子群生成三类伪球上的缺陷排布，并将理想排布与设计获得及模拟纳米挤出的碳结构比较。

Result: 证明仅由六边形构成的球面若无可变拓扑的曲率缺陷则为穿孔球面，并给出显式多面体构造；构造椭圆族缺陷排布，复现已知Beltrami伪球排布，提出双曲伪球排布，并与设计/模拟纳米挤出碳结构对照。

Conclusion: 缺陷排布由SL(2,R)子群组织，平衡高斯—博内框架统一描述碳基负曲率曲面；作者提出无穷多Fuchsian群可能对应无穷多Lobachevsky曲面。

Abstract: Carbon-made surfaces of constant negative Gaussian curvature realize portions of the Lobachevsky plane in the laboratory, with motivations ranging from analogue gravity, where the elliptic, Beltrami and hyperbolic pseudospheres correspond to the three static phases of the Ba\~{n}ados--Teitelboim--Zanelli black hole, to water desalination. We review and extend their construction around a single principle, the balanced Gauss--Bonnet theorem: the curvature fixed by the topology is shared among disclination defects, elastically deformed hexagons and boundaries. Curvature without defects cannot change the topology: a sphere made of hexagons only is a punctured sphere, as an explicit polyhedral construction shows. The arrangements of the defects on the three pseudospheres stem from the elliptic, parabolic and hyperbolic one-parameter subgroups of $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$: we construct them for the elliptic family, recover the known one of the Beltrami pseudosphere, and propose one for the hyperbolic pseudosphere. We compare these ideal arrangements with carbon structures obtained by design and by simulated nano-extrusion, and suggest that the infinitely many Fuchsian groups may correspond to the infinitely many Lobachevsky surfaces.

</details>


### [113] [Certifying Majorana persistence through Hamiltonian learning from boundary measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16832)
*Tohid Farajollahpour,Alireza Qaiumzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 有限末端测量结合哈密顿量学习与交换子界，可在不判定体拓扑的情况下认证Kitaev链中局域Majorana算符的持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭链端有限测量认证局域量子动力学与Majorana局域性，避免对体拓扑做完整层析，并为近期量子器件提供带失败概率的严格保证。

Method: 利用时间分辨单发边界测量和局域键标定构造局域哈密顿量系数的联合置信集；结合交换子界给出对称化自相关的态无关下界，且对所有兼容延拓有效。当第一键跃迁与配对相等且无额外边界项时，精确恒等式使仅凭末端量子比特即可推断，即使其余部分存在任意时间无关相互作用。并用合成基准及三比特、六比特IBM数据验证。

Result: 在边界甜点附近，合成基准可认证持续至约60倍测量窗口；IBM三比特和六比特记录强化了对编程相干哈密顿量的条件保证；量子点模型应用将调控精度与持续时间和失谐诱导分裂联系起来。

Conclusion: 末端有限测量足以稳健认证局域Majorana持续性和相关动力学，为近期器件提供可操作的认证框架，并将参数调控精度与持续存在时间定量关联。

Abstract: Finite measurements at a chain end can certify local quantum dynamics without determining bulk topology. We combine Hamiltonian learning with commutator bounds to certify the persistence of localized Majorana operators in Kitaev chains. Time-resolved single-shot boundary measurements and local bond calibrations define joint confidence sets for local Hamiltonian coefficients. Under explicit device assumptions, these sets yield state-independent lower bounds on symmetrized autocorrelations with a prescribed failure probability, valid for all compatible continuations. With equal hopping and pairing on the first bond and no additional boundary terms, an exact identity enables inference from the end qubit alone, even with arbitrary time-independent interactions elsewhere. Synthetic benchmarks near a boundary sweet spot certify persistence for up to about 60 times the measurement window. Three- and six-qubit IBM records strengthen conditional guarantees for the programmed coherent Hamiltonian. Applications to quantum-dot models relate tuning precision to persistence times and detuning-induced splitting.

</details>
