# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Integer and Fractional Chern Plateaus Shaped by Layer-Dependent Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17217)
*Ziyue Qi,Zhan Wang,Hongming Weng,Kun Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在扭角双层MoTe2三轨道模型中，层依赖无序显著影响陈绝缘体霍尔平台宽度与转变：等强度下位移场无序比格点势无序产生更窄平台和导电区，分数量子平台也呈相同依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Bloch波函数结构如何调控拓扑与无序的相互作用，特别是层依赖无序对整数与分数陈绝缘体霍尔平台稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用扭角双层MoTe2的真实三轨道模型，在格点势、位移场和扭转角无序下比较霍尔输运与体输运，并用复合费米子平均场理论分析分数量子态。

Result: 相同裸无序强度下，格点势无序产生宽整数平台且转变近直接；位移场无序给出窄平台、平台间导电区和明显霍尔电阻凹陷。差异源于Bloch态的层组成：位移场无序下层贡献部分抵消，削弱体局域化；复合费米子理论在νh=2/3和3/5给出分数平台，平台宽度对无序类型有相同依赖。

Conclusion: 层依赖无序是决定莫尔系统陈平台稳定性的重要机制，仅用裸无序强度不足以描述平台行为。

Abstract: The realization of integer and fractional Chern insulators in moir\'e systems provides a lattice setting for exploring how the structure of Bloch wave functions influences the interplay between topology and disorder. Here we investigate how layer-dependent disorder shapes Hall plateau widths and transitions. Using a realistic three-orbital model of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, we compare Hall and bulk transport under onsite-potential, displacement-field, and twist-angle disorder. At equal bare disorder strength, onsite-potential disorder produces broad integer plateaus with nearly direct transitions, whereas displacement-field disorder yields narrow plateaus separated by conducting regions with pronounced Hall-resistance dips. These contrasting behaviors arise from the layer composition of the Bloch states: contributions from different layer under displacement-field disorder partially cancel, weakening the bulk localization. Composite-fermion mean-field theory further yields fractional plateaus at $\nu_h=2/3$ and $3/5$ with the same disorder dependence of plateau widths. These findings identify layer-dependent disorder as an important mechanism shaping Chern plateau stability in moir\'e systems beyond bare disorder strength alone.

</details>


### [115] [Singlet-triplet superconductivity from repulsive interactions in a $p$-wave magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14609)
*Hanbit Oh,Laura Classen,Daniel F. Agterberg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究p波磁体中Van Hove奇点附近电子在局域排斥相互作用下的不稳定性竞争，发现超导在整个相图中占主导，并且通常是自旋单态与三重态的混合配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期提出的p波磁体具有奇宇称自旋劈裂，但其与Van Hove奇点和排斥相互作用结合时，何种电子不稳定性占优尚不清楚。

Method: 采用parquet重整化群（pRG）分析单子晶格p波磁体模型，并推广到更现实的两子晶格模型，考察不同配对通道与密度波不稳定性。

Result: 在纯排斥相互作用下，超导在所有相图中均为领先不稳定性；单子晶格模型中配对一般为单态-三重态混合，当Tc远小于Van Hove电子与其反演配对体之间的能量偏移时趋近等权重混合；两子晶格模型中超导仍占主导，但子晶格自由度导致多个单态-三重态配对通道竞争，并可出现有限动量等自旋配对密度波。

Conclusion: 该机制广泛适用于奇宇称磁体，并为实现节点拓扑超导提供了一条自然途径。

Abstract: Motivated by recent proposals of $p$-wave magnets, which host odd-parity spin splitting, we study electrons near Van Hove singularities with local repulsive interactions. We demonstrate how the symmetry of the $p$-wave spin splitting governs the competition among instabilities. Using a parquet renormalization group (pRG) analysis of a simple one-sublattice model of a $p$-wave magnet, we find that superconductivity is the leading instability over the entire phase diagram for purely repulsive interactions. The resulting superconducting state is generically a mixture of spin-singlet and spin-triplet pairing and approaches an equal-weight mixture when $T_c$ is small compared with the energy offset between each Van Hove electron and its inversion partner. Extending the pRG analysis to a more realistic two-sublattice model, we find that superconductivity remains the leading instability, while the sublattice degree of freedom leads to a competition among various singlet-triplet pairing channels and even a finite-momentum equal-spin pair-density wave. Our mechanism applies broadly to odd-parity magnets and opens a natural route to nodal topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [116] [Why "quantum spin liquids'' are quantum singlet liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16428)
*Sergey Syzranov,Arthur P. Ramirez*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 综述并理论论证：在无杂质极限下，低温不形成磁序的几何阻挫磁体应表现为无能隙的量子单态液体，并在热力学上留下特征信号而低温无磁性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋液体的实验证据间接，材料杂质可能破坏或掩盖QSL；同时理论对最简单几何阻挫晶格上的量子自旋基态仍缺乏共识，因此需澄清无杂质极限下的基态行为。

Method: 汇总现有几何阻挫磁性材料的实验证据，并辅以理论论证，重点考察无杂质极限下低温不发展磁序的体系。

Result: 这些体系会形成量子单态液体，即由非磁激发构成的无序态；理论论证确认其无能隙特征。该液体可在几何阻挫磁体的热力学性质中体现，但在低温下没有磁性特征。

Conclusion: 无杂质几何阻挫磁体若低温不磁序，其自然基态可理解为无能隙量子单态液体；热力学是探测该类状态的重要窗口，而低温磁性特征的缺失并不能排除其存在。

Abstract: The last couple of decades have seen enormous theoretical and experimental research efforts aimed at finding quantum spin liquids (QSLs) in geometrically frustrated (GF) magnetic materials. Experimental demonstrations of QSLs are challenging due to the indirect character of possible QSL signatures and the presence of impurities in materials, which may destroy or obscure a QSL. At the same time, theoretical literature often lacks consensus about the ground states of quantum spins on even the simplest GF magnetic lattices. We summarize experimental evidence that in the impurity-free limit, available GF magnetic materials that do not develop magnetic order at low temperatures would form {\it a quantum singlet liquid}, i.e. a disordered state of non-magnetic excitations. We further confirm the existence of such a singlet liquid and its gapless character with theoretical arguments. Such a liquid manifests itself in the thermodynamics of GF magnets while having no magnetic features at low temperatures.

</details>


### [117] [Thermal Hall sign reversal from competing magnons and magnon pairs in pyrochlore spin-nematic magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16440)
*Tetsuya Sato,Emika Takata,Masahiro Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究表明，磁振子对可由Berry曲率驱动的热霍尔响应提供特征性签名，并在阻挫磁体中实现热霍尔电导符号调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 束缚磁振子对与自旋向列序密切相关，但实验上难以将其与单磁振子区分，因此需要可观测的输运特征。

Method: 针对具有铁磁双二次交换的自旋-3/2烧绿石反铁磁体，在磁场诱导的全极化相中构建单磁振子与磁振子对能带，纳入Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用，并计算其本征热霍尔电导。

Result: 最低磁振子带与磁振子对带具有相反符号的Berry曲率；两类准粒子的能隙对磁场响应不同，其竞争贡献可导致热霍尔电导随磁场和温度发生符号反转。铬尖晶石氧化物估算表明效应可达实验可及量级；DM诱导的单磁振子与双磁振子杂化仍保留该定性签名。

Conclusion: 同时具有磁振子和磁振子对的阻挫磁体为调控热霍尔电导符号及通过热输运探测束缚磁振子对提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Bound magnon pairs are central to spin-nematic order, yet experimentally distinguishing them from single magnons remains challenging. We show that their Berry-curvature-driven heat transport provides a characteristic signature in the thermal Hall response. For a spin-$3/2$ pyrochlore antiferromagnet with ferromagnetic biquadratic exchange in the magnetic-field-induced fully polarized phase, we construct single-magnon bands together with magnon-pair bands, include the Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interaction, and evaluate their intrinsic thermal Hall conductivities. The lowest magnon and magnon-pair bands carry Berry curvatures of opposite sign. Because the gaps of the two quasiparticles respond differently to magnetic field, their competing contributions can drive both magnetic-field- and temperature-induced sign reversals of the thermal Hall conductivity. Estimates for chromium spinel oxides indicate that the effect can approach experimentally accessible magnitudes. We also find that the hybridization between the one- and two-magnon sectors induced by the DM interaction preserves this qualitative signature. These results suggest that a broad class of frustrated magnets with both magnons and magnon pairs provides a possible route to tuning the sign of the thermal Hall conductivity and probing bound magnon pairs through thermal transport experiments.

</details>


### [118] [Two Unconventional Hall Effects in the Kagome Magnet TmMn4Cr$_2$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16841)
*Mohamed El Gazzah,Fehmi Sami Yasin,Bishal Thapa,Sk Jamaluddin,Abhijeet Nayak,Resham Babu Regmi,Mary Kate Carroll,Igor I. Mazin,Nirmal J. Ghimire*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在kagome磁体TmMn4Cr2Sn6中识别出两个Hall异常：高场异常源于非连续自旋重取向及子晶格对Hall输运和磁化的不同贡献；低场Hall滞后在磁滞后可忽略时仍存在，提示手性相关贡献但微观起源未解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分非常规Hall信号与本征磁转变/子晶格效应，并检验基于磁化分解Hall输运的可靠性。

Method: 对TmMn4Cr2Sn6的Hall输运与磁化行为进行对比分析，分别考察高场和低场Hall异常及其与磁滞的对应关系。

Result: 高场Hall异常可归因于非连续自旋重取向和不同子晶格对Hall输运与磁化的差异贡献；低场Hall滞后在磁化滞后可忽略时仍持续存在，暗示手性相关贡献，但其微观机制尚未解决。

Conclusion: 磁转变可模拟非常规Hall信号，基于磁化的Hall分解存在局限；低场手性相关Hall贡献仍需进一步微观研究。

Abstract: We identify two Hall anomalies in the kagome magnet TmMn$_4$Cr$_2$Sn$_6$. We propose that the high-field anomaly reflects a discontinuous spin reorientation and differing sublattice contributions to Hall transport and magnetization. At low fields, Hall hysteresis persists despite negligible magnetic hysteresis, suggesting a chirality-related contribution, although its microscopic origin remains unresolved. These contrasting regimes show how magnetic transitions can mimic unconventional Hall signals and highlight the limits of magnetization-based Hall decomposition.

</details>


### [119] [Long-lived light-driven bimagnons at finite momentum in NiO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17335)
*Xian P. Yang,Christopher T. Parzyck,Martin Cross,Ella Blake,Octave Duros,Yinchuan Lv,Hari Paudyal,Che-Min Lin,Sharon S. Philip,Lingjia Shen,Daniel Jost,Giacomo Coslovich,Joshua J. Turner,Choongwon Seo,Elyse Schriber,Michael E. Flatt\'e,Xiaoqin Li,Yuri Suzuki,Durga Paudyal,Harold Y. Hwang,Georgi L. Dakovski,Matthias C. Hoffmann,Wei-Sheng Lee,Alexander H. Reid*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用中红外脉冲共振激发NiO的声子辅助双磁振子吸收，并通过时间分辨RIXS探测有限动量磁动力学，发现光致双磁振子关联具有约28 ps的长寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单磁振子的阻尼已被广泛测量和建模，但双磁振子（关联磁振子对）的寿命、相互作用和衰变机制仍不清楚；此前双磁振子测量多显示宽线宽或在200 fs内退相干。

Method: 用远低于带隙的中红外脉冲泵浦NiO的声子辅助双磁振子吸收，并用时间分辨共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）在有限动量下探测光激发后的磁动力学。

Result: 泵浦后单磁振子线宽和能量基本不变，表明长程磁序保持且无光载流子诱导退磁；在能量增益（反斯托克斯）侧出现泵浦诱导的双磁振子信号，其谱重约在泵浦后4 ps达到峰值，远超0.5 ps时间分辨率，随后以28±11 ps时间常数衰减。

Conclusion: 该衰减时间比此前报道的双磁振子退相干时间高两个数量级以上，确立了超越单磁振子态的长寿命光驱动双磁振子关联，类似于超冷气体中的高阶关联测量。

Abstract: Light can manipulate magnetic order and its excitations on ultrafast timescales, including through resonantly driven phonons. In antiferromagnets, light can also create correlated magnon pairs, or bimagnons. Unlike single magnons, whose damping has been measured and modeled in detail, little is known about the lifetime, interactions and decay of such correlated pairs: prior two-magnon measurements show broad linewidths or coherences that dephase within 200 fs. Here we pump the phonon-assisted two-magnon absorption of NiO with mid-infrared pulses, far below its bandgap, and probe the resulting magnetic dynamics at finite momentum with time-resolved resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS). Upon photoexcitation, the one-magnon linewidth and energy are essentially unchanged, indicating that the long-range magnetic order is preserved without the photocarrier-induced demagnetization characteristic of above-bandgap excitation. A pump-induced two-magnon signal appears on the energy-gain (anti-Stokes) side of the spectrum. Its spectral weight peaks 4 ps after the pump, well beyond our 0.5 ps time resolution, indicative of a delayed growth likely due to momentum redistribution of the light-induced magnon pairs across the Brillouin zone. This two-magnon signal decays with a time constant of $28\pm11$ ps, exceeding the previously reported two-magnon dephasing time by more than two orders of magnitude. These results establish long-lived light-driven bimagnon correlations beyond the one-magnon state, akin to the higher-order correlations measured in ultracold gases.

</details>


### [120] [Superlattice Stabilization of the Topological Quantum Hall Ferromagnet in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14679)
*Tiago Cecchi,Ahmed Khalifa,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 超晶格势可重整石墨烯中自旋与谷序竞争，在电荷中性处稳定铁磁量子霍尔拓扑绝缘体，而无需强面内磁场或强介电屏蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯量子霍尔区中自旋与谷序竞争可产生关联拓扑相，但如何可控稳定特定拓扑态仍是关键问题。

Method: 用广义重整化群计算超晶格势下的相互作用各向异性，并结合重正化各向异性的自洽动量空间Hartree-Fock计算确定相稳定性，最后验证与量子霍尔铁磁体的绝热连接。

Result: 超晶格势抑制与自旋极化竞争的各向异性，同时引入色散并偏好部分自旋极化的补偿半金属；自洽计算确定了绝缘自旋极化铁磁体的稳定区间，并证实其拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 超晶格势可作为调控相互作用石墨烯相图的手段，并用于访问其螺旋量子霍尔区。

Abstract: Competition between spin and valley orders makes graphene a versatile platform for engineering correlated topological phases in the quantum Hall regime. We show that a tunable superlattice potential reorganizes this competition, and stabilizes the ferromagnetic quantum Hall topological insulator at charge neutrality without requiring a large in-plane magnetic field or strong dielectric suppression of Coulomb interactions. The interaction anisotropies responsible for choosing between different competing states are first computed by a generalized renormalization group procedure that accounts for the superlattice potential. We find that the potential suppresses anisotropies that compete with spin-polarization. At the same time, the potential introduces dispersion which prefers a partly spin-polarized compensated semi-metal. Therefore, we carry out a self-consistent momentum-space Hartree-Fock computation with renormalized aniostropies to establish the regime of stability of the insulating spin-polarized ferromagnet. Finally, we verify that this ferromagnet is adiabatically connected to the quantum Hall ferromagnet, thereby confirming its topological character. Our results establish superlattice potentials as a means of controlling the phase diagram of interacting graphene and accessing its helical quantum Hall regime.

</details>


### [121] [Termination-selective Kondo hybridization of topological surface states in CeCuAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15022)
*Bo Chen,Chen Zhang,Hao Liu,Qi-Yi Wu,Zhong-Tuo Fu,Ying Zhou,Chun-Hui Lyu,Yang Luo,Zi-Jie Xu,Hai-Long Deng,Zheng-Tai Liu,Mao Ye,Zhen-Hua Chen,Yao-Bo Huang,Sheng-Tao Cui,Zhe Sun,Da-Wei Shen,Jun He,Yu-Xia Duan,Jian-Qiao Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用终止分辨ARPES、Ce 4d→4f共振光电子能谱和表面电子结构计算，在Kondo晶格CeCuAs2中区分出As1与CeAs2两种表面终止，并在CeAs2终止上发现拓扑Dirac表面态与近费米能级弱色散Ce 4f态共存，提供了终止选择性f-Dirac杂化的谱学证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kondo晶格中边界电子与局域f电子耦合可重塑低能电子结构，但表面终止如何调控拓扑边界态与Kondo关联的相互作用仍需微观探测。

Method: 结合终止分辨角分辨光电子能谱、Ce 4d→4f共振光电子能谱与表面电子结构计算，通过费米面几何和终止特异计算区分As1/CeAs2终止，并追踪4f与Dirac分支随温度的光谱权重变化。

Result: 在CeAs2终止上，与计算非平庸体拓扑相关的Dirac表面态和近EF弱色散Ce 4f带共存；共振光电子能谱确认低能4f特征并揭示Dirac分支能量范围内的晶体场精细结构；降温时4f与Dirac区域光谱权重均增加，而参考导带区域变化较小。

Conclusion: 观测到终止选择性的f-Dirac杂化及拓扑表面态中4f特征的发展，表明表面终止可作为调控Kondo关联与拓扑边界电子相互作用的微观手段。

Abstract: Strongly correlated topological states in Kondo lattices represent a frontier in condensed matter physics, where the coupling between boundary electrons and localized f states can reshape the low-energy electronic structure. Here, we combine termination-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), Ce 4$d \rightarrow 4f$ resonant photoemission, and surface electronic structure calculations to investigate the Kondo lattice compound CeCuAs$_2$. Two distinct surface electronic structures are assigned to the As1 and CeAs2 terminations through their characteristic Fermi-surface geometries and termination-specific calculations. On CeAs2, a Dirac surface state associated with the calculated nontrivial bulk topology coexists with a weakly dispersive Ce 4$f$ band near $E_F$. Resonant photoemission identifies the low-energy 4$f$ character and reveals crystal-field-related fine structure in the energy range traversed by the Dirac branch. On cooling, the 4$f$ and Dirac regions both gain spectral weight, whereas a reference conduction-band region changes much less. Together, these observations provide spectroscopic evidence for termination-selective $f$-Dirac hybridization and the development of 4$f$ character in a topological surface state. Our results identify surface termination as a microscopic means of controlling the interplay between Kondo correlations and topological boundary electrons.

</details>


### [122] [Maximally Chiral Eigenstate from Inverse Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15081)
*Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明有序量子态可支持单向有限速度、反向能量指数小的波，并通过重构非局域哈密顿量实现具有异常磁响应、对数熵和可调束缚对的基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过设计相互作用，使自旋-1/2海森堡体系同时呈现方向性动力学和非常规热力学，并提出可实验探测的判别特征。

Method: 对自旋-1/2海森堡本征态规定目标激发谱，重构非局域哈密顿量；用热力学构造选出有序且携带电流的基态，并进行自旋响应计算，包括加入交换项和更平滑模式选择。

Result: 得到单向有限速度、另一方向指数小能量的激发；磁矩有限但弱场响应斜率发散；每自旋熵仅随温度对数倒数消失，系数精确含黄金比例；发现能量可调而强度不变的束缚对；加入交换后最低能激发恢复二次型。

Conclusion: 结果将相互作用设计与方向性动力学及异常热力学联系起来，并建议用动量分辨自旋谱、弱场磁强计和低温熵测量检验这些反直觉特征。

Abstract: We show that an ordered quantum state can support waves with a finite speed in one direction and exponentially small energies in the other. We prescribe this spectrum for a spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg eigenstate and reconstruct a nonlocal Hamiltonian. The thermodynamic construction selects the ordered, current-carrying state as a ground state. Its magnetic moment remains finite while its response to a weak field has a divergent slope. The entropy per spin vanishes only inversely with a logarithm of temperature, with an exact coefficient containing the golden ratio. Our spin-response calculations reveal a bound pair whose energy can be tuned without changing its intensity. Added exchange and smoother mode selection restore quadratic excitations at the lowest energies. These results connect the design of interactions to directional dynamics and unusual thermodynamics. We propose momentum-resolved spin spectroscopy, weak-field magnetometry, and low-temperature entropy measurements to probe these counterintuitive signatures.

</details>


### [123] [Higher-order topology induced by altermagnetism in quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15139)
*Xingmin Huo,Xingchuan Zhu,Xingyu Ma,Shiping Feng,Huaiming Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究Thue-Morse与Ammann-Beenker准晶中由d波交错磁序与二维拓扑绝缘体耦合驱动的高阶拓扑相，并指出d_{x^2-y^2}波交错磁序可诱导零能角态，而常规反铁磁与d_{xy}波交错磁序不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁性具有零净磁化与动量依赖自旋劈裂，是探索新奇量子现象的重要平台；其概念虽已拓展至准晶，但在无平移对称性体系中动量依赖自旋劈裂及高阶拓扑相的形成机制仍不清楚。

Method: 将d波准晶交错磁序与二维拓扑绝缘体耦合，在Thue-Morse和Ammann-Beenker准晶中比较d_{x^2-y^2}波、d_{xy}波交错磁序及常规反铁磁构型对边缘能隙和高阶拓扑角态的影响。

Result: d_{x^2-y^2}波交错磁序表现出类铁磁特征，能够打开边缘能隙并诱导零能高阶拓扑角态；常规反铁磁和d_{xy}波交错磁序无法诱导高阶拓扑能隙。

Conclusion: 交错磁对称性及其与晶格角点的对齐在高阶拓扑相实现中起关键作用，高阶拓扑相的出现可作为非周期体系中识别交错磁序的特征信号。

Abstract: Altermagnetism (AM), a novel magnetic phase featuring zero net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provides a compelling platform for exploring exotic quantum phenomena. While recent studies have extended the concept of AM to quasicrystals, the momentum-dependent spin splitting in the absence of translational symmetry remains inadequately understood. Here, we investigate the emergence of higher-order topological phases (HOTPs) in Thue-Morse and Ammann-Beenker quasicrystals, driven by the coupling between $d$-wave quasicrystal AM orders and a two-dimensional topological insulator. We reveal that the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave AM exhibits a ferromagnetism-like aspect in opening edge gaps and inducing zero-energy higher-order topological corner states. In contrast, conventional antiferromagnetic and $d_{xy}$-wave AM configurations are unable to induce the higher-order topological gaps. These findings highlight the pivotal role of altermagnetic symmetry and its alignment with lattice corners in the realization of HOTPs and establish that the emergence of HOTPs can serve as a signature for identifying altermagnetic orders in aperiodic systems.

</details>


### [124] [Magnetism on emergent molecular-orbital lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15141)
*Zhan Liu,Chenrui Wang,Chen Fang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在ruby晶格紧束缚Hubbard模型中，分子轨道形成的新兴晶格可从三角变为蜂窝，从而在同一原子晶格与同一填充下实现阻挫/非阻挫反铁磁；分子形式因子及其零点可通过低能动态结构因子实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强轨道杂化形成跨越原子团簇的分子轨道，其中心构成不同于原子晶格的新兴晶格；部分填充分子轨道中的电子相互作用可能产生以分子轨道而非单原子为局域矩的新型磁性。

Method: 构建ruby晶格紧束缚Hubbard模型，改变跃迁参数以调控新兴分子轨道晶格；利用随机相近似计算低能动态结构因子，并分析分子形式因子对谱权重和不同布里渊区等效动量比值的影响。

Result: 同一原子晶格和同一填充下，随分子轨道晶格由三角变为蜂窝，可出现阻挫与非阻挫反铁磁；分子形式因子的零点会抑制低能谱权重，RPA结果显示低能积分结构因子在形式因子零点附近出现极小，且不同布里渊区选定等效动量间的比值与形式因子平方比一致。

Conclusion: 分子轨道磁性具有可由低能动态结构因子验证的普遍特征；新兴分子轨道晶格是调控同一原子晶格中磁性有序类型的关键自由度。

Abstract: In solids, strong hybridization between nearby atomic orbitals forms molecular orbitals (MOs), the range of which covers a cluster of atoms. The centers of molecular orbitals form an emergent lattice generally different from the atomic lattice. For partially filled MOs, electron interaction may induce a new type of magnetism on this emergent lattice where the local moments come from the molecular orbitals instead of single atoms. In this work, we show in a ruby-lattice tight-binding Hubbard model that frustrated and unfrustrated antiferromagnetism may appear on the same atomic lattice and at the same filling, as the emergent molecular-orbital lattice changes from triangular to honeycomb upon changing the hopping parameters. Moreover, we show that general molecular orbital magnetism may be experimentally verified in the low-energy dynamic structure factor: the molecular form factors, as the Fourier transforms of the molecular spin-density, modulate the low-energy dynamic structure factor across different Brillouin zones. Zeros of these form factors suppress low energy spectral weight irrespective of the magnetic order formed by the molecular moments. This is exemplified by random phase approximation calculations for the ruby-lattice model: the structure factor integrated over low energies exhibits minima around molecular form-factor zeros, while its ratios between selected equivalent momenta in different Brillouin zones agree with ratios of squared form factors.

</details>


### [125] [Kitaev Exchange and Altermagnetism in the Double Perovskite Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$: An Ab Initio Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15152)
*Alexey V. Ushakov,Sergey V. Strelsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算表明，Co2+基双钙钛矿Sr2CoTeO6具有约|K|~J/3的最近邻铁磁Kitaev交换，并因面心立方晶格产生强交换阻挫；它还是具有显著非相对论自旋劈裂的交变磁体，可产生横向压磁效应和非平庸磁光响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sr2CoTeO6因其非平庸磁性受到关注，需要从微观交换作用角度明确其Kitaev相互作用强度、阻挫特征及潜在磁响应。

Method: 采用从头算密度泛函理论计算分析最近邻交换作用、晶格几何阻挫、自旋劈裂及相关响应。

Result: 发现最近邻铁磁Kitaev交换约为|K|~J/3；结合面心立方晶格导致显著交换阻挫，使其成为具有中等Kitaev相互作用强度的Kitaev候选材料；同时确认其为具有可观非相对论自旋劈裂的交变磁体，可产生横向压磁效应和非平庸磁光响应。

Conclusion: Sr2CoTeO6是中等Kitaev相互作用强度且交换阻挫显著的Kitaev材料候选，并因交变磁性及非相对论自旋劈裂而具备压磁和磁光响应等可观测效应。

Abstract: The Co$^{2+}$-based double perovskite Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ attracts attention due to its nontrivial magnetic properties. \textit{Ab initio} density functional theory calculations reveal a substantial ferromagnetic Kitaev exchange $|K|\sim J/3$ between nearest neighbors that, combined with the face-centered cubic lattice, leads to pronounced exchange frustration and places Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ among Kitaev materials with moderate Kitaev interaction strength. In addition, Sr$_2$CoTeO$_6$ is found to be altermagnetic with a sizable non-relativistic spin splitting, which can give rise to a transverse piezomagnetic effect and a nontrivial magneto-optical response.

</details>


### [126] [Multifractal collective order : Application to multifractal superconductivity in dodecagonal quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15446)
*SeHyun Park,Junmo Jeon,SungBin Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 确定性非周期几何可稳定具有多重分形特征的集体序，而非仅由空间均匀序参量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集体有序描述常假设空间均匀序参量，但电子态可能强空间非均匀或具多重分形性；作者旨在检验非周期几何能否稳定多重分形集体序。

Method: 提出适用于超导、磁性和电荷通道的通用诊断：对序参量直接做标度分析，并检验三条判据——空间层级由底层几何决定、出现在主导序失稳中、且在单一平均场有序起始温度以下的自洽有序态中持续存在；并以十二边形氧化物准晶中实验解析的Ti网络超导性为案例。

Result: Ti网络上的超导满足全部三条判据；凝聚体在已解析空间尺度范围内呈多重分形标度，层级结构贯穿整个有序相，并将通量诱导的平衡电流导向几何锁定的环。

Conclusion: 确定性非周期几何可稳定多重分形集体序，为超越均匀序参量图像理解准晶等非周期体系中的有序提供判据与例证。

Abstract: Conventional descriptions of collective ordering often assume spatially uniform order parameters, although the underlying electronic states can exhibit strong spatial inhomogeneity or multifractality. Here, we demonstrate that deterministic aperiodic geometry can instead stabilize multifractal collective order. We formulate a general diagnostic applicable to superconducting, magnetic, and charge channels, combining direct scaling analysis of the order parameter with three criteria: its spatial hierarchy must be dictated by the underlying geometry, emerge in the leading ordering instability, and persist in the self-consistent ordered state below a single mean-field ordering onset. We demonstrate that superconductivity on the experimentally resolved Ti network of a dodecagonal oxide quasicrystal satisfies all three criteria. The resulting condensate exhibits multifractal scaling over the resolved range of spatial scales, retaining its hierarchical structure throughout the ordered phase and channeling flux-induced equilibrium currents along geometry-locked rings.

</details>


### [127] [Toward Self-Driving Multi-reference Calculations: Belief-Aware Orbital Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15682)
*Chen Li,Kaifan Pan,Xinyu Sun,Yinxuan Song,Lianhua He,Teng Lu,Haibo Ma,Zhenggang Lan,Yingjin Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将活性轨道选择重构为POMDP，并结合QUBO实现基于轨道占用数、组成和纠缠信息的序贯、信念更新式活性空间选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统活性轨道选择常依赖一次性阈值筛选，难以随计算进展自适应调整；作者希望模拟专家式选择，并支持复杂化学反应全过程的活性空间跟踪。

Method: 把活性轨道选择建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，并与二次无约束二值优化耦合：QUBO在固定电子数和轨道数下构造完整候选空间，POMDP逐步选择后续计算、根据每次完成结果更新信念并修正活性轨道；可单独或联合使用占用数、轨道组成和轨道纠缠信息。

Result: 在小分子价空间构建、金属化合物TiF3光谱计算以及1,2-二氧杂环丁酮在圆锥交叉附近的活性空间细化中验证了该方法。

Conclusion: 该方法将活性空间选择从一次性阈值过滤变为连续的信念感知序贯过程，可随计算推进自适应调整，且直接从目标分子的计算中学习、无需预训练数据集，适合跟踪复杂化学反应全过程。

Abstract: We reformulate active-orbital selection as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), analogous to decision-making in autonomous driving and robot control, and couple it with quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) to emulate expert-like selection of active orbitals. The three popular types of orbital information, i.e. occupation numbers, orbital composition, and orbital entanglements, can be used separately or jointly. QUBO constructs complete candidate spaces under fixed electron and orbital counts, while the POMDP selects successive calculations, updates its belief from each completed result, and thereby corrects and refines the active orbitals. This belief-aware sequential procedure enables continuous active-space selection rather than one-shot threshold-based filtering, so the space can adapt as the calculation proceeds. We demonstrate the approach for valence-space construction in small molecules, spectral calculation of metal compound TiF$_3$, and active-space refinement of 1,2-dioxetanone around conical intersections. Because selection learns from calculations on the molecule of interest without requiring a pre-existing training set, it is suited to following the entire course of complex chemical reactions.

</details>


### [128] [$^{55}$Mn-NMR/NQR study on the kagome metal Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$: Re-examining the possibility of spin-singlet paired state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15693)
*Qing-Ping Ding,Yongbin Lee,Charuni Dissanayake,Yasuyuki Nakajima,Yuji Furukawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过55Mn NMR/NQR谱与DFT计算研究Sc3Mn3Al7Si5单晶的低温磁性和电子性质，未发现约10 K自旋单态配对的明确证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sc3Mn3Al7Si5是接近铁磁不稳定性的Mn基kagome金属，且先前55Mn NMR研究暗示约10 K以下可能出现自旋单态配对态，因此需要澄清其低能磁性和电子性质。

Method: 对Sc3Mn3Al7Si5单晶进行55Mn NMR和NQR测量，并结合DFT计算确定Mn位点的四极频率、不对称参数和电场梯度主轴；测量Knight位移、1/T1T和线宽的温度依赖。

Result: 确定νQ=4.6~4.8 MHz、η=0.45~0.48及Mn位点EFG主轴；Knight位移K与直流磁化率χ在较高温区趋势相似，但约50 K以下K近常数而χ继续增加，表明χ低温增加非本征；1/T1T近似常数，微观上确认金属态；约10 K附近无1/T1T峰，K和线宽在低温也无降低。

Conclusion: NMR/NQR结果未显示Sc3Mn3Al7Si5在约10 K附近存在自旋单态配对的清晰特征。

Abstract: Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$ is a Mn-based kagome metal that has been proposed to be close to a ferromagnetic instability. Recent $^{55}$Mn nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) study suggested the emergence of a spin-singlet paired state below $\sim$10 K. To clarify its low-energy magnetic and electronic properties, we performed $^{55}$Mn NMR and nuclear quadrupole resonance (NQR) measurements on single-crystalline Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$. From the detailed NMR/NQR spectrum measurements and the DFT calculations, a quadrupolar frequency of $\nu_{\rm Q}$ = 4.6$\sim$4.8 MHz and an asymmetry parameter of $\eta$ = 0.45$\sim$0.48 as well as the principal axes of electric field gradient EFG for the Mn site were determined. The temperature dependence of Knight shift ($K$) shows a similar temperature dependence of the DC magnetic susceptibility $\chi$ except for the low-temperature region below $\sim$ 50 K where $K$ is almost constant while $\chi$ keeps increasing, which suggests that the increase in $\chi$ at low temperatures is not intrinsic. $^{55}$Mn spin-lattice relaxation rate divided by temperature ($1/T_1T$) is found to be nearly constant, confirming the metallic state of Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$ from a microscopic point of view. Furthermore, no peak in $1/T_1T$ is observed near 10 K, and neither the Knight shift nor the linewidth shows any reductions at low temperatures. These results suggest no clear signature for the spin-singlet pairing around 10 K in Sc$_3$Mn$_3$Al$_7$Si$_5$.

</details>


### [129] [Potts Order Preempts Emergence of 2-Form U(1) Spin Liquidity in Three-Dimensional Spin Vorticity Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15845)
*Michael D. Burke,Mohid U. Farooqi,Kristian Tyn Kai Chung,Michel J. P. Gingras*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 三维自旋涡度模型（SVM）中预言的无旋约束2-形式U(1)经典自旋液体并非真正基态：熵驱动的height locking会先发制人，使体系在中间尺度呈现自旋液体行为，但最终发生序化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期数值研究在烧绿石SVM中发现“双解钉点（pinch points）与弱全进全出（AIAO）序共存”的矛盾现象，需要解释U(1)自旋液体特征与弱序为何同时出现，并判断2-形式U(1)经典自旋液体的稳定性。

Method: 作者通过height mapping严格处理基态约束，并将约束映射与3态Potts反铁磁体联系起来；同时把SVM推广到角共享八面体和立方体格点，分析可能的对称破缺序与相变。

Result: 烧绿石中的弱AIAO序被识别为置换对称子格（PSS）序，但locking强度极弱；在角共享八面体和立方体模型中识别出无磁Bragg峰的隐藏序——破缺子格对称（BSS）序，并与集体顺磁性共存。三个模型均经历与粗粒化height理论中对称允许locking势一致的3D Ising相变；自旋液体仅在中间长度和温度尺度存在，临界Coulomb基态系综因有限locking长度而被截断。

Conclusion: 微观约束可决定阻挫自旋体系中涌现规范理论的最终稳定性；3D SVM中的2-形式U(1)经典自旋液体被熵驱动height locking预占，不能形成真正的临界Coulomb基态。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) spin vorticity models (SVMs) have been proposed to realize an exotic 2-form U(1) classical spin liquid (CSL), characterized by a zero-curl constraint, string excitations, and deconfined fluctuating membranes. A previous numerical study [PRB 111, 064417 (2025)] uncovered a puzzle in the pyrochlore lattice SVM: despite exhibiting pinch points characteristic of a U(1) spin liquid, it also develops weak all-in--all-out (AIAO) order. Here, we show that the emergence of the 2-form CSL in 3D SVMs is preempted by an intrinsic instability towards entropy-driven height locking. We establish this by resolving the ground state constraints using a height mapping, which we further relate to a 3-state Potts antiferromagnet. This identifies the pyrochlore's weak AIAO order as permutationally symmetric sublattice (PSS) order, albeit with an exceptionally weak locking strength. We extend the SVM to lattices of corner-sharing octahedra and cubes, where we identify the alternative broken-sublattice-symmetry (BSS) order, a hidden order without magnetic Bragg peaks coexisting with collective paramagnetism. All three models undergo a 3D Ising transition consistent with a symmetry-allowed locking potential in the coarse-grained height theory. The spin liquid persists over intermediate length and temperature scales, but a critical Coulomb ground state ensemble fails to materialize, its correlations cut off by the finite locking length. These findings illustrate how microscopic constraints can determine the ultimate stability of emergent gauge theories in frustrated spin systems.

</details>


### [130] [Universal Theory of Strange and Bad Metals from Spatially Random Interactions with Underdamped Quantum-Critical Bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15851)
*Aaron Kleger,Rufus Boyack*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一个(2+1)维费米子与欠阻尼量子临界玻色子经空间无序相互作用耦合的理论，用以解释奇异金属长期存在的T线性电阻率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理的重要挑战是理解奇异金属的宏观性质，尤其是其电阻率在多个数量级温区内保持随温度线性。

Method: 构建费米子与欠阻尼量子临界玻色子耦合的(2+1)维理论，通过空间无序相互作用并确立合适的尺度分离使玻色子保持欠阻尼；由此得到两类费米子自能：共形极限下的Σ~√ω，以及边缘费米液体下的Σ~ω log(1/ω)。

Result: 两类自能分别对应Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限之上和之下的区域，产生Planckian散射、精确T线性电阻率、光导率的ω/T标度以及低温下~T log(1/T)的热容。

Conclusion: 该理论在单一框架内统一了奇异金属与坏金属现象，表明强关联物质物理的理解正进入新阶段。

Abstract: One of the most important challenges in condensed matter physics is understanding the macroscopic properties of strange metals, particularly why their resistivity remains linear in temperature over several decades. We propose that a (2+1)d theory of fermions coupled to underdamped quantum-critical bosons through a spatially disordered interaction provides this long-sought-after theoretical explanation. The key novelty of our work is to establish the appropriate separation of scales so that the bosons are underdamped. Using this framework, we obtain two distinct forms for the fermionic self-energy: a conformal-limit solution, $\Sigma\sim\sqrt{\omega}$, and a marginal Fermi-liquid solution, $\Sigma\sim\omega\log(1/\omega)$, corresponding to regimes above and below the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit, respectively. These solutions produce Planckian scattering, exhibit exact $T$-linear resistivity, $\omega/T$ scaling in the optical conductivity, as well as $\sim T\log(1/T)$ heat capacity at low temperatures. Moreover, our theory naturally unifies strange- and bad-metal phenomena within a single framework, suggesting that we are now approaching a new epoch in understanding the physics of strongly correlated matter.

</details>


### [131] [Crystalline Equivalence Principle for Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15857)
*Ching-Yu Yao,Ryohei Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在微观格点层面刻画(1+1)与(2+1)维玻色晶格系统中晶格对称性与有限幺正内部对称性组合形成的对称性反常，并建立相应的晶格等价原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在不依赖连续场论描述或涌现洛伦兹不变性的前提下，系统分类玻色晶格系统中的晶格对称性反常，并统一理解LSM与SPT-LSM约束。

Method: 构造对称性算符的反常指标，推导其余循环条件与余边界模糊性；对结合 onsite Hilbert 空间置换与量子元胞自动机的广义晶格对称性，将反常指标分别归类为 H^3(BΓ~, U(1)_r) 与 H^4(BΓ~, U(1)_r)，其中 Γ~ 为晶格与内部对称性的总群，r 的系数扭曲编码空间取向反转。

Result: 当晶格对称性仅通过置换 onsite Hilbert 空间作用时，反常类到空间子群的限制平凡；非平凡总反常指标会阻碍任何对称可逆相。该框架纳入空间对称性置换内部对称性的调制对称性，并可复现LSM与SPT-LSM约束，后者表明谱序列微分可使候选LSM反常指标变平凡。

Conclusion: 论文在微观层面建立了玻色反常的晶格等价原理，给出了晶格对称性反常的算子指标与上同调分类，并证明其与对称可逆相阻碍及LSM/SPT-LSM约束的一致性。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic characterization of crystalline symmetry anomalies in bosonic lattice systems in (1+1) and (2+1) dimensions with a combination of crystalline and finite unitary internal symmetry. We construct anomaly indices of symmetry operators and derive their cocycle conditions and coboundary ambiguities. For generalized crystalline symmetries combining permutations of onsite Hilbert spaces with quantum cellular automata, anomaly indices take values in $H^3(B\widetilde \Gamma,U(1)_r)$ and $H^4(B\widetilde \Gamma,U(1)_r)$ respectively, where $\widetilde \Gamma$ is the total group of crystalline and internal symmetries, and the coefficient twist $r$ accounts for spatial orientation reversal. When crystalline symmetries act solely by permuting onsite Hilbert spaces, the anomaly classes restrict trivially to the spatial subgroup. These results establish a crystalline equivalence principle for bosonic anomalies at the microscopic level, without assuming a continuum description or emergent Lorentz invariance. We prove that a nontrivial total anomaly index obstructs any symmetric invertible phase. The framework incorporates modulated symmetries where spatial symmetries permute internal symmetries. Our results reproduce LSM and SPT--LSM constraints, with the latter illustrating how spectral-sequence differentials can trivialize candidate LSM anomaly indices.

</details>


### [132] [Learning Quantum Hamiltonians from the Dynamical Structure Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15859)
*Nisarga Paul,Thomas Schuster,Jason Alicea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明动态结构因子（DSF）蕴含足够信息，可高效重建多类量子哈密顿量，并提出三种基于低阶谱矩的层析协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别支配量子系统的微观相互作用对材料预测和量子技术验证至关重要，但从真实实验数据学习哈密顿量仍具挑战。

Method: 利用DSF低阶谱矩与微观耦合之间的简单关系，分别基于高温展开、强场极化和涨落耗散定理，构建三种互补的哈密顿量层析协议。

Result: DSF可有效重建广泛类别的量子哈密顿量；该框架能从常见实验数据实现实用且稳健的哈密顿量学习，并有望直接应用于中子散射实验。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从动态结构因子进行实用、稳健哈密顿量学习的方法，对中子散射等实验具有潜在即时应用价值。

Abstract: Identifying the microscopic interactions governing a quantum system is essential for developing predictive theories of materials and validating quantum technologies. Despite considerable progress, learning Hamiltonians from realistic experimental data remains a challenge. Motivated by neutron scattering and quantum simulations, we study the problem of learning a quantum Hamiltonian from the dynamical structure factor (DSF), which captures two-point correlation data in quantum magnets. We show that the DSF encodes sufficient information to efficiently reconstruct broad classes of quantum Hamiltonians. Our approach exploits simple relations between low-order spectral moments of the DSF and microscopic couplings, giving rise to three complementary Hamiltonian tomography protocols based on the high-temperature expansion, strong-field polarization, and the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, respectively. The framework we introduce establishes practical and robust Hamiltonian learning from common experimental data, with potentially immediate application to neutron scattering experiments.

</details>


### [133] [What Hall Measures in Strongly Correlated Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15940)
*Emily Z. Zhang,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出Hall电导的Liouvillian表述，将响应分解为对磁通敏感的多种多体干涉算子，并用Hubbard模型的Schrieffer-Wolff变换与高温展开解释强关联金属中Hall输运的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hall效应是探测强关联金属电子关联的敏感工具，但超越准粒子输运的微观解释仍不清楚，尤其是横向电荷响应如何由相干多体动力学和晶格几何决定。

Method: 作者建立Hall电导的Liouvillian形式，将响应组织为不同类别的磁通敏感多体干涉算子；对Hubbard模型作Schrieffer-Wolff变换，识别投影电荷运动与虚跳跃过程如何贡献包围磁通的定向轨迹；再用高温展开得到这些干涉过程对应的逆温度贡献层级。

Result: 该框架为 determinant quantum Monte Carlo 中观测到的整数幂律提供了可能的微观解释，并指出这些幂律出现在高温展开形式上可控温区以下；结果强调Hall输运对相干多体动力学的敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究建立了晶格几何、电子关联与横向电荷响应之间的联系，表明Hall输运可作为探测强关联体系中相干多体动力学和磁通敏感干涉过程的有效探针。

Abstract: The Hall effect provides a sensitive probe of electronic correlations in strongly interacting metals, yet its microscopic interpretation beyond quasiparticle transport remains poorly understood. Here, we develop a Liouvillian formulation of the Hall conductivity that organizes the response into different classes of flux-sensitive many-body interference operators. Using a Schrieffer-Wolff transformation of the Hubbard model, we identify how projected charge motion and virtual hopping processes contribute to oriented trajectories enclosing magnetic flux. A high-temperature expansion reveals a hierarchy of inverse-temperature contributions associated with these interference processes. This framework provides a possible microscopic interpretation of the integer power laws previously observed in determinant quantum Monte Carlo calculations, which emerge at temperatures below the regime where the expansion is formally controlled. Our results highlight the sensitivity of Hall transport to coherent many-body dynamics and establish a connection between lattice geometry, electronic correlations, and transverse charge response.

</details>


### [134] [Elastic tricriticality at the collapse of magnetic order in a valence fluctuating Eu-based compound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15984)
*Yassine Agarmani,Arif Ata,Lars Franke,Jan Zimmermann,Ulrich Tutsch,Marius Peters,Michelle Ocker,Kristin Kliemt,Cornelius Krellner,Bernd Wolf,Markus Garst,Michael Lang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出电子关联诱导的三临界点会与晶格弹性发生非微扰耦合，形成“弹性三临界性”，并在EuPd2(Si1-xGex)2单晶中通过压力与温度调控实验证实该场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上常假设自旋与电荷涨落和原子晶格的耦合较弱且可作微扰处理；作者旨在检验电子关联相关三临界点处晶格弹性耦合是否会非微扰地改变临界普适类。

Method: 理论上论证三临界点与晶体弹性的耦合会修正三临界普适类并导致压缩率随关联长度普适发散；实验上使用EuPd2(Si1-xGex)2单晶，通过温度和He气压力调控，测量相对长度变化等互补手段确认x≈0.1附近的三临界点，并研究弹性响应。

Result: 在x≈0.1附近确认了由强价态涨落导致磁序坍缩相关的三临界点；弹性响应测量发现清晰的弹性三临界性特征，尤其是显著增强的压缩率。

Conclusion: 强关联电子体系的三临界点不能忽略晶格弹性耦合；弹性三临界性构成受晶格弹性非微扰修正的新临界行为，压缩率随关联长度普适发散，需要在相关材料理论中纳入弹性自由度。

Abstract: Spin and charge fluctuations are key for understanding the properties of materials with strongly correlated electrons, but their coupling to the atomic crystal lattice is often assumed to be weak and perturbative. In this work, we demonstrate that at tricritical points (TCPs), that are associated with electronic correlations, a coupling to crystal elasticity is generically non-perturbative. It will modify the tricritical universality class leading to elastic tricriticality, characterized by a compressibility that diverges in a universal manner with the correlation length. To test this scenario experimentally, we use single crystals of EuPd$_2$(Si$_{1-x}$Ge$_x$)$_2$, where, depending on $x$ and pressure, magnetic order collapses due to strong valence fluctuations. First, we confirm the presence of a TCP for $x \approx 0.1$ by using complementary probes, in particular, measurements of relative length changes under control of temperature and He-gas pressure. Second, by studying the elastic response, we uncover clear signatures of elastic tricriticality, notably a drastically enhanced compressibility.

</details>


### [135] [Mott transitions in Dirac nodal-line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16033)
*Andressa R. Medeiros-Silva,Lucas O. Lima,Marcello B. da Silva Neto,Fakher F. Assaad,Natanael C. Costa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用平均场、行列式量子蒙特卡洛和弱耦合重整化群研究立方晶格 Hubbard 模型中节点点与节点线半金属的相互作用效应，发现节点线几何会显著改变临界耦合与低能不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节点流形几何如何影响相互作用驱动的量子相变，是节点线半金属中的核心问题；作者希望对比节点点与节点线半金属在 Hubbard 相互作用下的相变和低能行为。

Method: 在支持立方 π-通量节点点态和节点线态的立方晶格 Hubbard 模型上，结合平均场理论、行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟和弱耦合重整化群分析。

Result: 两种情形都需有限相互作用才出现反铁磁序；节点线系统因低能态密度增强而临界耦合更小，并表现出更强有限尺寸效应。节点点模型的量子临界行为与(3+1)维 Gross–Neveu 平均场标度相容。弱耦合 RG 表明节点线对相互作用不稳定：Hubbard 相互作用微扰意义下无关，但二阶自能产生对称性允许的 relevant 项 U^2 cos k_z，使节点线重构为孤立且强各向异性的节点点。

Conclusion: 节点线半金属存在由弱耦合相互作用诱导的节点线重构，区别于强耦合反铁磁转变；结果凸显节点流形几何在相互作用半金属中的关键作用。

Abstract: Nodal-line semimetals provide a natural setting to investigate how the geometry of a nodal manifold affects interaction-driven quantum phase transitions. Here, we study the Hubbard model on a cubic lattice supporting either nodal-point (cubic $\pi$-flux model) or nodal-line semimetallic states using mean-field theory, determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations, and a weak-coupling renormalization-group (RG) analysis. In both cases, antiferromagnetic order develops only above a finite interaction strength, with the nodal-line system exhibiting a smaller critical coupling due to its enhanced low-energy density of states and markedly stronger finite-size effects. For the nodal-point model, the quantum critical behavior is compatible with $(3+1)$-dimensional Gross--Neveu mean-field scaling. In contrast, the weak-coupling RG reveals that the nodal line itself is unstable against interactions: although the Hubbard interaction is perturbatively irrelevant, its second-order self-energy generates a symmetry-allowed relevant term proportional to $U^2\cos k_z$, reconstructing the nodal line into isolated, strongly anisotropic nodal points. This weak-coupling reconstruction is distinct from the strong-coupling antiferromagnetic transition and highlights the role of nodal-manifold geometry in interacting semimetals.

</details>


### [136] [Noninvertible Lieb-Schultz-Mattis Constraints and Deconfined Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16249)
*Linhao Li,Zhen Bi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文为一类由双线性相位映射算符实现的内部非可逆对称性建立一维qudit系统的Lieb-Schultz-Mattis定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LSM约束通常源于投影表示，本文旨在揭示内部非可逆对称性与平移/反演等晶体对称性之间的非平凡代数关系如何导致新的低能约束。

Method: 推导保持内部非可逆对称性及平移或反演对称性的任意Hamiltonian的LSM定理，并在具有竞争相变的Z4 qudit模型中用数值模拟验证。

Result: 证明此类Hamiltonian要么无能隙，要么具有简并基态；Z4 qudit模型展示分别自发破缺内部非可逆对称性与反演对称性的两个竞争相之间存在直接连续相变。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了涉及内部非可逆对称性的稳定退禁闭量子临界点，并表明非可逆对称性与晶体对称性的代数关系可产生超越投影表示机制的新型LSM约束。

Abstract: We derive Lieb-Schultz-Mattis theorems for one-dimensional qudit systems with a class of internal noninvertible symmetries implemented by bilinear phase map operators. We show that any Hamiltonian preserving these symmetries together with translation or inversion symmetry must either be gapless or have degenerate ground states. Unlike conventional LSM constraints based on projective representations, these new constraints arise from nontrivial algebraic relations between the internal and crystalline symmetries. We illustrate this mechanism in a $\mathbb{Z}_4$ qudit model hosting two competing phases that spontaneously break the internal noninvertible symmetry and inversion symmetry, respectively. Numerical simulations reveal a direct continuous transition between these phases, realizing a stable deconfined quantum critical point involving an internal noninvertible symmetry.

</details>


### [137] [Probing the Dirac Quantum Geometry in Quantum Magnets from Polarized Raman Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16491)
*Yuki Yamazaki,Doh Young Kim,Takahiro Morimoto,Bohm-Jung Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于对称性的磁拉曼谱提取原理，用于具有两带准粒子激发的二维量子磁体，可从圆偏振拉曼谱中分离量子度规与贝里曲率的顶点加权收缩，并实现无需绝对强度校准的局部谱学读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁拉曼光谱对准粒子量子几何敏感，但微观拉曼顶点通常阻碍直接的几何解释，因此需要建立可操作、可标定的几何提取方案。

Method: 利用相反圆偏振光谱分离量子度规和贝里曲率贡献；在C3对称的大质量狄拉克谷附近，对称性将主导拉曼与速度双重态固定到共同尺度，使未知拉曼强度和强度归一化在谱比中抵消；对反演相关的大质量狄拉克锥对，分析阈值频率处的归一化反向圆偏振拉曼不对称性。

Result: 发现归一化反向圆偏振拉曼不对称性在阈值频率处由陈数固定，并遵循普适的近阈值频率标度；该读出无需绝对强度校准或频率积分。作者在场诱导蜂窝Kitaev自旋液体中的马约拉纳费米子和蜂窝铁磁体中的拓扑磁振子中演示了该原理，并给出实验分辨条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维量子磁体的量子几何提供了一种对称性保护、局部且无需绝对标定的磁拉曼谱学读出方案，并指出在Kitaev自旋液体和拓扑磁振子体系中实验实现的条件。

Abstract: Magnetic Raman spectroscopy is sensitive to quasiparticle quantum geometry, yet microscopic Raman vertices generally prevent a direct geometric interpretation. Here we establish a symmetry-based extraction principle for two-dimensional quantum magnets with two-band quasiparticle excitations. Opposite circular-polarization spectra separate vertex-weighted quantum-metric and Berry-curvature contractions. Near a $C_3$-symmetric massive Dirac valley, symmetry fixes the leading Raman and velocity doublets up to a common scale, allowing the unknown Raman strength and intensity normalization to cancel from their spectral ratio. For an inversion-related pair of massive Dirac cones, the normalized counter-circular Raman asymmetry at frequency threshold $\Omega_{\rm th}$ is fixed by the Chern number and follows a universal near-threshold frequency scaling. This yields a local spectroscopic readout without absolute-intensity calibration or frequency integration. We demonstrate the principle for Majorana fermions in the field-induced honeycomb Kitaev spin liquid and for topological magnons in a honeycomb ferromagnet, and identify the conditions for resolving it experimentally.

</details>


### [138] [Thermal and dynamical behaviors of the spin-nematic phases in a spin-1 triangular lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16532)
*Lei Xu,Zhentao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用自旋-1三角晶格模型理论研究Na2BaNi(PO4)2中自旋向列物理的有限温度性质、相变与低能自旋动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Na2BaNi(PO4)2被认为是实现自旋向列物理的强候选材料，需要理论阐明小双二次相互作用、有限温度相变及动力学响应。

Method: 构建自旋-1三角晶格模型并加入小双二次项；用SU(3)相干态半经典方法参数化自旋向列波函数，结合半经典Monte Carlo、低能有效模型的量子Monte Carlo以及自旋波理论。

Result: 小双二次相互作用可在半经典框架中稳定自旋向列相；有限温度进入自旋向列相与半涡旋增殖相关的BKT转变有关；QMC确定的相边界与半经典分析定性一致；低能自旋动力学可由含小双二次项的自旋-1模型或有效自旋-1/2模型等价描述；零场半经典偶然简并对应动力学中的赝Goldstone模式。

Conclusion: 该模型复现了与Na2BaNi(PO4)2相关的自旋向列物理，揭示了双二次项稳定机制、BKT型有限温度转变及低能动力学中的简并与赝Goldstone模。

Abstract: The triangular lattice compound Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ has been recently established as a strong candidate for realizing the spin-nematic physics. In this theoretical work, we study the finite-temperature properties and the dynamics of a spin-1 triangular lattice model, which reproduces the spin-nematic physics and provides insights relevant to the compound. We show that a small biquadratic interaction can be used for stabilizing the SN phases in the semiclassical approach with SU(3) coherent states, which allows for a mathematical parameterization of the spin-nematic wave functions. With a semiclassical Monte Carlo calculation, the finite temperature transitions into the spin-nematic phases are shown to be related to BKT transitions associated with the proliferation of half vortices in the original spin-1 model. Using quantum Monte Carlo calculations with a low-energy effective model, the phase boundaries are determined and found to agree qualitatively with the semiclassical analysis. Within the spin wave approach, the low-energy spin dynamics can be captured equally well by either the spin-1 model with a small biquadratic term, or the effective spin-$1/2$ model. The accidental degeneracy of the semiclassical approach at zero field is revealed by explicit analytical parameterization, and is shown to be associated with the pseudo-Goldstone mode observed in the spin dynamics.

</details>


### [139] [Learning to control and predict light-driven electron-phonon dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16780)
*Matteo Puviani*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作建立一个可微优化与神经网络预测相结合的计算框架，在双格点电子-声子模型中设计驱动场以增强电子双占据，并学习预测非平衡可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格振动可通过非线性电子-声子耦合直接操控电子关联，但最优驱动场设计不能仅依赖脉冲幅度和频率，需要控制耦合动力学。

Method: 采用可微框架联合场优化与神经网络预测；在双格点电子-声子模型中比较分段常数、B样条、前馈和循环控制参数化，以最大化累积电子双占据为目标；在无耗散、零Hubbard相互作用条件下优化，并用独立循环网络根据前一时刻双占据、声子可观测量和外场预测下一步量。

Result: 所有控制参数化均提高双占据，其中B样条将平均总双占据提高约30%；循环网络能够学习预测下一步双占据和声子可观测量。

Conclusion: 结果提供了通过声子驱动塑造局域电子关联的计算概念验证，并表明学习模型可用于非平衡材料控制。

Abstract: Driving lattice vibrations provides a direct route to manipulating electronic correlations through nonlinear electron-phonon coupling. Finding suitable driving fields, however, requires control over the coupled dynamics beyond a simple choice of pulse amplitude and frequency. Here we develop a differentiable framework that combines field optimization with neural-network prediction in a two-site electron-phonon model. We compare piecewise-constant, B-spline, feed-forward, and recurrent control parameterizations, optimized to increase the accumulated electronic double occupation. For the dissipation-free model at zero Hubbard interaction, all the methods increase double occupation, with the B-spline raising the mean total double occupation of approximately 30%. Alongside control optimization, a separate recurrent network learns to predict the next-step double occupation and phonon observables from their preceding values and the applied field. These results establish a computational proof of concept for shaping local electronic correlations through a phonon drive and identify a promising route toward learned models for nonequilibrium materials control.

</details>


### [140] [Symmetry-preserving gapped Dirac state revealed by site-selective $^{13}$C NMR in $\alpha$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16939)
*Takahiko Sekine,Kazuya Miyagawa,Koichi Hiraki,Reizo Kato,Kazushi Kanoda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 位选择性13C NMR表明α-(BETS)2I3低温绝缘相保持反演与时间反演对称性，是无磁序、对称性保持的有能隙Dirac态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-(BETS)2I3在约50 K发生金属-绝缘体转变或尖锐交叉，被提议为自旋轨道耦合诱导的Dirac绝缘体，但其绝缘基态性质及对称性是否破缺尚不明确。

Method: 采用位选择性13C核磁共振，测量低温下的谱线分裂、线宽、核自旋-晶格弛豫率和Knight shift，并提取各分子位点的自旋磁化率。

Result: 2 K下谱线无额外分裂，可由A(=A′)、B、C三个位点的四重态和两个二重态完全描述，表明反演对称性保持；无异常线宽或弛豫率临界增强，排除磁有序和时间反演对称性破缺；Knight shift给出χ_C>χ_A>χ_B，类似压力下α-(BEDT-TTF)2I3的倾斜Dirac锥特征；但后者中由无隙倾斜Dirac锥引起的微弱场致类亚铁磁自旋激发在本体系中缺失。

Conclusion: α-(BETS)2I3绝缘基态为对称性保持的有能隙Dirac态，为该系统候选关联拓扑相提供微观约束。

Abstract: We report evidence that the low-temperature insulating phase of $\alpha$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$ preserves both inversion and time-reversal symmetries using site-selective $^{13}$C nuclear magnetic resonance. $\alpha$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$ undergoes a metal-insulator transition or sharp crossover at approximately 50 K and has been proposed as a candidate spin-orbit-coupling-induced Dirac insulator, yet the nature of its insulating ground state remains unresolved. The NMR spectra exhibit no additional splitting down to 2 K and remain fully described by one quartet and two doublets associated with the three types of molecular sites, A (=A$^{\prime}$), B and C sites, demonstrating the preservation of inversion symmetry. Furthermore, no anomalous line broadening or critical enhancement of the nuclear spin-lattice relaxation rate is observed, indicating the absence of magnetic ordering and time-reversal-symmetry breaking. Analysis of the Knight shifts yields site-specific spin susceptibilities satisfying $\chi_{\mathrm{C}} > \chi_{\mathrm{A}} > \chi_{\mathrm{B}}$, a characteristic feature of tilted Dirac-cone electronic states as observed in $\alpha$-(BEDT-TTF)$_2$I$_3$ under pressure. However, the faint field-induced ferrimagnetic-like spin excitations observed in $\alpha$-(BEDT-TTF)$_2$I$_3$, which are ascribed to the gapless tilted Dirac cones, are absent in $\alpha$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$. These results demonstrate that the insulating ground state of $\alpha$-(BETS)$_2$I$_3$ is a symmetry-preserving gapped Dirac state and provide microscopic constraints on proposed correlated topological phases in this molecular Dirac system.

</details>


### [141] [Unconstrained $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory on a triangular lattice: deconfined fermions in a vison crystal and superfluidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16988)
*Welberth Kennedy,Jo\~ao C. In\'acio,Fakher F. Assaad*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 三角晶格上半填充的无约束 Z2 规范理论耦合费米子：弱横场下形成退禁闭的 vison 晶体与 π 通量 Dirac 半金属，强场下经一阶相变进入禁闭 s 波超流，并给出 T×h 相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方格子中对应相变是连续的，而三角几何会引入全局 Z2/Ising 对称性并可能改变退禁闭行为、相结构与临界性；因此研究晶格几何和阻挫对 Z2 规范-费米子模型的影响。

Method: 采用辅助场量子蒙特卡洛模拟三角晶格半填充的无约束 Z2 规范理论耦合费米子，调节横向场 h 并扩展到有限温度，分析面通量 Ising 序、vison 晶体、π 通量 Dirac 半金属、超流及相变性质。

Result: 弱 h 时全局 Z2 对称性自发破缺，稳定 vison 晶体；该相退禁闭，费米子在有序 π 通量背景中传播并形成 Dirac 半金属。增大 h 触发一阶量子相变到禁闭 s 波超流，禁闭与全局对称破缺同时发生，区别于方格子的连续相变。有限温度下，超流通过 Kosterlitz-Thouless 相变融化，vison 晶体经历热 Ising 相变，从而确定 T×h 相图。

Conclusion: 三角几何导致该 Z2 规范-费米子模型的相和临界性发生定性变化；结果建立了从退禁闭 vison 晶体到禁闭超流的相图，并突出晶格几何/阻挫在规范理论退禁闭与相变中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate an unconstrained $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory coupled to fermions on the triangular lattice at half filling, using auxiliary field quantum Monte Carlo simulations. The triangular geometry gives rise to a global $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry that acts as an Ising symmetry on the plaquette fluxes. At weak transverse field $h$, this symmetry is spontaneously broken, stabilizing a vison crystal. We argue that this phase is deconfined: fermions, charged under an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge field, propagate in an ordered $\pi$-flux background and form a Dirac semimetal. Increasing the transverse field triggers a first-order quantum phase transition to a confined $s$-wave superfluid, at which confinement and global symmetry breaking occur simultaneously. This contrasts with the square lattice, where the corresponding transition is continuous. We extend our analysis to finite temperature and determine the thermal phase boundaries of both ordered phases. The superfluid melts through a Kosterlitz--Thouless transition, whereas the vison crystal undergoes a thermal Ising transition. Together, our results establish the $T \times h$ phase diagram and demonstrate how the triangular geometry leads to qualitative changes in both the phases and the criticality of the model.

</details>


### [142] [Theory of critical behavior at non-polar structural phase transitions from functional renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17081)
*Max Fornoville,Kilian Fraboulet,Michael M. Scherer,Laura Classen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究由内部与空间对称性交织产生的非极性结构序临界现象，构建并分析含额外动能项 $h(\partial_i\phi_i)^2$ 的 $O(d)$ 模型，发现该耦合在热与量子 Wilson-Fisher 不动点均无关，但会带来显著标度修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 $O(N)$ 模型广泛描述连续相变，但某些非极性结构序（如偶极反铁磁、自旋轨道耦合磁体、非极性铁电体及部分向列系统）的序参量同时受内部与空间对称性约束，需要扩展的有效理论。

Method: 从交织内部与空间对称性的序参量出发，得到带 $h(\partial_i\phi_i)^2$ 项的 $O(d)$ 有效模型；该动能项改变纵向与横向涨落的相对刚度。利用泛函重整化群（FRG）研究热相变与量子相变，推导并分析流方程，特别关注两点函数的张量结构以一致提取动能项的重整化。

Result: 由于内部与空间对称性交织，空间导数与虚时导数被区分，量子模型不能简单由热模型作 $d\rightarrow d+1$ 维扩展得到。FRG 分析表明，额外动能耦合在热和量子 $O(d)$ Wilson-Fisher 不动点均为无关算符，因而量子-经典对应重新出现；但其修正标度指数虽小却不可忽略，在临界点附近导致可观的标度修正。

Conclusion: 该扩展 $O(d)$ 模型仍由 Wilson-Fisher 临界行为主导，额外动能耦合不改变普适类，但会对有限温度/量子临界区域的可观测标度修正产生重要影响。

Abstract: $O(N)$ models provide a versatile framework for describing continuous phase transitions in a wide variety of physical systems. We investigate an extension of these models arising from order parameters that transform under interlocked internal and spatial symmetries which describe transitions towards non-polar structural order. Such order occurs, for instance, in dipolar antiferromagnets, spin-orbit-coupled magnets, non-polar ferroics, and certain nematic systems. The resulting effective theory is an $O(d)$ model with an additional symmetry-allowed kinetic term $h(\partial_i\phi_i)^2$, which modifies the relative stiffness of longitudinal and transverse fluctuations. We study both thermal and quantum phase transitions within this $O(d)$ model using the functional renormalization group (FRG). In contrast to conventional $O(N)$ models, the interlocking of internal and spatial symmetries distinguishes spatial and imaginary-time derivatives, such that the quantum model is not simply obtained from the thermal one by a dimensional extension $d\rightarrow d+1$. We derive and analyze the corresponding FRG flow equations, paying particular attention to the tensor structure of the two-point function, which is essential for consistently extracting the flow of the kinetic terms. Our analysis shows that the additional kinetic coupling is irrelevant at the $O(d)$ Wilson-Fisher fixed point in both thermal and quantum cases, where quantum-to-classical correspondence thus re-emerges. However, its small but sizable correction-to-scaling exponent gives rise to substantial corrections to scaling in the vicinity of the critical point.

</details>


### [143] [A neural quantum state variational Monte Carlo study of Aharonov-Casher bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17144)
*Conor Smith,Nicol\'as Morales-Dur\'an,Jingtian Shi,Andrew J. Millis,Miguel A. Morales,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用神经网络变分蒙特卡洛研究Aharonov-Casher模型中分数陈绝缘体与广义Wigner晶体的竞争，并给出强能带混合下分数填充的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔材料中分数拓扑相与非拓扑晶态之间存在精细竞争，尤其在强能带混合和传统方法难以处理的系统尺寸下，需要可靠比较两类基态。

Method: 构建受磁通附着启发的变分波函数以描述分数化相，并与描述晶态相的Slater-Jastrow拟设进行能量比较；采用神经网络变分蒙特卡洛在连续模型中计算相图。

Result: 发现嵌入非均匀量子几何的离散平移对称性对ν=1/3和ν=2/3分数化态影响不同，且能带混合可稳定非公度晶态基态。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了拓扑分数相与非拓扑晶态相之间的微妙竞争，并表明神经网络变分方法可用于强能带混合莫尔体系的大尺寸相图研究。

Abstract: We use neural-network-based variational Monte Carlo to investigate the competition between fractional Chern insulators and generalized Wigner crystals in the Aharonov-Casher Hamiltonian, a continuum model for moir\'e materials. We introduce a variational wave function inspired by flux attachment that captures fractionalized phases and, by comparing its energy with that of a Slater--Jastrow ansatz that captures crystalline phases, we determine the phase diagram at fractional fillings in regimes of strong band mixing and for system sizes beyond the reach of other methods. We find that discrete translation symmetry, encoded in a nonuniform quantum geometry, affects fractionalized states at filling fractions $\nu=1/3$ and $\nu=2/3$ differently, and that band mixing can stabilize incommensurate crystalline ground states. Our results further reveal the delicate competition between topological and non-topological crystalline phases.

</details>


### [144] [Reentrant Nematicity from Landau Quantization near a Pomeranchuk Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17150)
*Lu\'is E. Leite Mac\^edo,Daniel G. Barci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用近四极Pomeranchuk不稳定性的平均场理论研究二维费米液体中Landau量子化诱导的向列序，并讨论其在de Haas–van Alphen振荡中的可观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零磁场下各向同性的二维费米液体可在Landau量子化下产生向列序；作者希望厘清磁场诱导向列性的相图及其在量子振荡测量中的表现，尤其是热力学约束对解释的影响。

Method: 在四极Pomeranchuk不稳定性附近建立平均场理论，计算磁场变化下的向列/各向同性相区与相变性质，并分别在固定化学势和固定粒子密度下分析de Haas–van Alphen振荡的频率、回旋质量和谐波振幅。

Result: 随磁场变化可出现交替的向列相与各向同性相区，相变可为连续或一阶。固定化学势时，磁场导致的费米面形变可显著改变振荡频率，即使回旋质量变化不大；固定粒子密度时，费米面面积守恒使主振荡频率保持不变，但回旋质量与谐波振幅仍对向列序敏感。

Conclusion: 这些对比行为提供了磁场诱导向列性的实验判据，并表明量子振荡测量中热力学约束（固定化学势还是固定密度）对数据解释至关重要。

Abstract: Landau quantization can induce nematic order in a two-dimensional Fermi liquid that is isotropic at zero magnetic field. Using a mean-field theory near a quadrupolar Pomeranchuk instability, we show that varying the magnetic field produces alternating nematic and isotropic regions, with transitions that can be either continuous or first order. The resulting nematic state also leaves distinctive signatures in the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations. At fixed chemical potential, the field-dependent deformation of the Fermi surface can produce substantial changes in the oscillation frequency, even when the cyclotron mass varies only modestly. At fixed particle density, conservation of the Fermi-surface area preserves the leading oscillation frequency, while the cyclotron mass and harmonic amplitudes remain sensitive to nematic order. These contrasting behaviors provide experimentally relevant signatures of field-induced nematicity and reveal the importance of thermodynamic constraints in quantum-oscillation measurements.

</details>


### [145] [The de Haas--van Alphen effect in a two-dimensional nematic Fermi fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17166)
*Lu\'is E. Leite Mac\^edo,Daniel G. Barci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究二维相互作用电子体系中朗道量子化与向列序的相互影响，表明量子振荡既能揭示也能调控电子有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子振荡常用于探测金属费米面，但当电子相互作用使费米面自发形变时，其行为变得复杂，因此需要理解量子振荡与向列序等电子有序之间的耦合。

Method: 在垂直磁场下采用均匀平均场近似，研究可发展向列序的二维相互作用电子模型，并结合Pomeranchuk不稳定性分析朗道量子化、de Haas--van Alphen振荡、磁化和磁化率。

Result: 随磁场变化，朗道量子化可促进或抑制向列序，在Pomeranchuk不稳定附近形成各向同性与向列区域交替，并出现连续和一阶转变；向列序反过来改变dHvA振荡且依赖热力学条件：固定化学势时振荡频率可显著变化而回旋质量仅小幅变化，固定粒子密度时费米面积守恒保持主导Onsager频率但回旋质量和谐波振幅仍敏感，磁化和磁化率提供额外转变特征。

Conclusion: 量子振荡与电子向列序之间存在双向耦合，该框架有助于解释强关联金属中量子振荡与电子有序相关的实验现象。

Abstract: Quantum oscillations are widely used to probe the Fermi surface of metals, but their behavior becomes more complex when electronic interactions can spontaneously deform that surface. We investigate this interplay in a two-dimensional model of interacting electrons capable of developing nematic order, using a uniform mean-field approximation in a perpendicular magnetic field. We show that Landau quantization can favor or suppress nematic order as the field varies, producing alternating isotropic and nematic regions near a Pomeranchuk instability, with both continuous and first-order transitions. Conversely, the evolving nematic order modifies the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations in ways that depend on the thermodynamic conditions. At fixed chemical potential, the oscillation frequency can change significantly even when the cyclotron mass varies only modestly. At fixed particle density, conservation of the Fermi-surface area preserves the leading Onsager frequency, while the cyclotron mass and harmonic amplitudes remain sensitive to nematic order. The magnetization and magnetic susceptibility provide additional signatures of the nematic transitions. These results show how quantum oscillations can both reveal and influence electronic order, offering a framework for interpreting experiments in strongly correlated metals.

</details>


### [146] [Lattice response theory for subsystem symmetry-protected topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17182)
*Yuki Furukawa,Takuya Okuda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文建立基于格点响应理论的子系统对称保护拓扑（SSPT）相框架，系统构造规范场与响应作用量，并用纠缠子实现SSPT堆叠，分类交集配对型及更一般的SSPT相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSPT相缺乏统一、系统的格点响应理论描述，尤其需要涵盖全局、子系统及调制对称性，并刻画超越交集配对的SSPT相及其反常。

Method: 从qudit系统上一般X型微观对称作用出发构造时空格点规范场；由规范不变响应作用量构造相应entangler并证明其实现与响应作用量描述的SSPT相堆叠；用交集配对和cluster entangler实现一大类SSPT相，并进一步研究涉及Bockstein同态的type-II群上同调子系统类比及type-III cocycle子系统类比。

Result: 证明广泛SSPT相可由交集配对描述并由cluster entangler实现；构造一个新的3+1维SSPT相，受两个2-foliated平面子系统对称性和一个全局0-form对称性保护，其响应作用量为type-III cocycle的子系统类比；边界上0-form对称性通过堆叠2+1维线性SSPT相作用，表现为混合't Hooft反常。

Conclusion: 格点响应理论为SSPT相提供统一构造与堆叠框架，可描述交集配对型及更一般的子系统上同调型SSPT相，并揭示新型高维SSPT相及其边界混合反常。

Abstract: We develop a framework to study subsystem symmetry-protected topological (SSPT) phases using lattice response theory. We begin with a systematic construction of spacetime lattice gauge fields from general microscopic $X$-type symmetry actions on qudit systems, including global symmetries, subsystem symmetries, and more general modulated symmetries. Given a gauge-invariant response action, we then construct a corresponding entangler and explicitly show that it implements stacking with the SSPT phase described by the response action. We show that a broad class of SSPT phases can be described by intersection pairings and realized by cluster entanglers. We also study SSPT phases beyond intersection pairings, including those described by subsystem analogues of type-II group cocycles involving the Bockstein homomorphism. We construct a new 3+1d SSPT phase protected by two 2-foliated planar subsystem symmetries and a global 0-form symmetry, whose response action is a subsystem analogue of a type-III cocycle. On its boundary, the 0-form symmetry acts by stacking with a 2+1d linear SSPT phase, signaling a mixed 't Hooft anomaly.

</details>


### [147] [Optimizing Chern Bands for Moore-Read States: A Parent Hamiltonian Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17188)
*Bishoy M. Kousa,Yung-Chun Chao,Jung-Jung Su,Allan H. MacDonald*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种通过优化相互作用来促进无磁场下Moore-Read非阿贝尔拓扑序的框架，并用三粒子谱成本函数评估体系接近理想Moore-Read极限的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: moiré材料中分数量子反常Hall态的实现，激发了在零磁场下稳定非阿贝尔拓扑序的研究兴趣。

Method: 将能带投影后的三粒子谱与Moore-Read母Hamiltonian（由三体相互作用定义）进行比较，并定义成本函数来量化体系与理想Moore-Read极限的接近程度；该方法应用于朗道能级和扭转MoTe2的陈带。

Result: 建立了基于三粒子谱比较的相互作用优化与评估框架，并在朗道能级及扭转MoTe2陈带中进行了应用；摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为在零磁场moiré平台中通过相互作用优化稳定Moore-Read非阿贝尔态提供了可操作的判据和框架。

Abstract: The recent realization of fractional quantum anomalous Hall states in moir\'e materials motivates efforts to stabilize non-Abelian topological order in the absence of a magnetic field. Here, we propose a framework for optimizing interactions to favor the non-Abelian Moore-Read state. Our approach compares band-projected three-particle spectra to that of the Moore-Read parent Hamiltonian, which is defined by a three-body interaction, and quantifies the comparison by defining a cost function that estimates proximity to the ideal Moore-Read limit. We apply our approach to Landau levels and to the Chern bands of twisted MoTe$_2$.

</details>


### [148] [Cubic, Non-Fermi Liquid Exchange Couplings for Doublet Impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17192)
*Anna I. T\'oth,Davide Marenduzzo,Andrew D. Huxley*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构造立方金属中双重态杂质的非费米液体量子杂质模型，区分晶体对称性保护的与非保护的非费米液体行为，并推导破坏非保护标度区的立方微扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解半整数总角动量J传导电子与双重态杂质耦合时，哪些非费米液体行为受晶体对称性保护、哪些不被保护。

Method: 构造双重态杂质量子杂质模型并进行晶体对称性分析，显式检验时间反演对称性，并推导非对称性保护模型的立方微扰。

Result: 确认仅自旋1/2杂质与J=3/2传导电子的Kondo模型、以及由四极Kondo模型导出的两通道Kondo模型受晶体对称性保护；四极Kondo模型保持时间反演对称。另构造两个非保护立方模型：非Kramers与Kramers双重态杂质分别由Γ8与Γ6或Γ7传导电子共同屏蔽；后者预期实现新非费米液体不动点但尚未求解。

Conclusion: 非对称性保护的非费米液体标度区会被立方微扰驱动离开；新Kramers双重态模型提供待研究的新非费米液体不动点。

Abstract: We construct non-Fermi liquid (NFL) quantum impurity models for doublet impurities in a cubic metal, involving conduction electrons with half-integer total angular momentum, $J$. We highlight the only two crystal-symmetry-protected NFL quantum impurity models: the spin-half impurity, $J=3/2$ conduction electron Kondo model, and the two-channel Kondo model originally derived in the context of the quadrupolar Kondo model. We show explicitly that the quadrupolar Kondo model preserves time-reversal symmetry. We also construct two cubic quantum impurity models in which NFL behavior is not symmetry-protected. Each has $\Gamma_8$ and either $\Gamma_{6}$ or $\Gamma_{7}$ conduction electrons screening the impurity simultaneously, one model for a non-Kramers doublet and the other for a Kramers doublet impurity ground state. To our knowledge, the latter model has not been considered before; we expect it to realize a new NFL fixed point, but its solution is left for future work. Finally, we derive the relevant cubic perturbation that drives the non-symmetry-protected systems away from their NFL scaling regimes.

</details>
